\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}\equiv{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{(k^{*}+1)}

final estimator computed via KF at iteration
(k^{*}+1)
using
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}

\widehat{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}\equiv{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(k^{*}+1)}

pseudo-MSE computed via KF at iteration
(k^{*}+1)
using
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}

\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\equiv\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}\equiv{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*}+1)}

final estimator computed via KS at iteration
(k^{*}+1)
using
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}

\widehat{\mathbf{P}}_{t|T}\equiv{\mathbf{P}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*}+1)}

pseudo-MSE computed via KS at iteration
(k^{*}+1)
using
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}

\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}

WLS estimator computed using
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}

Appendix A
Further details on estimation

Hereafter, we assume without loss of generality that
\bm{\mu}_{n}=\mathbf{0}_{n}
and
p_{F}=1
with
\mathbf{A}\equiv\mathbf{A}_{1}
. When
p_{F}>1
, it is enough to write the VAR in (
4
) in companion form and to modify the estimation accordingly, using the augmented state vector
(\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\cdots\mathbf{F}_{t-p_{F}+1}^{\prime})^{\prime}
.

A.1
Principal Component estimators

Let
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{x}
be the sample covariance matrix of the data and denote as
\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x}
the diagonal matrix with entries the
r
-largest eigenvalues of
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{x}
, and as
\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{n}^{x}
the
n\times r
matrix of the corresponding normalized eigenvectors. Moreover,
let
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{(0)}
be a
r\times r
diagonal matrix with entries

\mathbb{I}([\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{n}^{x}]_{1j}\geq 0)-\mathbb{I}([\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{n}^{x}]_{1j}<0)
,
j=1,\ldots,r
. Then,

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}=\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{n}^{x}\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{(0)}(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x})^{1/2},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\mathbf{x}_{nt}=(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x})^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{(0)}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{n}^{x\prime}\mathbf{x}_{nt},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{\xi}}_{nt}=\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t},\hskip 9.24994ptt=1,\ldots,T

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}=\left(\sum_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right)^{-1},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}=\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1},\hskip 9.24994ptt=1,\ldots,T,

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(0)}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}^{\prime}.

Finally, letting
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}
be the
i
-th row of
\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}
, and
\widetilde{\xi}_{it}
the
i
th component of
\widetilde{\bm{\xi}}_{nt}
, then

\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{\xi}_{it}^{\,2},\hskip 18.49988pti=1,\ldots,n.

The vector of initial estimates of parameters is then:

\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(0)}=\left(\text{vec}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{1}^{2(0)}\cdots\widehat{\sigma}_{n}^{2(0)}\;\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)})^{\prime}\;\text{vech}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(0)})^{\prime}\right)^{\prime},

and it is used to run the first iteration of the EM algorithm.

A.2
Kalman filter and smoother

The following iterations are stated for given initial conditions
\mathbf{F}_{0|0}
and
\mathbf{P}_{0|0}
and given the true parameters
\bm{\varphi}_{n}
.

Forward iterations - Filtering

The Kalman filter is based on the forward iterations for
t=1,\ldots,T
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}=\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t-1|t-1},

(A.1)

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}=\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{t-1|t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}+\bm{\Gamma}^{v},

(A.2)

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|t}=\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}),

(A.3)

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{t|t}=\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}.

(A.4)

Moreover, by combining (
A.2
) and (
A.4
), we obtain the Riccati difference equation:

\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}+\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}=\bm{\Gamma}^{v}.

(A.5)

Backward iterations - Smoothing

The Kalman smoother is then based on the backward iterations for
t=T,\ldots,1
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|T}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{F}_{t|t}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t+1|T}-\mathbf{F}_{t+1|t}),

(A.6)

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{t|T}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{P}_{t|t}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{-1}(\mathbf{P}_{t+1|T}-\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t})\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{t|t}.

(A.7)

Finally,
{\mathbf{C}}_{t,t-1|T}
can be obtained from a state space model with an augmented state vector containing both
\mathbf{F}_{t}
and
\mathbf{F}_{t-1}
, by taking the
r\times r
off-diagonal block of the
2r\times 2r
matrix defined in (
A.7
) but for the augmented model.

An equivalent way of implementing (
A.6
), which does not require matrix inversion is in
Durbin and
Koopman 2012
, which is defined by the backward iterations for
t=T,\ldots,1

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|T}=\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\mathbf{r}_{t-1},

(A.8)

\displaystyle\mathbf{r}_{t-1}=\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1})+\mathbf{L}^{\prime}_{t}\mathbf{r}_{t},

(A.9)

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{t|T}=\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{N}_{t-1}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}),

(A.10)

\displaystyle\mathbf{N}_{t-1}=\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{L}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{N}_{t}\mathbf{L}_{t},

(A.11)

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{t}=\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n},

(A.12)

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{t,t+1|T}=\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\mathbf{L}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{N}_{t}\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}),\hskip 18.49988pt\mathbf{C}_{t,t-1|T}=\mathbf{C}_{t,t+1|T}^{\prime},

(A.13)

where
\mathbf{r}_{T}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
,
\mathbf{N}_{T}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
and by construction
\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{t|t}=\mathbf{L}_{t}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
.

Intialization of Kalman filter and smoother

The Kalman filter is initialized as follows. At the first iteration of the EM algorithm, i.e., when
k=0
, we set
{\mathbf{F}}^{(0)}_{0|0}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
and
{\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}_{0|0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, consistently with Assumption
6
(b). Other initializations as
{\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}_{0|0}=\kappa_{0}\mathbf{I}_{r}
for some finite real
\kappa_{0}>0
are also possible.
At any successive iteration of the EM algorithm, i.e, when
k>0
, we set
{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}_{0|0}={\mathbf{F}}_{0|T}^{(k-1)}
and
{\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}_{0|0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
.

To run the Kalman smoother we start using the last predictions of the Kalman filter. Thus, for any
k\geq 0
, we set
{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}_{T+1|T}=\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)}{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}_{T|T}
where
{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}_{T|T}
is obtained from (
A.3
), and we set
{\mathbf{P}}^{(k)}_{T+1|T}=\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)}\mathbf{P}_{T|T}^{(k)}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)\prime}+\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k)}
, where
{\mathbf{P}}^{(k)}_{T|T}
is obtained from (
A.4
).

A.3
Stopping rule for the EM algorithm

To stop the EM algorithm we adopt the following convergence rule. We fix a maximum finite number of iterations
k_{\max}
, and we stop it at the first iteration
k^{*}\leq k_{\max}
such that:

\Delta\ell_{k^{*}}=\frac{\big|\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k^{*}+1)})-\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k^{*})})\big|}{\frac{1}{2}\big|\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k^{*}+1)})+\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k^{*})})\big|}<\varepsilon,

(A.14)

where
\varepsilon
is a pre-specified tolerance level. In this case, the log-likelihood is computed using its prediction error formulation obtained from the Kalman filter:

\displaystyle\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}),

where
\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}
and
\underline{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}
are computed using (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), respectively, when using generic values of the parameters.
Similar convergence criteria can be found in
Booth and
Hobert 1999
and
McLachlan and
Krishnan 2007
.

Appendix B
Proof of main results

Hereafter, we assume without loss of generality that
\bm{\mu}_{n}=\mathbf{0}_{n}
and
p_{F}=1
with
\mathbf{A}\equiv\mathbf{A}_{1}
.

B.1
Proof of Proposition
1

Consider the EM algorithm initialized using the PC estimators of the parameters as defined in Section
A.1
.
At
k^{*}=0
, from (
13
), we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(1)}=\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)\prime}+\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(0)}\right)^{-1}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}x_{it}\right).

(B.1)

Now,

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,2\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|,

(B.2)

and

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}x_{it}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})x_{it}\right\|.

(B.3)

Throughout, let
\bm{y}_{t}=\mathbf{F}_{t}
or
\bm{y}_{t}=x_{it}
. Then, we have to consider

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(0)})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(0)})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,I+II+III,\;\text{ say.}

(B.4)

Let us consider each term in (
B.4
). First,

\displaystyle I\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t+1|t}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\left\{\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|T}^{(0)}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|t+1}^{(0)}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(B.5)

by Lemmas
D.8
,
D.11
, and
D.18
, and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
D.3
(i). Second, from (
D.49
) and (
D.53
) in the proof of Lemma
D.16

\displaystyle II\leq

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1})\left\{\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1|t-1}^{(0)}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\right\}=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(B.6)

because of Lemma
D.18
and since

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right\|\leq n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|M_{\lambda}=O_{p}(1),

(B.7)

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|\leq n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right\|\leq\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|M_{\lambda}=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|\leq\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}-n^{-1/2}\mathbb{E}[\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}]\right\|+n^{-1/2}\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}]\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2})+O(1),

by Assumption
1
(a), Lemmas
C.2
,
C.12
(i),
C.12
(ii),
C.12
(iii), and
C.12
(iv), and because

n^{-1}\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}]\|^{2}\leq n^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{jt}^{2}\xi_{it}^{2}]|\leq\mathrm{K}_{\xi}
, by Assumption
2
(d). Note that the first and third relations in (
B.7
) cover also the case
\bm{y}_{t}=\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}
.

Finally, let us consider the last term in (
B.4
). From (
D.55
) in the proof of Lemma
D.17

\displaystyle III\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\|\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\|n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}n^{-1/2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,III_{a}+III_{b}+III_{c}+III_{d},\;\text{ say.}

(B.8)

Then,

III_{a}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(B.9)

by (
B.7
) and (
D.57
)-(
D.59
) in the proof of Lemma
D.17
.
Moreover,

III_{b}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-2},T^{-1})),

(B.10)

by (
B.7
) and Lemmas
D.1
(ii) and
D.5
(v).
Regarding
III_{c}
, if
\bm{y}_{t}=\mathbf{F}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle III_{c}

\displaystyle=n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(B.11)

by Lemmas
D.5
(iii) and
C.8
(iv). If
\bm{y}_{t}=x_{it}
, we have

\displaystyle III_{c}\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})),

(B.12)

by Assumption
1
(a), and Lemmas
D.5
(iii),
C.8
(iv), and
C.8
(v).
Last,

III_{d}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.13)

by (
B.7
) and Lemma
D.5
(v). From (
B.8
), (
B.9
), (
B.10
), (
B.11
), (
B.12
), and (
B.13
)

\displaystyle III=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(B.14)

Combining (
B.5
), (
B.6
), and (
B.14
) we have

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.15)

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})x_{it}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.16)

which once substituted into (
B.2
) and (
B.3
), jointly with Lemma
D.12
give

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)\prime}+\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(0)}\}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(0)}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))+O_{p}(n^{-1}),

and

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}x_{it}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

Therefore, from (
B.1
)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(1)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.17)

with
\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}=(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it})
. And by Lemma
D.19
(i) we also have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

(B.18)

indeed, recalling that
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
by Assumption
6
(b), by Lemma
C.12
(i) and Weyl’s inequality
(
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, Theorem 1)
we have
|\nu^{(r)}(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2})
which implies

\|(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1).

From (
B.17
) and (
B.18
)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(1)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(1)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|+\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(B.19)

Moreover, by letting
\bm{y}_{t}=n^{-1/2}\mathbf{x}_{nt}
the above proof leads to

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{x}_{nt}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.20)

and, therefore, using also Lemma
D.19
(ii), we have

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(B.21)

This proves parts (a.1) and (a.2) when
k^{*}=0
.

Following the same reasoning leading to (
B.19
) and by Lemma
D.19
(iv) we can easily prove that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}-\mathbf{A}\|\leq\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}-\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|+\|\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.22)

where
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}
is defined in (
15
) and
\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}=(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime})(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime})^{-1}
(recall that
\mathbf{F}_{0}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
by Assumption
1
(i)).
To prove (
B.22
) we also use the fact that
\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{C}_{t,t-1|T}^{(0)}\|=O(n^{-1})
since it can be obtained by the upper right block of
\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(0)}
when this is computed from the the Kalman smoother having the augmented state vector
(\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\,\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime})^{\prime}
. This proves part (a.4) when
k^{*}=0
.

Likewise, using (
B.22
) and the same reasoning leading to (
B.19
), by Lemma
D.19
(v), we can easily prove also that

\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(1)}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v}\|\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(1)}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}\|+\|\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(B.23)

where
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(1)}
is defined in (
16
) and
\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}
. To prove (
B.23
) we need to use also the intermediate quantity

T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(1)}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}
, which is
\min(n,\sqrt{T})
-consistent because of (
B.22
). This proves part (a.5) when
k^{*}=0
.

Finally, using again the same reasoning leading to (
B.19
), by Lemma
D.19
(iii), we can prove that

|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(1)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(1)}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|+|\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.24)

where
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(1)}
is defined in (
14
) and
\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}
. To prove (
B.24
) we need to use also the intermediate quantity

T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(1)\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}
, which is
\min(n,\sqrt{T})
-consistent because of (
B.19
). This proves part (a.3) when
k^{*}=0
.

Now, from (
B.19
) and (
B.24
) using the same reasoning of the proof of Lemma
D.4
(ii), which in turn requires (
B.15
) and (
B.16
), again it follows that

n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{(1)2}-\sigma_{i}^{2})\right|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(B.25)

And, using the same reasoning as in the proof of Lemma
D.5
but using now (
B.21
), (
B.24
), and (
B.25
),

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(1)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(1)})^{-1}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(B.26)

\displaystyle n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(1)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)

\displaystyle n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(1)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(1)})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

From (
B.21
), (
B.22
), (
B.23
), and the relations in (
B.26
) and following the same steps as in the proofs of Lemmas
D.8
,
D.11
, and
D.12
, we get

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}_{t|t-1}\|=O_{p}(1),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|(\mathbf{P}^{(1)}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1),

(B.27)

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),\hskip 9.24994pt\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}^{(1)}_{t|T}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

and also
\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(1)}\|=O_{p}(1)
and
\|\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(1)}\|=O_{p}(1)
by the same arguments in Lemma
D.14
. It follows that we can apply the same steps as in the proofs of Lemmas
D.15
,
D.16
, and
D.17
to get

\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(1)}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1/2},T^{-1/2})).

This proves part (b) when
k^{*}=0
.

Then we can show that (
B.15
) and (
B.16
) still hold when using
{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(1)}
in place of
{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(0)}
and using also the last of (
B.27
) we prove
\min(n,\sqrt{T})
-consistency of
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(2)}
. Similarly we can prove
\min(n,\sqrt{T})
-consistency of
n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(2)}
,
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(2)}
,
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(2)}
, and
\sigma_{i}^{2(2)}
. It is then clear that we can repeat the same reasoning leading to (
B.25
), (
B.26
), and (
B.27
) but when
k^{*}=1
. So these arguments hold for all
k^{*}\geq 0
. This completes the proof.
\Box

B.2
Proof of Proposition
2

For part (a.1), for any
k^{*}\geq 0
, we have (recall that
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*}+1)}
)

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})+(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})+(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})+({\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,L.1+L.2+L.3+L.4,\;\text{ say.}

(B.28)

From Lemma
E.23

\displaystyle\|L.1\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1}T^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n},T^{-3/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

(B.29)

From Lemma
E.22
(i)

\displaystyle\|L.2\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})).

(B.30)

From Lemma
E.11
(i)

\displaystyle\|L.3\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})).

(B.31)

From Lemma
D.19
(i)

\displaystyle\|L.4\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(B.32)

By using (
B.29
), (
B.30
), (
B.31
), and (
B.32
) into (
B.28
), we prove part (a.1).

For part (a.2), for any
k^{*}\geq 0
, we have (recall that
\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k^{*}+1)}
)

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{**})+(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})+(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})+({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}),

and the proof follows from Lemmas
D.19
(ii),
E.11
(ii), and
E.25
(i), and since

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1}T^{-1}{\log n},T^{-2})),

(B.33)

by Lemma
E.22
(i).

For part (b), from part (a.1) and (
B.28
),
if
n^{-1}\sqrt{T}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\to 0
, as
n,T\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{T}({\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)+o_{p}(1).

(B.34)

Now, since
\{\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\}
is strongly mixing with exponentially decaying coefficients by
Bradley 2005

(see also (
E.5
) in the proof of Lemma
E.1
), and given that by Assumption
5
the following Cramér condition holds

\sup_{m\geq 1}r^{-1/\delta}(\mathbb{E}[|\xi_{it}F_{jt}|^{m}])^{1/m}\leq K,

for some finite positive reals
\delta\in\left(0,\frac{\delta_{v}\delta_{\xi}}{\delta_{v}+\delta_{\xi}}\right)
and
K
independent of
t
,
i
, and
j

(
Kuchibhotla and
Chakrabortty 2022
, Section 2)
, then the Central Limit Theorem by
Ibragimov 1962
applies, i.e.,

T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0}_{r},\lim_{T\to\infty}T^{-1}\sum_{s,t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\xi_{it}\xi_{is}\right]\right).

(B.35)

Therefore, by Lemmas
C.12
(i) and
C.13
, and Assumption
1
(b)

\displaystyle\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}-(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\right\|\,\|(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(B.36)

Thus from (
B.34
), (
B.35
), and (
B.36
), by Slutsky’s Theorem, we have

\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0}_{r},\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}),

where,

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\left\{\lim_{T\to\infty}T^{-1}\sum_{s,t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\xi_{is}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\right]\right\}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}=\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{s,t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\right]\right\},

since
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
because of Assumption
6
(b) and
\{\mathbf{F}_{t}\}
and
\{\xi_{it}\}
are independent processes because of Lemma
C.11
. This proves part (b). Part (c) is straightforward. This completes the proof.
\Box

B.3
Proof of Proposition
3

Recall the definitions
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\equiv\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}\equiv\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*}+1)}
, for any
k^{*}\geq 0
. From Lemmas
F.4
(ii) and
F.4
(iii),

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(B.37)

where
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}.

Now,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\rm A+\rm B+\rm C+\rm D,\;\text{ say.}

(B.38)

Let us consider each term in (
B.38
). First, consider term
\rm A
and notice that

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{**}\|+n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}\|+n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1}T^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n},T^{-3/2}{\log n}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),

(B.39)

by Lemmas

E.11
(ii),

E.22
(i), and

E.25
(i) (see also (
B.33
) in the proof of Proposition
2
).

Therefore, from (
B.39
)

\displaystyle\rm A\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\{\rm A.1+\rm A.2\}\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|,\;\text{ say.}

(B.40)

Then,

\displaystyle\rm A.1\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(B.41)

by Lemmas
C.3
(iii),
C.8
(iv), and
C.13
.
Moreover,
{\rm A}.2=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))
,
because of (
B.39
) and Lemmas
C.2
,
C.3
(iii), and Assumption
2
(a) which implies
\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\leq C_{\xi}
. This, jointly with (
B.40
) and (
B.41
) implies that

\displaystyle\rm A=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),

(B.42)

since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
because
\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=1
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, by Assumption
6
(b).

Second, by Proposition
2
(a) and Lemma
F.2
(v)

\displaystyle\rm B=

\displaystyle\,\|n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}n^{-1/2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n},n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n})),

(B.43)

and again since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
because
\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=1
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, by Assumption
6
(b).

Third,

\displaystyle\rm C\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

(B.44)

by Lemmas
C.3
(iii) and
C.7
(i).

Fourth, and last,

\displaystyle\rm D\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\rm D.1+\rm D.2+\rm D.3,\;\text{say.}

(B.45)

Then,

{\rm D.1}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-3/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))
, by Lemmas
C.7
(i) and
F.2
(iv).
Moreover,

\displaystyle\rm D.2=

\displaystyle\,n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,

\displaystyle\cdot\Big\{n^{-1}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|+n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}[(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}]\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|+n^{-1}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}[(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}]\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\{\rm D.2.a+\rm D.2.b+\rm D.2.c\},\;\text{ say.}

We then have the following results. First,

\displaystyle\rm D.2.a=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\xi_{it}\right\|\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|n^{-1}\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-3/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

(B.46)

by Lemmas
C.7
(i) and
F.1
(i). Second,

\displaystyle\rm D.2.b=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\{(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2})^{-1}-({\sigma}_{i}^{2})^{-1}\}\xi_{it}\right\|=n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2}\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2})^{-1}\{\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-{\sigma}_{i}^{2}|\,\left\{n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\{\sigma_{i}^{2}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2})\}^{-1}\xi_{it}\right\|+n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{4})^{-1}\xi_{it}\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-3/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

by Lemmas

(ii). Third, clearly by (

) and (
B.47
)

\displaystyle{\rm D.2.c}=o_{p}(\max(n^{-3/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

B.4
Proof of Proposition
4

First notice that

\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it}=(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}+\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})=(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}+\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})+(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}).

(B.67)

Then,

\displaystyle|\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1/2},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n}))+O_{p}(\max(T^{-1/2},n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T))+o_{p}(\max(n^{-1/2},T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(\max(n^{-1/2},T^{-1/2})),

(B.68)

by Propositions
2
(a) and
3
(a), Assumption
1
(a), and since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
because
\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=1
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, by Assumption
6
(b).
This proves part (a).

For part (b), let us denote
\delta_{nT}=\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})
, for simplicity of notation. Consider the first term on the rhs of (
B.67
). Define
\bm{K}_{\Lambda}=n(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}
. Then, from (
B.64
) in the proof of Proposition
3

\displaystyle\delta_{n,T}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})=

\displaystyle\,\delta_{n,T}\,n^{-1}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{K}_{\Lambda}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{j}^{\prime}(\sigma_{j}^{2})^{-1}\xi_{jt}+O_{p}(\delta_{n,T}T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\delta_{nT}\,n^{-1/2}\mathcal{A}_{it}+o_{p}(1),\;\text{ say,}

(B.69)

since
\delta_{nT}T^{-1}\leq\delta_{nT}\max(n^{-1},T^{-1})=\delta_{nT}\delta_{nT}^{-2}\to 0
. Similarly, consider the second term on the rhs of (
B.67
) and define
\bm{K}_{F}=(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}
. Then, from (
B.34
) in the proof of Proposition
2

\displaystyle\delta_{n,T}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})=

\displaystyle\,\delta_{n,T}T^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{K}_{F}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{s}\xi_{is}+O_{p}(\delta_{n,T}n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\delta_{nT}\,T^{-1/2}\mathcal{B}_{it}+o_{p}(1),\;\text{ say,}

(B.70)

since
\delta_{nT}n^{-1}\leq\delta_{nT}\max(n^{-1},T^{-1})=\delta_{nT}\delta_{nT}^{-2}\to 0
. From Propositions
2
(b) and
3
(b), as
n,T\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{it}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}(0,\mathcal{C}^{F}_{it})\hskip 9.24994pt\text{ and }\hskip 9.24994pt\mathcal{B}_{it}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}(0,\mathcal{C}^{\lambda}_{it}),

(B.71)

where
\mathcal{C}^{F}_{it}=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\bm{\lambda}_{i}
and
\mathcal{C}^{\lambda}_{it}=\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}\mathbf{F}_{t}
.
Moreover,
\mathcal{A}_{it}
and
\mathcal{B}_{it}
are asymptotically independent, since the former is a cross-sectional sum of random variables, while the latter is the sum of a given time series and under Lemmas
C.1
(i)-
C.1
(iii) are weakly serially and cross-sectionally correlated in the same sense as assumed by
Bai 2003
.

Define
a_{nT}=\delta_{nT}n^{-1/2}
and
b_{nT}=\delta_{nT}T^{-1/2}
. Then, substituting (
B.69
) and (
B.70
) into (
B.67
), we obtain

\displaystyle\delta_{nT}(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})

\displaystyle=a_{nT}\mathcal{A}_{it}+b_{nT}\mathcal{B}_{it}+\delta_{nT}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=a_{nT}\mathcal{A}_{it}+b_{nT}\mathcal{B}_{it}+o_{p}(\delta_{nT}\max(n^{-1/2},T^{-1/2}))+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=a_{nT}\mathcal{A}_{it}+b_{nT}\mathcal{B}_{it}+o_{p}(1),

(B.72)

because of (
B.68
).
From (
B.71
) and (
B.72
), by Slutsky’s theorem and following the same reasoning as in
Bai 2003
, as
n,T\to\infty
, we have

\delta_{nT}{\left(a_{nT}^{2}\mathcal{C}^{F}_{it}+b_{nT}^{2}\mathcal{C}^{\lambda}_{it}\right)^{-1/2}}{(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})}=(n^{-1}\mathcal{C}^{F}_{it}+T^{-1}\mathcal{C}^{\lambda}_{it})^{-1/2}(\widehat{\chi}_{it}-\chi_{it})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}\mathcal{N}(0,1),

which completes the proof.
\Box

(i).

E.25

, we have (recall that

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}))

For part (a.4), for any

)

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v}-{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v}-\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v**})+(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v**}-\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v*})+(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v*}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}})+(\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v})

and

E.24
(iii). This completes the proof of part (a).

To prove part (b), we need sharper rates and, to this end, we use the closed form expressions of the estimated parameters in (
14
), (
15
), and (
16
).

Start with part (b.1). From (
14
), for any
k^{*}\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle+\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\bigg\{2T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}\bigg\}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}

\displaystyle-2\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})x_{it}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}+(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})^{\prime}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})-2(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})^{\prime}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle+\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\bigg\{2T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}\bigg\}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}

\displaystyle-2\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})x_{it}.

(B.74)

Therefore, since by construction
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})=0
, from (
B.74
)

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}\right|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|^{2}\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|^{2}\bigg\{2\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bigg\|+\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}\bigg\|

\displaystyle+\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\bigg\|\bigg\}+2\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|\,\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})x_{it}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|^{2}\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|^{2}\{2\mathcal{A}+\mathcal{B}+\mathcal{C}\}+2\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|\mathcal{D},\;\text{say.}

(B.75)

For term
\mathcal{A}
on the rhs of (
B.75
) we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(B.76)

by Lemma
G.2
(i). Term
\mathcal{B}
is dominated by term
\mathcal{A}
. For term
\mathcal{C}
on the rhs of (
B.75
) we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(B.77)

by Lemma
F.3
(iv) when
k^{*}\geq 1
and by Lemma
D.12
when
k^{*}=0
.
For term
\mathcal{D}
on the rhs of (
B.75
) we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\leq

\displaystyle\,\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}\bigg\|\,\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\xi_{it}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(B.78)

by Lemmas
G.2
(i) and
G.2
(ii), and Assumption
1
(a).

Now by substituting (
B.76
), (
B.77
), and (
B.78
) into (
B.75
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}\right|=

\displaystyle\,|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|=\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|^{2}\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

(B.79)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

by (
B.28
)-(
B.31
) in the proof of Proposition
2
, Lemma
C.12
(i) combined with Assumption
6
(b), and
since
\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
by Proposition
2
and Assumption
1
(a).

Therefore, from (
B.79
) and Lemma
D.19
(i),
if
n^{-1}\sqrt{T}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\to 0
, as
n,T\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2})=

\displaystyle\,\sqrt{T}({\sigma}_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}-\sigma_{i}^{2})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}^{2}+\sqrt{T}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})-\sqrt{T}\sigma_{i}^{2}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\xi_{it}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}\}+o_{p}(1),

(B.80)

where we used also (
D.65
) in the proof of Lemma
D.19
.
Now, by Assumption
2
(c) and
Davidson 1994
, we have that
\{\xi_{it}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}\}
is strongly mixing with exponentially decaying coefficients
and such that by Assumption
5

\sup_{m\geq 1}r^{-1/\delta_{\xi}}(\mathbb{E}[|\xi_{it}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|^{m}])^{1/m}\leq K_{1}

for some finite positive real
K_{1}
independent of
t
and
i

(
Kuchibhotla and
Chakrabortty 2022
, Section 2)
. Then, the Central Limit Theorem by
Ibragimov 1962
applies, i.e.,

\displaystyle T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\xi_{it}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}\}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\rightarrow}}\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{4}]-\sigma_{i}^{4}).

(B.81)

From (
B.80
), (
B.81
), and Slutsky’s Theorem, and by noticing that the excess kurtosis is given by
\kappa_{i}=\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{4}]/\sigma_{i}^{4}-3
, so that

\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{4}]=\sigma_{i}^{4}(\kappa_{i}+3)
, we prove part (b.1).

For part (b.2), from (
15
), for any
k^{*}\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}

\displaystyle\,-\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\leq\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left({\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{C}}^{(k^{*})}_{t,t-1|T}\right)-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\|\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left({\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{P}}^{(k^{*})}_{t-1|T}\right)\right\}^{-1}-\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\}^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left({\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{C}}^{(k^{*})}_{t,t-1|T}\right)-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left({\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{P}}^{(k^{*})}_{t-1|T}\right)\right\}^{-1}-\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathfrak{A}+\mathfrak{B}+\mathfrak{C},\;\text{say.}

(B.82)

Now,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\|\,+\max_{t=2,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{C}}^{(k^{*})}_{t,t-1|T}\|\right\}\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(B.83)

and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\|+\max_{t=2,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}^{(k^{*})}_{t-1|T}\|\right\}

\displaystyle\cdot\left\|\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left({\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{P}}^{(k^{*})}_{t-1|T}\right)\right\}^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(B.84)

where we used: Lemma
G.2
(i), Lemma
F.3
(iv) when
k^{*}\geq 1
or Lemma
D.12
when
k^{*}=0
which can be both applied to
\mathbf{C}_{t,t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}
can be obtained by the upper right block of
\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}
when this is computed from the the Kalman smoother having the augmented state vector
(\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\,\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime})^{\prime}
,
and we also used
the fact that
\|(T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
by Lemma (
C.13
).
For
\mathfrak{B}
we also used Lemma
C.12
(i) combined with the fact that
\|\bm{\Gamma}_{1}^{F}\|\leq 1
by Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and Assumption
6
(b). Clearly
\mathfrak{C}
is dominated by
\mathfrak{A}
and
\mathfrak{B}
.

By substituting (
B.83
) and (
B.84
) into (
B.82
), we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})).

Therefore,
if
n^{-1}\sqrt{T}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\to 0
, as
n,T\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}})-\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}))=\sqrt{T}(\text{vec}({\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})-\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}))+o_{p}(1),

and the proof of part (b.2) follows directly from
Hamilton 1994
and Slutsky’s Theorem.

For part (b.3), following a decomposition analogous to the one in (
B.74
), from (
16
), for any
k^{*}\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v}=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})(\mathbf{F}_{t}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}+(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})^{\prime}

\displaystyle-(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})^{\prime}

\displaystyle+T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}({\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{P}}^{(k^{*})}_{t|T})-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}+\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}({\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{P}}^{(k^{*})}_{t-1|T})-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}({\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{C}}^{(k^{*})\prime}_{t,t-1|T})-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\}

\displaystyle-\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}({\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k^{*})\prime}+{\mathbf{C}}^{(k^{*})}_{t,t-1|T})-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}.

Therefore, since by construction
T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})\mathbf{F}_{t-1}^{\prime}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}
, by using again Lemmas
G.2

and Lemma
F.3
(iv) when
k^{*}\geq 1
or Lemma
D.12
when
k^{*}=0
which can be both applied to
\mathbf{C}_{t,t-1|T}^{(k^{*})}
, as argued above,
and using also (
B.73
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})).

Therefore,
if
n^{-1}\sqrt{T}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\to 0
, as
n,T\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\text{vech}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})-\text{vech}(\bm{\Gamma}^{v}))=\sqrt{T}(\text{vech}({\bm{\Gamma}}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}})-\text{vech}(\bm{\Gamma}^{v}))+o_{p}(1),

and the proof of part (b.3) follows directly from
Hamilton 1994
and Slutsky’s Theorem. This completes the proof.

\Box

B.6
Proof of Corollary
1

For part (a), for ease of notation and without loss of generality let
s(j)=j
for all
j=1,\ldots,\bar{n}
.
Then, from (
B.28
), since
\bar{n}
is finite,

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}(\text{vec}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{\bar{n}})-\text{vec}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{\bar{n}}))=\left\{\mathbf{I}_{\bar{n}}\otimes\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\text{vec}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}t}^{\prime})\right)+o_{p}(1).

(B.85)

Moreover, since
\bar{n}
is finite we can still apply the Central Limit Theorem by
Ibragimov 1962
so that, as
T\to\infty
,

T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\text{vec}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}t}^{\prime})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0}_{\bar{n}r},\bm{\Sigma}_{\bar{n}}),

(B.86)

with

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{\bar{n}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{T\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{1t}\\
\vdots\\
\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{\bar{n}t}\end{array}\right)\right\}\left\{T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{1t}\\
\vdots\\
\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{\bar{n}t}\end{array}\right)\right\}^{\prime}\,\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{T\to\infty}T^{-1}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}t}\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\right]=\lim_{T\to\infty}T^{-1}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}t}\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}s}^{\prime}\right]\otimes\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\right],

(B.93)

where we used the fact that
\{\mathbf{F}_{t}\}
and
\{\bm{\xi}_{nt}\}
are independent processes because of Lemma
C.11
.
The proof of part (a.1) follows from Lemmas
C.12
(i) and
C.13
, (
B.85
), (
B.86
), and Slutsky’s theorem. For part (a.2) just notice that, if
\mathbb{E}[(\bm{\xi}_{n1}^{\prime}\cdots\bm{\xi}_{nT}^{\prime})^{\prime}(\bm{\xi}_{n1}^{\prime}\cdots\bm{\xi}_{nT}^{\prime})]=\mathbf{I}_{T}\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}
for all
n,T\in\mathbb{N}
, then

\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}t}\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}s}^{\prime}\right]=\mathbf{0}_{\bar{n}\times\bar{n}}
if
t\neq s
while
\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}t}\bm{\xi}_{\bar{n}t}^{\prime}\right]=\bm{\Sigma}_{\bar{n}}^{\xi}
. By substituting into (
B.93
) we get
\bm{\Sigma}_{\bar{n}}=\bm{\Sigma}_{\bar{n}}^{\xi}\otimes\bm{\Gamma}^{F}
, and

\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{\bar{n}}=(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{n}}\otimes\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\bar{n}}^{\xi}\otimes\bm{\Gamma}^{F})(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{n}}\otimes\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}=\bm{\Sigma}_{\bar{n}}^{\xi}\otimes(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}
, thus proving part (a.2).

Turning to part (b), for ease of notation and without loss of generality let
s(j)=j
for all
j=1,\ldots,\bar{T}
.
Then, from (
B.64
) in the proof of Proposition
3
, since
\bar{T}
is finite

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\bm{F}}_{\bar{T}}-\bm{F}_{\bar{T}})=\left(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes n^{-1/2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right)\bm{\Xi}_{n\bar{T}}+o_{p}(1).

(B.94)

where
\bm{\Xi}_{n\bar{T}}=(\bm{\xi}_{n1}^{\prime}\cdots\bm{\xi}_{n\bar{T}}^{\prime})^{\prime}
. Moreover, since
\bar{T}
is finite we can still apply the Central Limit Theorem in Assumption
2
(e), so that as
n\to\infty

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes n^{-1/2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right)\bm{\Xi}_{n\bar{T}}=n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\text{vec}\left(\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}(\xi_{i1}\cdots\xi_{i\bar{T}})\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0}_{\bar{T}r},\bm{\Omega}_{\bar{T}}),

(B.95)

with

\displaystyle\bm{\Omega}_{\bar{T}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\left(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right)\mathbb{E}[\bm{\Xi}_{n\bar{T}}\bm{\Xi}_{n\bar{T}}^{\prime}]\left(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\xi_{i1}\\
\vdots\\
\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\xi_{iT}\end{array}\right)\right\}\left\{n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\xi_{i1}\\
\vdots\\
\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\xi_{iT}\end{array}\right)\right\}^{\prime}\,\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left\{\mathbb{E}[\bm{\zeta}_{i\bar{T}}\bm{\zeta}_{j\bar{T}}^{\prime}]\otimes\left({\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{j}^{\prime}}(\sigma_{i}^{2}\sigma_{j}^{2})^{-1}\right)\right\},

and recall that
\bm{\zeta}_{i\bar{T}}=(\xi_{i1}\cdots\xi_{i\bar{T}})^{\prime}
. The proof of part (b.1) follows from (
B.66
) in the proof of Proposition
3
, (
B.94
), (
B.95
), and Slutsky’s theorem. For part (b.2) just notice that, if
\mathbb{E}[(\bm{\zeta}_{1T}^{\prime}\cdots\bm{\zeta}_{nT}^{\prime})^{\prime}(\bm{\zeta}_{1T}^{\prime}\cdots\bm{\zeta}_{nT}^{\prime})]=\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{T}
for all
n,T\in\mathbb{N}
, then

\mathbb{E}[\bm{\zeta}_{i\bar{T}}\bm{\zeta}_{j\bar{T}}^{\prime}]=\mathbf{0}_{\bar{T}\times\bar{T}}
if
i\neq j
while
\mathbb{E}[\bm{\zeta}_{i\bar{T}}\bm{\zeta}_{i\bar{T}}^{\prime}]=\sigma_{i}^{2}\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}
. By substituting into (
B.93
) we get
\bm{\Omega}_{\bar{T}}=\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda}
, and

\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{\bar{T}}=(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})(\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}=\mathbf{I}_{\bar{T}}\otimes(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}
, thus proving part (b.2).
This completes the proof.
\Box

B.7
Proof of Proposition
6

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, from
Barigozzi 2023
, the asymptotic covariances of the PC estimator of the loadings is

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny PC}}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1}\left(\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}]\right)(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})^{-1},

(B.102)

where
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, by Assumption
6
(b). Therefore, the expression of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny PC}}
in (
B.102
)
coincides with the expression of
\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{i}
for the asymptotic covariances of the EM estimator given in Proposition
2
(b). This proves part (a).

Turning to part (b). Since, because of Proposition
3
,
\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})]=\mathbf{0}_{r}
, then

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}n\mathbb{C}\mathrm{ov}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t},\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]-n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})]\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

(B.103)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}n\{(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\}.

Similarly, because of Lemma
H.1
,
\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})]=\mathbf{0}_{r}
, which implies

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny PC}}=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}n\mathbb{C}\mathrm{ov}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t},\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]-n\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})]\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

(B.104)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}n\{(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}n\{(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\},

where we used the fact that
\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}
, because of Assumption
6
(b).

Moreover, if
\sqrt{n\log n}/T\to 0
(which implies also
\sqrt{n}/T\to 0
), Proposition
3
(see in particular (
B.64
) in its proof) and Lemma
H.1
(see in particular (
H.8
) in its proof) jointly imply that

\displaystyle\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\\
\sqrt{n}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\sqrt{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\\
\sqrt{n}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\end{array}\right)+o_{p}(1)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0}_{2r},\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}&\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}\\
\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\prime}&\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny PC}}\end{array}\right)\right),

where

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n\mathbb{C}\mathrm{ov}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t},\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]-n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})]\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

(B.111)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}n\{(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\}.

From (
B.103
) and (
B.104
) it follows that

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{W}}^{\text{\tiny PC}}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}=

\displaystyle\,\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle+\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}+\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{1}+\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{2}+\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{2}^{\prime},\;\text{say.}

(B.112)

Let us now define
\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}=\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}-\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}
, which is the
n\times n
matrix of off-diagonal entries of the idiosyncratic covariance. Because of (
B.103
), (
B.111
), and
Lemma
C.9
we can write

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{2}=

\displaystyle\,\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]-n\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}=\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}

(B.113)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\{n(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\left[\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\{n(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}\left[\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\{\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\right\}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1/2}

\displaystyle-(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\{\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\right\}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{1/2}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}.

Now, let
\bm{\mathfrak{V}}_{n}=\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}
, then, for any
h,k=1,\ldots,r
,

\displaystyle[\bm{\mathfrak{V}}_{n}]_{hk}

\displaystyle=\!\!\!\sum_{i,j,\ell,m=1}^{n}[\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}]_{hi}\,[(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}]_{ij}\,[\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}]_{j\ell}[(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}]_{\ell m}\,[\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}]_{mk}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell=1\\
i\neq\ell\end{subarray}}^{n}[\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}]_{ih}\,[(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}]_{ii}\,[\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}]_{i\ell}[(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}]_{\ell\ell}\,[\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}]_{\ell k}.

(B.114)

Now, let
\bm{\iota}_{ni}
be the
n
-dimensional vector with
i
th entry equal one and all other equal zero, and let
\bm{s}_{j}
the
r
-dimensional vector with
j
th entry equal one and all other equal zero. Then, since
\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}=\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}
, there exists a finite positive integer
\bar{n}
, such that, for all
n\geq\bar{n}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}|v_{ij}^{\chi}|

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}|\bm{\iota}_{ni}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\bm{s}_{j}|

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}|\bm{\iota}_{ni}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\bm{s}_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\iota}_{ni}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\chi}\|\,\|\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\|\,\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}|(\mu_{jn}^{\chi})^{-1}|

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\|{n^{-1}\underline{C}_{r}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\|\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\|\,\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|{n^{-1}\underline{C}_{r}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq M_{\lambda}^{2}M_{F}{n^{-1/2}\underline{C}_{r}^{-1}},

(B.115)

where we used Assumptions
1
(a) and
1
(b), and Lemmas
C.1
(iv) and
C.2
.

Therefore, from (
B.114
) and (
B.115
), and Assumption
2
(a),

\displaystyle\max_{h,k=1,\ldots r}|[\bm{\mathfrak{V}}_{n}]_{hk}|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{h=1,\ldots r}\max_{i=1,\ldots n}|[\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}]_{ih}|\right)^{2}\left(\max_{i=1,\ldots n}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\right)^{2}\sum_{i,\ell=1}^{n}|[\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}]_{i\ell}|

\displaystyle\leq{C_{v}^{2}}\left\{n\left(\min_{i=1,\ldots n}\sigma_{i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right\}^{-1}\sum_{i,\ell=1}^{n}|[\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}]_{i\ell}|\leq n^{-1}{C_{v}^{2}C_{\xi}^{2}}\sum_{i,\ell=1}^{n}|[\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}]_{i\ell}|.

(B.116)

Moreover, since by assumption we have

\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\sum_{i,\ell=1}^{n}|[\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}]_{i\ell}|=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i,\ell=1\\
i\neq\ell\end{subarray}}^{n}|[\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}]_{i\ell}|=0,

then, from (
B.116
), we have
\lim_{n\to\infty}\max_{h,k=1,\ldots r}|[\bm{\mathfrak{V}}_{n}]_{hk}|=0
.
Therefore,

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\{\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\right\}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}.

(B.117)

Following the same reasoning it also holds that

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\{\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{O}}_{n}^{\xi}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\right\}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}.

(B.118)

By substituting, (
B.117
) and (
B.118
) into (
B.113
), we obtain

\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{2}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}.

Finally,

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{1}=

\displaystyle\,\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}+\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle-\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}-\lim_{n,T\to\infty}\left\{n\,\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{\prime}]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}+\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny PC}}-(\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}+\bm{\mathcal{U}}_{t}^{\prime}).

Therefore,
\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{1}
is positive definite since
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
and
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny PC}}
are positive definite by Assumptions
1
(a),
2
(a), and
2
(f),
and, from (
B.112
) we prove part (ii). This completes the proof.
\Box

Appendix C
General lemmas

Lemma C.1
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
:

(i)

for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
and
T\in\mathbb{N}
,
(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{js}]|\leq M_{1}
, for some finite positive real
M_{1}
independent of
n
and
T
;

(ii)

for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
and
t\in\mathbb{Z}
,
n^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]|\leq M_{2}
, for some finite positive real
M_{2}
independent of
n
and
t
;

(iii)

for all
i\in\mathbb{N}
and
T\in\mathbb{N}
,
T^{-1}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]|\leq M_{3}
, for some finite positive real
M_{3}
independent of
i
and
T
;

(iv)

for all
j=1,\ldots,r
,
\underline{C}_{j}\!\leq\lim\inf_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}{\mu_{jn}^{\chi}}\leq\lim\sup_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}{\mu_{jn}^{\chi}}\leq\!\overline{C}_{j}
,
for some finite positive reals
\underline{C}_{j}
and
\overline{C}_{j}
;

(v)

for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
\mu_{1n}^{\xi}=\|\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\|\leq M_{2}
, where
M_{2}
is defined in part (ii);

(vi)

for all
j=1,\ldots,r
,
\underline{C}_{j}\leq\lim\inf_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}{\mu_{jn}^{x}}\leq\lim\sup_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}{\mu_{jn}^{\chi}}\leq\overline{C}_{j}
, and for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
\mu_{r+1,n}^{x}\leq M_{2}
, where
M_{2}
is defined in part (ii).

Proof.
Using Assumptions
2
(a) and
2
(b), we have:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{js}]|

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=-(T-1)}^{T-1}\left(1-\frac{|k|}{T}\right)|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{j,t-k}]|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=-\infty}^{\infty}\rho^{|k|}M_{ij}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=-\infty}^{\infty}\rho^{|k|}M_{ii}+n^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1,i\neq j}^{n}\sum_{k=-\infty}^{\infty}\rho^{|k|}M_{ij}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=-\infty}^{\infty}\rho^{|k|}\sigma_{i}^{2}+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i}^{n}\sum_{k=-\infty}^{\infty}\rho^{|k|}M_{ij}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{\xi}(1+\rho)}{1-\rho}+\frac{M_{\xi}(1+\rho)}{1-\rho}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}^{2}+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i}^{n}M_{ij}\leq C_{\xi}+M_{\xi},

and

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]|

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=-(T-1)}^{T-1}\left(1-\frac{|k|}{T}\right)|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{i,t-k}]|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=-\infty}^{\infty}\rho^{|k|}M_{ii}\leq\frac{1+\rho}{1-\rho}\sigma_{i}^{2}\leq\frac{C_{\xi}(1+\rho)}{1-\rho}.

Defining,
M_{1}=\frac{(C_{\xi}+M_{\xi})(1+\rho)}{1-\rho}
,
M_{2}=C_{\xi}+M_{\xi}
, and
M_{3}=\frac{C_{\xi}(1+\rho)}{1-\rho}
, we prove parts (i), (ii), and (iii).

For part (iv), first notice that, for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
, the
r
non-zero eigenvalues of
\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\chi}
are also the
r
eigenvalues of
n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}
. Thus, by
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, for all
j=1,\ldots,r
and all
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we have

n^{-1}{\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})}\ \nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})\leq n^{-1}{\mu_{jn}^{\chi}}\leq n^{-1}{\nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})}\ \nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F}).

The proof then follows from Assumptions
1
(a) and
1
(b). Indeed, by continuity of eigenvalues, Assumption
1
(a) implies that, for all
j=1,\ldots,r
, and all
n>N_{0}

n^{-1}{\nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})}=\nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}),

(C.1)

and there exist finite positive reals
