m_{\lambda}
and
M_{\lambda}
such that
0<m_{\lambda}^{2}\leq\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})\leq\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})\leq M_{\lambda}^{2}<\infty
.
Similarly, Assumption
1
(b), implies that there exist finite positive reals
m_{F}
and
M_{F}
such that
0<m_{F}\leq\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})\leq\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})\leq M_{F}<\infty
.

For part (v), by Assumptions
2
(a) and
2
(b):

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\|\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]|=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\sigma_{i}^{2}+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i}^{n}M_{ij}\leq C_{\xi}+M_{\xi}=M_{2}.

Part (vi) follows from parts (iv) and (v) and Weyl’s inequality
(
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, Theorem 1)
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.2
.

Under Assumption
1
, as
n\to\infty
,
n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O(1)
.

Proof.

By definition and using Assumption
1
(a),

\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1/2}\sqrt{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})}=\sqrt{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})}\leq M_{\lambda}.

This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.3
.

For any
r\times r
symmetric and positive definite matrix
\bm{P}
with
\|\bm{P}\|\leq C_{P}
for some finite positive real
C_{P}
, under Assumptions
1
and
2
, as
n\to\infty
,

(i)

n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}\|=O(1)
;

(ii)

n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}\|=O(1)
;

(iii)

n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(1)
;

(iv)

n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(1)
;

(v)

n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O(1)
;

(vi)

n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O(1)
;

(vii)

n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O(1)
;

(viii)

n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O(1)
.

Proof.
By
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004

\displaystyle n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})}\leq\frac{n}{\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})+\nu^{(r)}(\bm{P}^{-1})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime})\nu^{(n)}((\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1})+[\nu^{(1)}(\bm{P})]^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})[\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})]^{-1}+[\nu^{(1)}(\bm{P})]^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})C_{\xi}^{-1}+C_{P}^{-1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{-1}\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})C_{\xi}^{-1}+n^{-1}C_{P}^{-1}},

(C.2)

by Assumption
2
(a) and since
\bm{P}
finite by assumption. Then, as shown in (
C.1
) in the proof of Lemma
C.1
(iv),

\underline{C}_{j}\leq\lim\inf_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\leq\lim\sup_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\leq\overline{C}_{j},\hskip 18.49988ptj=1,\ldots,r.

(C.3)

Thus, we have
\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\geq\underline{C}_{r}
, which, once substituted in (
C.2
), proves part (i).

For part (ii) the proof is the same as part (i), but in (
C.2
) we use Lemma
C.1
(v) instead of Assumption
2
(a), thus replacing
C_{\xi}
with
M_{2}
.
Parts (iii) and (iv) are obvious by just setting
\bm{P}^{-1}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}
in the first step of (
C.2
).

For part (v) we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})=n^{-1}\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1})

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime})\nu^{(1)}((\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1})=\frac{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})}{n\nu^{(n)}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})}{nC_{\xi}^{-1}},

(C.4)

by Assumption
2
(a). Then, by (
C.3
) in the proof of Lemma
C.6
, we have
\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(1)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\leq\overline{C}_{1}
, which, once substituted in (
C.4
), proves part (v).

For part (vi) the proof is the same as part (v), but in (
C.4
) we use Assumption
2
(f) instead of Assumption
2
(a), thus replacing
C_{\xi}^{-1}
with
L_{\xi}
.

For part (vii)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\leq\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\leq\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}=O(\sqrt{n}),

by Lemma
C.2
and Assumption
2
(a). And for part (viii) the proof is the same as for part (vii) but using Assumption
2
(f) instead of Assumption
2
(a). This proves parts (vii) and (viii) and completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.4
.

Given two invertible matrices
\bm{K}
and
\bm{H}
the following holds:

(\bm{H}+\bm{K})^{-1}=\bm{K}^{-1}-(\bm{H}+\bm{K})^{-1}\bm{H}\bm{K}^{-1}.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle(\bm{H}+\bm{K})^{-1}

\displaystyle=(\bm{H}+\bm{K})^{-1}-\bm{K}^{-1}+\bm{K}^{-1}=(\bm{H}+\bm{K})^{-1}(\bm{K}-(\bm{H}+\bm{K}))\bm{K}^{-1}+\bm{K}^{-1}

\displaystyle=(\bm{H}+\bm{K})^{-1}(-\bm{H})\bm{K}^{-1}+\bm{K}^{-1}=\bm{K}^{-1}-(\bm{H}+\bm{K})^{-1}\bm{H}\bm{K}^{-1}.

(C.5)

This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.5
.

For
m<n
with
m
independent of
n
and given

(a)

an
m\times m
matrix
\bm{A}
symmetric and positive definite with
\|\bm{A}\|\leq M_{A}
;

(b)

an
n\times n
matrix
\bm{B}
symmetric and positive definite with
\|\bm{B}\|\leq M_{B}
;

(c)

an
n\times m
matrix
\bm{C}
such that
\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{C}
is positive definite with

\underline{M}_{C_{j}}\leq\lim\inf_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{C})\leq\lim\sup_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{C})\leq\overline{M}_{C_{j}},\hskip 18.49988ptj=1,\ldots,m;

where,
M_{A}
,
M_{B}
,
\underline{M}_{C_{j}}
and
\overline{M}_{C_{j}}
are finite positive reals independent of
n
and
m
, then the following holds

\|(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}-\mathbf{I}_{m}\|=O(n^{-1}).

Proof.
In Lemma
C.4
set
\bm{K}=\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}
and
\bm{H}=\bm{A}^{-1}
, then,

\displaystyle(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}=(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}-(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{A}^{-1}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}.

(C.6)

which implies

(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}-\mathbf{I}_{m}=(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{A}^{-1}.

(C.7)

Then,
by Weyl’s inequality
(
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, Theorem 1)
:

\nu^{(m)}(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})\geq\nu^{(m)}(\bm{A}^{-1})+\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})=\{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{A})\}^{-1}+\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}).

(C.8)

From (
C.8
), we have

\displaystyle\|(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{A}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\|\ \|\bm{A}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=\left\{\nu^{(m)}(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})\right\}^{-1}\ \left\{\nu^{(m)}(\bm{A})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})+\{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{A})\}^{-1}\right\}^{-1}\ \left\{\nu^{(m)}(\bm{A})\right\}^{-1}.

(C.9)

For first term on the rhs of (
C.9
), the
m
eigenvalues of
\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}
are also the
m
largest non-zero eigenvalues of
\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}
, and the
m
largest non-zero eigenvalues of
\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\prime}
are also the
m
eigenvalues of
\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{C}
. Therefore, because of
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
:

n^{-1}\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1})\geq n^{-1}\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\prime})\nu^{(m)}(\bm{B}^{-1})=n^{-1}\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{C})\left\{{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{B})}\right\}^{-1}.

Thus, by conditions (b) and (c)

n\left\{\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})\right\}^{-1}\leq n\left\{\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{C})\right\}^{-1}\nu^{(1)}(\bm{B})\leq\frac{M_{B}}{\underline{M}_{C_{m}}}.

Moreover, by condition (a),
\nu^{(1)}(\bm{A})>0
and
\nu^{(1)}(\bm{A})\leq M_{A}
, i.e.,
\{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{A})\}^{-1}\geq M_{A}
, and

\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})+\{\nu^{(1)}(\bm{A})\}^{-1}\geq\nu^{(m)}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})\geq\frac{M_{B}}{\underline{M}_{C_{m}}}.

For the second term on the rhs of (
C.9
), by condition (a),

\left\{\nu^{(m)}(\bm{A})\right\}^{-1}\leq\frac{1}{L}_{A},

for some finite positive real
L_{A}
. Hence, from (
C.9
)

n\|(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{A}^{-1}\|\leq\frac{M_{B}}{\underline{M}_{C_{m}}L_{A}}.

and by using it in (
C.7
) we complete the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.6
.

For any
r\times r
symmetric and positive definite matrix
\bm{P}
with
\|\bm{P}\|\leq M_{P}
for some finite positive real
M_{P}
, under Assumptions
1
and
2
, as
n\to\infty
,

(i)

n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\|=O(1)
;

(ii)

n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\|=O(1)
;

(iii)

n^{2}\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(1)
;

(iv)

n^{2}\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(1)
.

Proof.

Both results follow from Lemma
C.5
since:
\bm{P}
satisfies condition (a) by assumption,
\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}
satisfies condition (b) because of Assumption
2
(a) and
\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}
satisfies condition (b) because of Lemma
C.1
(v) and Assumption
2
(f), and
{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}
satisfies condition (c) since it is positive definite because of Assumptions
1
(a), and, moreover, its eigenvalues are such that

\underline{C}_{j}\leq\lim\inf_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\leq\lim\sup_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\leq\overline{C}_{j}
, for
j=1,\ldots,r,
as shown in (
C.3
) in the proof of Lemma
C.3
.

Turning to part (iii),

\displaystyle n^{2}\|

\displaystyle(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq n^{2}\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\bm{P}^{-1})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})-\mathbf{I}_{r}\|\,\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(1).

by part (i) and Lemma
C.3
(iii). This proves part (iii).

Part (iv) is proved in the same way but using Lemma
C.3
(iv) instead of Lemma
C.3
(iv).
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.7
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(ii)

n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(iii)

n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\left\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}^{\prime}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

(vi)

n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

where
\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}=(\bm{\xi}_{n1}\cdots\bm{\xi}_{nT})^{\prime}
.

Proof.
Throughout, let
\lambda_{ij}
be the
(i,j)
the entry of
\bm{\Lambda}_{n}
.
For part (i), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{r}n^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\lambda_{ij}\xi_{it}}{\sigma_{i}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq r\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}n^{-2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{|\lambda_{ij}|\,|\lambda_{kj}|}{\sigma_{i}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{it}\xi_{kt}\right]

\displaystyle\leq rn^{-1}M_{\lambda}^{2}C_{\xi}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{kt}]|\leq rM_{\lambda}^{2}C_{\xi}^{2}M_{2},

(C.10)

where in the third step we used Assumption
1
(a) (since
\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}|\lambda_{ij}|\leq\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\leq M_{\lambda}
, for all
i=1,\ldots,n
), and Assumption
2
(a), and in the last step we used Lemma
C.1
(ii).
By Chebychev’s inequality and since the constants in (
C.10
) do not depend on
t
, we prove part (i).

For part (ii), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[n^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[n^{-1}\text{tr}\left\{\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\text{tr}\left\{\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}\right](\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right\}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\text{tr}\left\{\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq rn^{-1}\nu^{(1)}\left(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right)

\displaystyle=rn^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O(1),

(C.11)

by Lemma
C.3
(vi). By Chebychev’s inequality and since the constants in (
C.11
) do not depend on
t
, we complete the proof of part (ii).

For part (iii), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\lambda_{ij}\xi_{it}}{\sigma_{i}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq r\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{|\lambda_{ij}|\,|\lambda_{kj}|}{\sigma_{i}^{2}\sigma_{k}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{it}\xi_{ks}\right]

\displaystyle\leq rn^{-1}T^{-1}M_{\lambda}^{2}C_{\xi}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{ks}]|\leq rM_{\lambda}^{2}C_{\xi}^{2}M_{1},

by Assumptions
1
(a),
2
(a), and Lemma
C.1
(i).
By Chebychev’s inequality we prove part (iii).

For part (iv), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda_{ik}\xi_{it}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}rM_{\lambda}^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]|\leq rM_{\lambda}^{2}M_{2},

by Assumption
1
(a) and Lemma
C.1
(ii). By Chebychev’s inequality we prove part (iv).

Part (v) is proved as parts (iv), but using also Assumption
2
(a).

For part (vi), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}\right\|^{2}_{F}\right]=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{2}]=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\sigma_{i}^{2}\leq C_{\xi},

by Assumption
2
(a). By Chebychev’s inequality we prove part (vi).
This completes the proof.

\Box

Lemma C.8
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\sqrt{nT}\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\sqrt{nT}\,\|n^{-3/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\sqrt{nT}\,\|n^{-2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\sqrt{nT}\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

\sqrt{nT}\|n^{-3/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1)
;

(vi)

\sqrt{nT}\|n^{-1}T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}\|_{F}=O_{p}(1);

where
\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}=(\bm{\xi}_{n1}\cdots\bm{\xi}_{nT})^{\prime}
.

Proof.

Throughout, let
\lambda_{ij}
be the
(i,j)
the entry of
\bm{\Lambda}_{n}
.
For part (i),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{F}\right]=

\displaystyle\,\,n^{-2}T^{-2}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\sum_{k,h=1}^{r}\lambda_{ih}\lambda_{jh}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{js}F_{kt}F_{ks}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-2}T^{-2}M_{\lambda}^{2}r^{2}\max_{k,h=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{js}]|\,|\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}F_{ks}]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}T^{-1}M_{\lambda}^{2}r^{2}M_{1},

(C.12)

by Lemma
C.1
(i) and because
|\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}F_{ks}]|\leq 1
by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Assumption
6
(b). By Chebychev’s inequality we prove part (i).

For part (ii),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\|n^{-3/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}_{F}\right]=\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-3/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda_{ik}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}n^{-1}T^{-1}\max_{j=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}n^{-2}T^{-2}\max_{j=1,\ldots,n}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{i,\ell=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\xi_{\ell s}\xi_{js}]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}n^{-1}T^{-1}\mathrm{K}_{\xi},

by Assumptions
1
(a) and
2
(d). By Chebychev’s inequality we prove part (ii).

For part (iii),
following the proof of part (ii),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|n^{-2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right\|^{2}_{F}\right]=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{k,h=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\lambda_{ik}\lambda_{jh}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}n^{-4}T^{-2}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\sum_{i_{1},j_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2},j_{2}=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i_{1}t}\xi_{j_{1}t}\xi_{i_{2}s}\xi_{j_{2}s}]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}n^{-1}T^{-1}\mathrm{K}_{\xi},

by Assumptions
1
(a) and
2
(d). By Chebychev’s inequality we prove part (iii).

Parts (iv) and (v) are proved as parts (i) and (ii), respectively, but using also Assumption
2
(a).

For part (vi)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\left\|n^{-1}T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right]=n^{-2}T^{-3}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}F_{kt}\xi_{it}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\xi_{is}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-2}T^{-3}C_{\xi}^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}=1}^{T}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[F_{kt_{1}}F_{kt_{2}}\xi_{it_{1}}\xi_{jt_{2}}\xi_{is}\xi_{js}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-2}T^{-2}rC_{\xi}^{2}\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\max_{t_{1},t_{2}=1,\ldots,T}|\mathbb{E}[F_{kt_{1}}F_{kt_{2}}]|\max_{s=1,\ldots,T}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}=1}^{T}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it_{1}}\xi_{jt_{2}}\xi_{is}\xi_{js}]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}T^{-1}rC_{\xi}^{2}\max_{k=1,\ldots,r}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\mathbb{E}[F_{kt}^{2}]\,\mathrm{K}_{\xi}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}T^{-1}rC_{\xi}^{2}\mathrm{K}_{\xi},

by Assumptions
2
(a) and
2
(d), Lemma
C.11
, and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality jointly with Assumption
6
(b). By Chebychev’s inequality we prove part (vi).
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.9
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
6
, for all
n>N_{0}
:

\|n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-n^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{S}}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}\|=0
,
for some
r\times r
positive diagonal matrix
\bm{\mathcal{S}}
independent of
n
with entries
\mathbb{I}([\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}]_{1j}\geq 0)-\mathbb{I}([\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}]_{1j}<0)
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, and where
N_{0}
is defined in Assumption
1
(a).

Proof.
By Assumption
6
(b), for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

n^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\chi}=n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}=n^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}.

(C.13)

First notice that, the
r
non-zero eigenvalues of
n^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\chi}
are the
r
eigenvalues of
n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}
and for all
n>N_{0}
,
\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}\|=0
, by Assumption
1
(a). Moreover,
\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}
is diagonal and positive definite by Assumptions
6
(b) and
1
(a), respectively. Hence, for all
n>N_{0}
,

n^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}=\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}\;\text{ and }\;n(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}=\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}.

(C.14)

Furthermore, it must be that the columns of
\bm{\Lambda}_{n}
span the same space as the columns of
\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}
. Since the eigenvectors are normalized and for all
n>N_{0}
,
\|n^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}-\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}\|=0
by (
C.14
), there exist two
r\times r
matrices
\mathbf{K}_{1n}
and
\mathbf{K}_{2n}
such that, for all
n>N_{0}
,

n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}=\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{K}_{1n}\;\text{ and }\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}=n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{K}_{2n}.

(C.15)

Let

\mathbf{K}_{1}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1/2}(\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}

and

\mathbf{K}_{2}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sqrt{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}.

Then, by linear projection from (
C.15
) we have that

\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbf{K}_{1n}=\mathbf{K}_{1},

(C.16)

which is positive definite since the columns of
\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}
are linear combinations of the columns of
\bm{\Lambda}_{n}
so, for all
n>N_{0}
,
\text{rk}(n^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})=\text{rk}(n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})=\text{rk}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})=r
by Assumption
1
(a). Moreover, for all
n>N_{0}
,
\|\mathbf{K}_{1}\|\leq n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|
, which is finite by Lemma
C.2
.

Similarly, from (
C.15
) we also have that

\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbf{K}_{2n}=\mathbf{K}_{2}.

(C.17)

which exists and is positive definite, since
\|n(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}-n\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|=0
, for all
n>N_{0}
, and
\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}
is finite and positive definite by Assumption
1
(a). Moreover, for all
n>N_{0}
,
\|\mathbf{K}_{2}\|\leq n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=\|\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|
, which is finite by Assumption
1
(a) and Lemma
C.2
.

By using (
C.15
) into the rhs of (
C.13
), we get, for all
n>N_{0}
,

n^{-2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{K}_{2n}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{K}_{2n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}=\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime},

which, since eigenvectors are normalized, implies, that, for all
n>N_{0}
, we can write

n^{-2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{K}_{2n}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}\mathbf{K}_{2n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}=n^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}.

(C.18)

From (
C.18
) we must have

\mathbf{I}_{r}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{K}_{2n}=\mathbf{K}_{1}\mathbf{K}_{2}
,
so, as expected
\mathbf{K}_{1}=\mathbf{K}_{2}^{-1}
and
\mathbf{K}_{2}=\mathbf{K}_{1}^{-1}
. It follows that, for all
n>N_{0}
,

\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}=\mathbf{I}_{r}.

(C.19)

Now, from (
C.13
), we can also write that for all
n>N_{0}

n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{R}_{n}=n^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2},

(C.20)

for some
r\times r
matrix
\bm{R}_{n}
. Let,

\bm{R}=\lim_{n\to\infty}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1/2}\mathbf{K}_{2n}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}=\mathbf{K}_{2}\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1/2}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}.

(C.21)

Hence, for all
n>N_{0}
,
\bm{R}=\mathbf{K}_{2}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{1/2}
by (
C.14
). So
\bm{R}
is finite by Assumption
1
(a) and since
\mathbf{K}_{2}
is finite.

Moreover, from (
C.21
)

\displaystyle\bm{R}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left\{\lim_{n\to\infty}\sqrt{n}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1/2}\right\}\mathbf{K}_{2}^{-1}=\left\{\lim_{n\to\infty}\sqrt{n}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1/2}\right\}\mathbf{K}_{1}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sqrt{n}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1/2}\mathbf{K}_{1n}=\lim_{n\to\infty}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}.

(C.22)

From (
C.21
) and (
C.22
), and using Assumption
1
(a), we also have

\displaystyle\bm{R}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}=\lim_{n\to\infty}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1/2}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1/2}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}

\displaystyle=\left\{\lim_{n\to\infty}n(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\right\}\bm{R}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}.

(C.23)

Hence, for all
n>N_{0}
, by (
C.14
),

\bm{R}^{-1}=(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{-1}\bm{R}^{\prime}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}
. Thus,
\bm{R}^{-1}
is finite by Assumption
1
(a) and since
\bm{R}_{2}
is finite.
It follows that
\bm{R}
is positive definite.

Moreover,
\bm{R}
is orthogonal, indeed, from (
C.13
) and (
C.21
)

\displaystyle\bm{R}\bm{R}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\chi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}=\mathbf{I}_{r}.

(C.24)

By substituting (
C.24
) into (
C.23
), for all
n>N_{0}
, by (
C.14
),

\displaystyle\bm{R}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\bm{R}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}.

(C.25)

By right-multiplying (
C.25
) by
\bm{R}
and left-multiplying by
\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}
we have

\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}=\bm{R}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}\bm{R},

which implies
\bm{R}=\bm{\mathcal{J}}
where
\bm{\mathcal{J}}
is an
r\times r
diagonal matrix with entries
\pm 1
independent of
n
. Therefore, from (
C.20
), for all
n>N_{0}
,

\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\mathcal{J}}=n^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}

or, equivalently, for all
n>N_{0}
,

n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}=n^{-1/2}\mathbf{V}_{n}^{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{J}}(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{1/2}.

Finally, by Assumption
6
(c) it must be that
\bm{\mathcal{J}}=\bm{\mathcal{S}}
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.10
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
3
,
as
n\to\infty
,
n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
.

Proof.

We have,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|n^{-1/2}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]=n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\bm{\lambda}_{i}+\sigma_{i}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\bm{\lambda}_{i}+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\sigma_{i}^{2}\leq M_{\lambda}^{2}\|\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\|\leq M_{\lambda}^{2}M_{F}+C_{\xi},

(C.26)

by Assumption
3
, Assumptions
1
(a) and
1
(b), and Assumption
2
(a). The proof of part (i) follows by Chebychev’s inequality and by noticing that the bound in (
C.26
) does not depend on
t
.
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.11
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
3
, the processes

\{\xi_{it},\,i\in\mathbb{N},\,t\in\mathbb{Z}\}
and
\{F_{jt},\,j=1,\ldots,r,\,t\in\mathbb{Z}\}
are mutually independent.

Proof.
It is enough to notice that
\mathbf{F}_{t}=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{A}^{k}\mathbf{H}\mathbf{u}_{t-k}
, then, by Assumption
3
, we complete the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.12
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
3
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

for
k=0,1
,
T\mathbb{E}[\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t-k}^{\prime}-\bm{\Gamma}^{F}_{k}\|_{F}^{2}]=O(1)
, with
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}_{k}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t-k}^{\prime}];

(ii)

T\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}[\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\|^{2}]=O(1)
;

(iii)

T\mathbb{E}[\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|_{F}^{2}]=O(1)
;

(iv)

T\max_{i,j=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}[|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]|^{2}]=O(1)
;

(v)

T\max_{i,j=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}[|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}x_{jt}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}x_{jt}]|^{2}]=O(1)
;

(vi)

T\mathbb{E}[\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}-n^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}^{\xi}_{n}\|_{F}^{2}]=O(1)
;

(vii)

T\mathbb{E}[\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\mathbf{x}_{nt}^{\prime}-n^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}^{x}_{n}\|_{F}^{2}]=O(1)
.

Proof.

Part (i) follows since
\{\mathbf{F}_{t}\}
is ergodic, because of Assumption
1
(d) which implies that
\{\mathbf{F}_{t}\}
has summable autocovariances, and therefore
\{\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t-k}^{\prime}\}
is also ergodic (
White 2001
, Theorem 3.35
, and
Stout 1974
, pp. 170, 182
). In particular,
\bm{\Gamma}_{k}^{F}
is finite because of Assumptions
1
(b),
1
(d) and
1
(e), and
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|^{4}\|]
is also finite because of Assumptions
1
(d)-
1
(g). See also
Hamilton 1994
, which can be applied using the fact that
\{\mathbf{v}_{t}\}
is an independent process by Assumption
1
(f) and thus it is a martingale difference process. This proves part (i).

For part (ii), as
T\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{jt}\xi_{it}\right)^{2}\right]=T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[F_{jt}\xi_{it}F_{js}\xi_{is}\right]

(C.27)

\displaystyle\leq T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}|\mathbb{E}\left[F_{jt}F_{js}\right]|\,|\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}\right]|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]|\mathbb{E}\left[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}\right]|\leq T^{-1}rM_{F}M_{3},

because of Lemma
C.1
(iii), and where we also used Lemma
C.11
, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and the fact that
F_{jt}
is weakly stationary by Assumptions
1
(b),
1
(d), and
1
(e). By noticing that the constants on the rhs of (
C.27
) do not depend on
i
we prove part (ii). Part (iii) follows directly from part (ii), indeed

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{jt}\xi_{it}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{jt}\xi_{it}\right)^{2}\right]\leq T^{-1}rM_{F}M_{3}.

For part (iv), notice that, since
\{\xi_{it}\}
is a strongly mixing process with exponentially decaying coefficients, because of Assumption
2
(c), then it is also ergodic
(
White 2001
, Proposition 3.44
, and
Rosenblatt 1972
), so that also
\{\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\}
is ergodic (
White 2001
, Theorem 3.35
, and
Stout 1974
, pp. 170, 182
).
In particular,
\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]
is finite by Assumption
2
(a) and
\mathbb{E}[|\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\xi_{is}\xi_{js}|]
is also finite because by Assumption
2
(d), and both are bounded by constant independent of
i
and
j
.

This proves part (iv). Part (v) follows directly from parts (i), (ii), and (iv). Part (vi) follows directly from part (iv), and part (vii) follows directly from part (v).
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma C.13
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
3
, as
n,T\to\infty
,
\|(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

From Lemma
C.12
(i), and
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
which is Weyl’s inequality

\displaystyle\left|\nu^{(r)}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)-\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})\right|\leq\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

This implies (note that
x-y\geq-|x-y|
for any
x,y\in\mathbb{R}
)

\displaystyle\det\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)=

\displaystyle\,\prod_{j=1}^{r}\nu^{(j)}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\geq\left\{\nu^{(r)}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right\}^{r}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})-\left|\nu^{(r)}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)-\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})\right|\right\}^{r}>0,

by Assumption
1
(b). Thus,
\|(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Appendix D
Lemmas necessary for proving Proposition
1

Lemma D.1
.

Consider the initial estimator of the loadings
\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}=(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{1}^{(0)}\cdots\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{\prime}
defined in Section
A.1
, then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
;

(ii)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
Both results are direct consequences of
Barigozzi 2023
, see also
Bai 2003
under similar assumptions. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.2
.

Consider the initial estimator of the factors
\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}
defined in Section
A.1
, then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

for
k=0,1
,
\min(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})\,\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=k+1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\mathbf{F}}_{t-k}^{\prime}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})\,\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\bm{\xi}}_{nt}^{\prime}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\min(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})\,\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\bm{\xi}}_{nt}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\min(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})\,\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\xi}_{it}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
;

(v)

\min(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})\,\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
For part (i), by definition

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\}\mathbf{F}_{t}+\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\right\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}

\displaystyle+({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}.

(D.1)

Using (
D.1
) the first term on the rhs of (
D.4
) is such that

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{n}\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\right\}n^{-1/2}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\right\}O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle+\|n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\left\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(D.2)

by Lemma
D.1
(ii), which does not depend on
t
, and Lemmas
C.2
(jointly with Assumption
1
(a)),
C.12
(i), and
C.12
(iii), and part (i).
The case
k=1
is proved in the same way and this proves part (iii).

For part (ii), as in part (i), we have

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\bm{\xi}}_{nt}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\bm{\xi}}_{nt}^{\prime}\right\|+\left\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\bm{\xi}}_{nt}^{\prime}\right\|\right\}O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle+\|n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\left\|n^{-3/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\bm{\xi}}_{nt}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

by Lemma
D.1
(ii), which does not depend on
t
, and Lemmas
C.2
(jointly with Assumption
1
(a)),
C.12
(iii), and
C.12
(vi), and Lemma
C.8
(ii). This proves part (ii).

Part (iii) follows directly from part (ii) but using Lemma
C.8
(iii).

For part (iv), as in part (i), we have

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\xi}_{it}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\xi}_{it}\right\|+\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\xi}_{it}\right\|\right\}O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle+\|n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\left\|n^{-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\xi}_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

by the same arguments used to prove part (ii) and noticing that now
|\xi_{it}|=O_{p}(1)
by Assumption
2
(a). Part (v) is proved as part (iv) setting
\xi_{it}=1
.
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.3
.

Consider the initial estimator of the VAR parameters
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}
and
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(0)}
defined in Section
A.1
, then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(0)}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

For part (i), in agreement with Assumption
1
(i), we can always set
\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{0}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
, so it follows that, by construction
T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
. Then, by Assumption
6
(b), we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}-\bm{\Gamma}_{1}^{F}

\displaystyle=\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\}+\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}-\bm{\Gamma}_{1}^{F}\right\}.

(D.3)

Now,

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}){\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}+T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}-\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}

\displaystyle+T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}-\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}.

(D.4)

Now, by Lemma
D.2
(i) the first and second term in the rhs of (
D.4
) are
O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))
, while the third term on the rhs is dominated by the first two. Hence, for the first term on the rhs of (
D.3
) we have

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

The second term on the rhs of (
D.3
) is
O_{p}(T^{-1/2})
, by Lemma
C.12
(i).
This proves part (i).

Part (ii) follows from the results in
Forni
et al. 2009
combined with part (i). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.4
.

Consider the initial estimator of the idiosyncratic variances
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}
,
i=1,\ldots,n
, defined in Section
A.1
, then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{(0)2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
;

(ii)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,n^{-1}|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{(0)2}-\sigma_{i}^{2})|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

Start from

\displaystyle(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2})=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[(x_{it}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right\}+\left\{\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right\}

\displaystyle-2\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right\}-2\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\right\},

(D.5)

since
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
by Assumption
6
(b),
\mathbb{E}[{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\xi_{it}]=\mathbf{0}_{r}
by Lemma
C.11
, and
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
by construction.
For part (i), from (
D.5
) we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right|+\left|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle+2\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right|+2\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\right|

\displaystyle+2\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\right|+2\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right|+\left|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle+2\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+2\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|\,\{\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\}

\displaystyle+2\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|\,\{\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\}

\displaystyle+2\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|+2\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle+2\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|\,\{\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(D.6)

where, we used multiple times Assumption
1
(a) and Lemma
D.1
(i), and, we also used:
Lemma
C.12
(v) for the first term,
Lemma
C.12
(i) for the fourth term,
Lemma
D.2
(i) for the fifth term,
Lemma
C.12
(ii) for the sixth and seventh term, and Lemma
D.2
(iv) for the last term. This proves part (i).

As for part (ii), from (
D.5
) we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2})\right|\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right\}\right|+n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\}\right|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right\}\right|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\right\}\right|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\right|+2n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right\}\right|+n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\}\right|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right\|+2n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|+2n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(D.7)

The result in (
D.7
) follows from repeated use of Lemmas
C.2
,
C.12
(i),
D.1
(ii), and
D.2
(i) as well as the following results. First,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right\}\right|\leq n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\mathbf{x}_{nt}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{x}_{nt}\mathbf{x}_{nt}^{\prime}]\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

by Lemma
C.12
(vii). Second,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

by Lemma
C.8
(i). Third,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

by Lemma
D.2
(iii). Fourth,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

by Lemmas
C.12
(iii) and
D.1
(ii). And, last

\displaystyle n^{-1}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

by Lemmas
C.12
(iii) and
D.2
(ii). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.5
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

\min(n,\sqrt{T})\,\sqrt{n}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

Start with

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}

\displaystyle\,-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\leq 2n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,2n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|n^{-1/2}\|\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|.

(D.8)

Consider each term on the rhs of (
D.8
). The first term is

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(D.9)

by Lemma
D.1
(ii), and also Assumption
2
(a) for which
\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\leq C_{\xi}
, and Lemma
C.2
for which
n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|=O(1)
. The third term is

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}

\displaystyle-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|n^{-1/2}\|\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\left\{\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\|+\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\right\}n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(D.10)

by Lemmas
C.2
and
D.1
(ii), Assumption
2
(a), and since, by Lemma
D.4
(i), for all
j=1,\ldots,n
,

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=\left\{\nu^{(n)}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})\right\}^{-1}=\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}\right\}^{-1}=\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}{\sigma}_{i}^{2}+\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}{\sigma}_{i}^{2}+\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-{\sigma}_{i}^{2})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{C_{\xi}^{-1}-|\widehat{\sigma}_{j}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{j}^{2}|\right\}^{-1}\leq C_{\xi}+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(D.11)

By the same arguments, the fourth term is

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}

\displaystyle-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|^{2}\left\{\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\|+\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\right\}=o_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(D.12)

Finally, the second term on the rhs of (
D.8
) is

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)})^{-1}-(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left(\sum_{k,h=1}^{r}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda_{ih}\lambda_{ik}\{(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)})^{-1}-(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\}\right]^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)})^{-1}-(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)})^{-1}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\{\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}C_{\xi}n^{-1}\left|\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}\right\}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,rM_{\lambda}C_{\xi}^{2}n^{-1}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(D.13)

by Lemma
D.4
(ii), (
D.11
), and Assumptions
1
(a) and
2
(a). By substituting (
D.9
), (
D.10
), (
D.12
), and (
D.13
) into (
D.8
), we prove part (i).

For part (ii), we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|^{2}=n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)})^{-1}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}+\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\sigma_{i}^{2}\{(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2})/\sigma_{i}^{2}+1\}^{-1}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2})/\sigma_{i}^{2}+1\right\}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\left\{1-\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2})/\sigma_{i}^{2}+o\left(\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2})/\sigma_{i}^{2}\right)\right\}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}(\sigma_{i}^{2})^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right\|^{2}+\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2})/\sigma_{i}^{2}\right|\right\}^{2}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right\|^{2}+\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\right\}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}\right|\right\}^{2}L_{\xi}^{2}\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|^{2}\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-2},T^{-1})),

by Lemmas
C.2
,
D.1
(ii),
D.4
(ii), and Assumptions
2
(a) and
2
(f). This proves part (ii).

For part (iii), by part (ii) and
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
which is Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}|\nu^{(r)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})-\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}){\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})|\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(D.14)

Moreover (note that
x-y\geq-|x-y|
for any
x,y\in\mathbb{R}
),

\displaystyle\det(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})=

\displaystyle\,\prod_{j=1}^{r}\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\geq\left\{\nu^{(r)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\right\}^{r}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})-|\nu^{(r)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})-\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}){\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})|\right\}^{r},

thus, by Lemma
C.3
(iv), which implies
\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})>0
,
and (
D.14
), it follows that, with probability tending to one as
n,T\to\infty
, we have
\det(n^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})>0
, or, equivalently
n^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}
is positive definite, i.e.,

n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
. This proves part (iii).

For part (iv), we have

\displaystyle n\|

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

because of parts (i) and (iii) and Lemma
C.3
(iii).

Part (v) follows directly from parts (ii) and (iv). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.6
.

For all
T\in\mathbb{N}
,

(i)

\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
,
\mathbf{P}_{t|t}
, and
\mathbf{P}_{t|T}
are deterministic
r\times r
matrices, for all
t=1,\ldots,T
;

(ii)

\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\|
, for all
t=1,\ldots,T-1
;

(iii)

\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t-1}
,
\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t}
, and
\mathbf{P}_{0,t|T}
are deterministic
r\times r
matrices, for all
t=1,\ldots,T
;

(iv)

\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t+1|t}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t-1}\|
, for all
t=1,\ldots,T-1
.

Proof.
Since
\mathbf{P}_{0|0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
is deterministic, then, for all
t=1,\ldots,T
,
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
,
\mathbf{P}_{t|t}
, and
\mathbf{P}_{t|T}
do not depend on the actual observations because of (
A.2
) and (
A.4
). This proves part (i).

As for part (ii), since
\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}
is based on less information than
\mathbf{F}_{t+1|t}
and since
{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}
and
\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}
are deterministic, then
({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t+1|t})
is a positive definite matrix for all
t=1,\ldots,T-1
(see, e.g.,
Harvey 1990
, Chapter 3.3, p. 123
). As consequence,
\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\|
for all
t=1,\ldots,T-1
(see, e.g.,
Marshall
et al. 2011
, Proposition L1, p. 360
).

The proof of parts (iii) and (iv) is identical to parts (i) and (ii), respectively, since also in this case
\mathbf{P}_{0,0|0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
. This completes the proof.

\Box

Lemma D.7
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, for all
T\in\mathbb{N}
,

(i)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\|\leq M_{P}
for some finite positive real
M_{P}
;

(ii)

\min_{t=1,\ldots,T}\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})\geq\underline{M}_{P}
for some finite positive real
\underline{M}_{P}
.

(iii)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t-1}\|\leq M_{P}
for some finite positive real
M_{P}
;

(iv)

\min_{t=1,\ldots,T}\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t-1})\geq\underline{M}_{P}
for some finite positive real
\underline{M}_{P}
.

Proof.
Given that
\mathbf{P}_{0|0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
is obviously positive definite, by (
A.2
) and Weyl’s inequality
(
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, Theorem 1)
it follows that

\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{P}_{1|0})\geq\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{A}^{\prime})+\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{v})\geq M_{v}^{-1},

(D.15)

for some finite positive real
M_{v}^{-1}
, since
\bm{\Gamma}^{v}
has full rank by Assumption
1
(e) and
\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{A}^{\prime})
is real and such that
\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{A}^{\prime})\geq(\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{A}))^{2}\geq 0
. From
\mathbf{P}_{0|0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
and (
A.2
) it follows also that

\|\mathbf{P}_{1|0}\|\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{P}_{0|0}\|+\|\bm{\Gamma}^{v}\|^{2}\leq M_{A}^{2}+M_{v}^{2}=M_{P},\;\text{ say.}

(D.16)

By Lemma
D.6
(ii) and (
D.16
), we have

\max_{t=2,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{1|0}\|\leq M_{P},

since
M_{P}
is independent of
t
and
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
is deterministic because of Lemma
D.6
(i). This proves part (i).

For part (ii), by
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
and (
A.2
)

\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})\geq\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{A}^{\prime})\nu^{(r)}(\mathbf{P}_{t|t})+\nu^{(r)}(\bm{\Gamma}^{v})\geq M_{v}^{-1},

(D.17)

by the same arguments leading to (
D.15
) and since
\nu_{\min}(\mathbf{P}_{t|t})\geq 0
because
\mathbf{P}_{t|t}
is at least positive semidefinite by construction. By letting
\underline{M}_{P}=M_{v}^{-1}
, we prove part (ii).
Parts (iii) and (iv) are proved exactly as parts (i) and (ii), respectively, since also in this case
\mathbf{P}_{0,0|0}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
.
This completes the proof.

\Box

Lemma D.8
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|t-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|(\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
For part (i),

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}^{(0)}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

by Lemma
D.7
(i) and since the second term on the rhs depends only on the estimation error of
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}
,
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(0)}
,
n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}
,

n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}

and
n^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}
, which are all bounded by Lemmas
D.1
(ii),
D.3
,
D.5
(ii), and
D.5
(iv). This proves part (i).

Part (ii) is proved in the same way as part (i) but using Lemma
D.7
(ii). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.9
.

For
m<n
with
m
independent of
n
and given

(a)

an
n\times n
matrix
\bm{A}
symmetric and positive definite with
\|\bm{A}\|\leq M_{A}
;

(a)

an
m\times m
matrix
\bm{B}
symmetric and positive definite with
\|\bm{B}\|\leq M_{B}
;

(c)

an
n\times m
matrix
\bm{U}
such that
\|n^{-1}\bm{U}^{\prime}\bm{U}\|\leq M_{U}
and
\text{rk}(\bm{U})=m
;

(d)

an
m\times n
matrix
\bm{V}
such that
\|n^{-1}\bm{V}\bm{V}^{\prime}\|\leq M_{V}
and
\text{rk}(\bm{V})=m
;

where,
M_{A}
,
M_{B}
,
M_{U}
, and
M_{V}
are finite positive reals independent of
n
and
m
, then the following holds

(i)

(\bm{A}+\bm{U}\bm{B}\bm{V})^{-1}=\bm{A}^{-1}-\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{U}\bm{B}(\mathbf{I}_{m}+\bm{V}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{U}\bm{B})^{-1}\bm{V}\bm{A}^{-1}
;

(ii)

(\bm{A}+\bm{U}\bm{B}\bm{V})^{-1}=\bm{A}^{-1}-\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{U}\bm{B}\bm{V}(\mathbf{I}_{m}+\bm{V}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{U}\bm{B}\bm{V})^{-1}\bm{A}^{-1}
.

Proof.
Both results are proved by
Henderson and
Searle 1981
.
\Box

Lemma D.10
.

For any
r\times r
symmetric and positive definite matrix
\bm{P}
with
\|\bm{P}\|\leq M_{P}
for some finite positive real
M_{P}
, under Assumptions
1
and
2

\displaystyle\bm{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}=\bm{P}(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+\bm{P})^{-1}.

Proof.
In Lemma
D.9
(i) set
\bm{A}={\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}
,
\bm{B}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
,
\bm{U}={\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\bm{P}
, and
\bm{V}={\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}
. Then, by noticing that the assumptions of Lemma
D.9
are satisfied because of Assumptions
1
(a) and
2
(a), it follows that:

(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}=({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P}(\mathbf{I}_{r}+\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bm{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}

\displaystyle=\bm{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\{({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P}(\mathbf{I}_{r}+\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}

\displaystyle=\bm{P}\{\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P}(\mathbf{I}_{r}+\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P})\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}.

(D.18)

Now, in Lemma
D.9
(ii) set
\bm{A}=(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}
,
\bm{B}=\bm{P}
,
\bm{U}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, and
\bm{V}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, and notice that the assumptions therein are satisfied because of Lemmas
C.3
(iii) and
C.3
(v).
Then, for the last line of (
D.18
) we have

\displaystyle\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P}(\mathbf{I}_{r}+\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{P})\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}=((\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}+\bm{P})^{-1}.

(D.19)

By substituting (
D.19
) into (
D.18
) we complete the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.11
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
From (
A.4
) by using Lemma
D.10
, but with
\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)}
in place of
\bm{\Lambda}_{n}
and
\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}
,
it holds that:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(0)}=

\displaystyle\,{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}+\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}+\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\right\}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}.

(D.20)

Then, by setting in Lemma
C.4

\bm{K}={\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}
and
\bm{H}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}
, for the last line of (
D.20
) we have

\displaystyle((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}=

\displaystyle\,(\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}

(D.21)

\displaystyle-((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}(\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}.

By substituting (
D.21
) into (
D.20
) we get

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(0)}=

\displaystyle\,{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}.

(D.22)

Finally, by using again (
D.21
) into (
D.22
)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(0)}=

\displaystyle\,{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\left\{({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}-((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle\cdot(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle\cdot(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}

\displaystyle-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}

\displaystyle\cdot({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}.

(D.23)

Notice that we could use Lemmas
C.4
and
D.10
to derive (
D.23
) since all inverses used
are well defined because of Lemmas
D.5
(iii),
D.8
(i), and
D.8
(ii) and (
D.11
) in the proof of Lemma
D.4
.

Therefore, from (
D.23
)

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot\|((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n^{-1}),

because of Lemmas
D.5
(iii),
D.8
(i), and
D.8
(ii), and since, by
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
which is Weyl’s inequality,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\|=\left\{\nu^{(r)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\nu^{(r)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1})+\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left[\nu^{(1)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\right]^{-1}+\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left[\nu^{(1)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})\right]^{-1}+1\right\}^{-1}\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{1-\left[\nu^{(1)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})\right]^{-1}\right\}\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})\right\}^{-1}+O_{p}(n^{-2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(1),

(D.24)

again by Lemmas
D.5
(iii),
D.8
(i), and
D.8
(ii). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.12
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|T}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
From (
A.7
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|(\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{(0)})^{-1}\|^{2}\{\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|T}^{(0)}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{(0)}\|\}.

(D.25)

Start with
t=T-1
, then from (
D.25
),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}^{(0)}\|\leq
