\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|(\mathbf{P}_{T|T-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\|^{2}\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T}^{(0)}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T-1}^{(0)}\|\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(D.26)

by Lemmas
D.8
(i),
D.8
(ii), and
D.11
, and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
D.3
(i). From (
D.26
)
it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-1|T}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-1|T-1}\|+\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-1|T-1}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1})+O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(D.27)

Thus, at
t=T-2
, from (
D.25
) and (
D.27
),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}^{(0)}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|(\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{(0)})^{-1}\|^{2}\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}^{(0)}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{(0)}\|\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(D.28)

From (
D.28
) it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-2|T}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-2|T-2}\|+\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-2|T}-\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{T-2|T-2}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1})+O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(D.29)

Since all the bounds in (
D.26
)-(
D.29
) are the same for all
t
, from Lemma
D.11
and (
D.25
) we have

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|T}\|\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|t}\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\mathbf{P}^{(0)}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n^{-1}).

This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.13
.

For
m<n
, and given symmetric positive definite matrices
\bm{A}
of dimension
m\times m
and
\bm{B}
of dimension
n\times n
, and for
\bm{C}
of dimension
n\times m
with
\text{rk}(\bm{C})=m
, the following holds

\bm{A}\bm{C}^{\prime}(\bm{C}\bm{A}\bm{C}^{\prime}+\bm{B})^{-1}=(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}.

(D.30)

Proof.

Recall the Woodbury forumla

\displaystyle(\bm{C}\bm{A}\bm{C}^{\prime}+\bm{B})^{-1}=\bm{B}^{-1}-\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}.

(D.31)

Denote
\bm{D}=(\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C})^{-1}
then
from (
D.31
) the lhs of (
D.30
) is equivalent to

\displaystyle\bm{A}\bm{C}^{\prime}\left[\bm{B}^{-1}-\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}\bm{D}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\right]=\bm{A}\left[\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}-\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}\bm{D}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\right]=\bm{A}\left[\bm{I}-\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}\bm{D}\right]\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}.

Then, (
D.30
) becomes

\bm{A}\left[\bm{I}-\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}\bm{D}\right]\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}=\bm{D}\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1},

or equivalently multiplying both sides on the right by
\bm{B}\bm{C}(\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{C})^{-1}

\bm{A}\left[\bm{I}-\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}\bm{D}\right]=\bm{D}.

(D.32)

Now multiplying (
D.32
) on the left by
\bm{A}^{-1}
and on the right by
\bm{D}^{-1}

\left[\bm{D}^{-1}-\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}\right]=\bm{A}^{-1},

which is equivalent to

\bm{A}^{-1}+\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}-\bm{C}^{\prime}\bm{B}^{-1}\bm{C}-\bm{A}^{-1}=\bm{0}_{m\times m},

which is always true.
\Box

Lemma D.14
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
, for all
s=0,\ldots,T
,

\|\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|s}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t\leq s
.

Proof.

Let

\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}^{(0)}}[\bm{F}_{s}\bm{F}_{s}^{\prime}]
, which is
rs\times rs
having the
r\times r
generic
(t_{1},t_{2})
block denoted by
[\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}]_{t_{1},t_{2}}
and such that
[\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}]_{t_{2},t_{1}}=[\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}]_{t_{1},t_{2}}^{\prime}
and

\text{vec}\left([\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}]_{t_{1},t_{2}}\right)=(\mathbf{I}_{r}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)})^{|t_{1}-t_{2}|}(\mathbf{I}_{r^{2}}-\{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\})^{-1}\text{vec}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(0)}),\hskip 9.24994ptt_{1},t_{2}=1,\ldots,s.

Notice that although
\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}
depends on
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}
and
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(0)}
, for simplicity of notation, hereafter, we omit such dependence.
Let also
{\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s}=\mathbb{E}[\bm{F}_{s}\bm{F}_{s}^{\prime}]
, clearly
{\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s}
is positive definite, since by Assumptions
1
(b) and
1
(d),

\|[{\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s}]_{t_{1},t_{2}}\|\leq\|\bm{\Gamma}^{F}\|
for all
t_{1}\neq t_{2}
. Moreover, recall that
{\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s}
is a block-Toeplitz matrix and define the corresponding circulant matrix as
{\bm{\Phi}}^{F}_{s}
, then (see, e.g.,
Gray 2006
, Lemma 4.3 and Section 3.1
).

s^{-1}|\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s})-\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Psi}}^{F}_{s})|=O(s^{-1/2})
,
and

\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Psi}}^{F}_{s})=O(s)
.
Thus,

\|{\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s}\|=\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s})\leq\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Psi}}^{F}_{s})+|\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s})-\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Psi}}^{F}_{s})|=O(s)+O(\sqrt{s}),

which implies

s\|({\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s})^{-1}\|=O(1).

(D.33)

Now the
r\times r
generic
(t_{1},t_{2})
block of
({\bm{\Omega}}^{F})_{s}^{-1}
is an analytic function of
\mathbf{A}
and
\bm{\Gamma}^{v}
, which, in the case
r=1
, is given by (see
Akaike 1973
for the case
r>1
)

[({\bm{\Omega}}^{F}_{s})^{-1}]_{t_{1},t_{2}}=\mathbb{E}[v_{t}^{2}](1-A^{2})^{-2}\cdot\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccccc}1&\text{if}&t_{1}=t_{2}=1&\text{and}&t_{1}=t_{2}=s,\\
1+A^{2}&\text{if}&t_{1}=t_{2}&\text{and}&1<t_{1},t_{2}<s,\\
-A&\text{if}&|t_{1}-t_{2}|=1,\\
0&\text{otherwise}.\end{array}\right.

Then, because of Lemma
D.3
and (
D.33
), we have that, as
n,s\to\infty
,
s(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s})^{-1}
is positive definite with probability tending to one, i.e.,

\displaystyle s\|(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1).

(D.34)

Let now

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{K}}_{ns}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime})\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s}(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime})+(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime})(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})+(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s})^{-1}\right\}^{-1}\left\{(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime})(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle=\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}^{F(0)}_{s})^{-1}\right\}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\right\},

(D.35)

which is
rs\times ns
and where in the second line we used Lemma
D.13
. Notice that all the inverses in (
D.35
) are well defined by (
D.34
),
Lemma
D.5
(iii), and (
D.11
) in the proof of Lemma
D.4
.

Then, by definition of linear projection, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|s}^{(0)}=

\displaystyle\,\mathrm{Proj}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{(0)}}[{\mathbf{F}}_{t}|\bm{X}_{ns}]=(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{ns}^{(0)}\}\bm{X}_{ns}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\right\}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\right\}\bm{X}_{ns}

(D.36)

\displaystyle+(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\left[\{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{ns}^{(0)}\}^{-1}-\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\right\}\right]\bm{X}_{ns},

where
\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}
is the
t
th row of
\mathbf{I}_{s}
and
\bm{X}_{ns}=(\mathbf{x}_{n1}^{\prime}\cdots\mathbf{x}_{ns}^{\prime})^{\prime}
is an
ns
-dimensional vector.

Then,
by
(
C.6
) in the proof of Lemma
C.5
,
we have

\displaystyle n^{3/2}s

\displaystyle\left\|\{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{ns}^{(0)}\}^{-1}-\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1})\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{2}s\left\|\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{s}^{F(0)})^{-1}\right\}-\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot n^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1})\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n^{2}s\left\|\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{s}^{F(0)})^{-1}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{s}^{F(0)})^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\cdot n^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1})\right\|=O_{p}(1).

(D.37)

Indeed, for the first term on the rhs of (
D.37
), by Lemmas
C.3
(i),
C.3
(v), and
D.5
(i), and by (
D.34
), we have

\displaystyle n^{2}s

\displaystyle\left\|\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{s}^{F(0)})^{-1}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{s}^{F(0)})^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n\left\|\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}+(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{s}^{F(0)})^{-1}\right\}\right\|\,s\|(\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{s}^{F(0)})^{-1}\|\,n\|\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(1).

(D.38)

Alternatively to bound the first term on the rhs of (
D.38
) we can use directly Lemma
D.5
(iii) and
D.34
.

And, for the second term on the rhs of (
D.37
), by Lemmas
C.3
(vii) and
D.5
(ii), we have

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1})\|=O_{p}(1).

(D.39)

Now, let

\widehat{\bm{\Pi}}_{s}^{(0)}=\left[\{\widehat{\bm{K}}_{ns}^{(0)}\}^{-1}-\left\{\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}(\mathbf{I}_{s}\otimes\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\right\}\right],

and similarly define
\bm{\Pi}_{s}
when using the true parameters. By Lemmas
D.1
(ii),
D.3
,
D.5
(ii), and
D.5
(iv), and (
D.37
), we have

\displaystyle n^{3/2}s\|\widehat{\bm{\Pi}}_{s}^{(0)}-\bm{\Pi}_{s}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(D.40)

Moreover,
\bm{\Pi}_{s}
is
rs\times ns
such that

\displaystyle(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\bm{\Pi}_{s}\bm{X}_{ns}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{\tau=1}^{s}\bm{\pi}_{1\tau}\mathbf{x}_{n\tau}\\
\vdots\\
\sum_{\tau=1}^{s}\bm{\pi}_{s\tau}\mathbf{x}_{n\tau}\end{array}\right)=\left(\sum_{\tau=1}^{s}\bm{\pi}_{1\tau}\mathbf{x}_{n\tau}\cdots\sum_{\tau=1}^{s}\bm{\pi}_{s\tau}\mathbf{x}_{n\tau}\right)\bm{\iota}_{t,s}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tau=1}^{s}\bm{\pi}_{t\tau}\mathbf{x}_{n\tau},

(D.44)

with
\bm{\pi}_{t\tau}
,
t,\tau=1,\ldots,n
being
r\times n
sub-block of
\bm{\Pi}_{s}
and since
\text{vec}(\bm{A}\bm{B}\bm{C})=(\bm{C}^{\prime}\otimes\bm{A})\text{vec}(\bm{B})
. Furthermore, since clearly from (
D.33
)

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,s}\sqrt{s}\|(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})[({\bm{\Omega}}_{s})^{-1}]\|=O(1),

\displaystyle\max_{\tau=1,\ldots,s}\sqrt{s}\|[({\bm{\Omega}}_{s})^{-1}](\bm{\iota}_{\tau,s}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\|=O(1),

(D.45)

using the same reasoning as in (
D.38
) and (
D.39
), but when considering the true parameters, we also have that
\max_{t,\tau=1,\ldots,s}\|\bm{\pi}_{t\tau}\|=O(n^{-3/2}s^{-1/2})
. Thus, from (
D.44
) we have

\displaystyle\|(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\bm{\Pi}_{s}\bm{X}_{ns}\|\leq\max_{t,\tau=1,\ldots,s}\|\bm{\pi}_{t\tau}\|\,\left\|\sum_{\tau=1}^{s}\mathbf{x}_{n\tau}\right\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(D.46)

since, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
C.1
(iii)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{\tau=1}^{s}\mathbf{x}_{n\tau}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\tau_{1}=1}^{s}\sum_{\tau_{2}=1}^{s}\mathbb{E}[x_{i\tau_{1}}x_{i\tau_{2}}]=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\tau_{1}=1}^{s}\sum_{\tau_{2}=1}^{s}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{F}_{\tau_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{\tau_{2}}]\bm{\lambda}_{i}+\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i\tau_{1}}\xi_{i\tau_{2}}]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\tau_{1}=1}^{s}\sum_{\tau_{2}=1}^{s}\mathbb{E}[x_{i\tau_{1}}x_{i\tau_{2}}]=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\tau_{1}=1}^{s}\sum_{\tau_{2}=1}^{s}|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}^{|\tau_{1}-\tau_{2}|}\bm{\lambda}_{i}|+|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i\tau_{1}}\xi_{i\tau_{2}}]|

\displaystyle\leq nM_{\lambda}^{2}\left\{\sum_{\tau_{1}=1}^{s}\sum_{\tau_{2}=1}^{s}\|\mathbf{A}^{|\tau_{1}-\tau_{2}|}\|+\sum_{\tau_{1}=1}^{s}\sum_{\tau_{2}=1}^{s}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{i\tau_{1}}\xi_{i\tau_{2}}]|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq nsM_{\lambda}^{2}\sum_{k=-(s-1)}^{s-1}\left(1-s^{-1}|k|\right)M_{A}^{k}+nsM_{\lambda}^{2}M_{3}

\displaystyle\leq nsM_{\lambda}^{2}2\sum_{k=0}^{s-1}M_{A}^{k}+nsM_{\lambda}^{2}M_{3}

\displaystyle\leq nsM_{\lambda}^{2}2(1-M_{A})^{-1}+nsM_{\lambda}^{2}M_{3}.

Thus, from (
D.36
), (
D.37
), (
D.40
), and (
D.46
), and since
\text{vec}(\bm{A}\bm{B}\bm{C})=(\bm{C}^{\prime}\otimes\bm{A})\text{vec}(\bm{B})
and
\|\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r}\|=1
,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|s}^{(0)}\|\leq\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+\left\|(\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\bm{\Pi}_{s}\bm{X}_{ns}\right\|+\|\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r}\|\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Pi}}_{s}^{(0)}-\bm{\Pi}_{s}\|\,\|\bm{X}_{ns}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\|+O_{p}(n^{-1})+o_{p}(n^{-1}s^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(1),

(D.47)

where we used also Lemmas

C.3
(v),
C.3
(vii),
C.10
,
D.5
(i), and
D.5
(ii), and the facts that
\|\bm{X}_{ns}\|=O(\sqrt{ns})
by the same reasoning as in Lemma
C.10
, and
\|\bm{\iota}_{t,s}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r}\|=1
.
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.15
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

n\|\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|T}-\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
.

Proof.
From (
A.6
) and (
A.1
)

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\,\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t+1|t}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,\{\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|T}^{(0)}\|+\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|t}^{(0)}\|\}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\,\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t+1|t}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,\{\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|T}^{(0)}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\,\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|\}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(D.48)

by Lemmas

D.8
(ii),

D.11
, and

D.14
(when
s=T
and
s=t
), and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
D.3
(i). This completes the proof.

Lemma D.16
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

n\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}(0)}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
,
where
{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}(0)}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}.

Proof.
From (
A.3
) and (
A.1
), by Lemma
D.13

\displaystyle{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{(0)}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}+\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+(\widehat{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\right\}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|t-1}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}

\displaystyle+\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+(\widehat{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\right\}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|t-1}^{(0)}.

(D.49)

Notice that the inverses in (
D.49
) are all well defined by Lemmas
D.5
(iii) and
D.8
(ii) and (
D.11
) in the proof of Lemma
D.4
.

Now, by Lemma
C.5

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(D.50)

Furthermore, by Lemmas
C.6
(iii) and
D.5
(iv)

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|=O(n^{-2}),

(D.51)

and by Lemmas
C.3
(vii) and
D.5
(ii),

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(\sqrt{n}).

(D.52)

Indeed, we can apply Lemmas
C.5
and
C.6
(iii), since
\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
by Lemma
D.8
(ii),

\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)

by (
D.11
) in the proof of Lemma
D.4
, and, by Lemmas
C.2
and
D.1
(ii) we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,2n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\|+n^{-1}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,2n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|+n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},{T}^{-1/2})).

which, by Weyl’s inequality
(
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, Theorem 1)
, implies

\displaystyle n^{-1}|\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})-\nu^{(j)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})|\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},{T}^{-1/2})),

and, therefore, for
j=1,\ldots,r,
,

\underline{C}_{j}\leq\text{p-}\lim\!\!\!\!\!\!\inf_{n,T\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\leq\text{p-}\lim\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{n,T\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\leq\overline{C}_{j}.

By using (
D.50
), (
D.51
), (
D.52
) into (
D.49
):

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{(0)}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+\|\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}+(\widehat{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{(0)})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\,\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|t-1}^{(0)}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(D.53)

by Lemmas
C.10
and
D.14
(when
s=t-1
), and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
D.3
(i). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.17
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

\min(\sqrt{n},\sqrt{T})\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
.

Proof.
From Lemmas
D.15
and
D.16

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(0)}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(0)}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(D.54)

where
{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}(0)}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}.

Now,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(0)}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,A+B+C+D,\;\text{ say.}

(D.55)

Let us consider each term in (
D.55
). First, consider term
A
and let

\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}=(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}
. Then, from
Barigozzi 2023

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})).

(D.56)

Therefore, from (
D.56
)

\displaystyle A\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\{A.1+A.2\}\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|,\;\text{ say.}

(D.57)

Then,

\displaystyle A.1\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(D.58)

by Lemmas
C.3
(iii) and
C.8
(iv),
and also, recalling that
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
by Assumption
6
(b), by Lemma
C.12
(i) and Weyl’s inequality
(
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, Theorem 1)
we have
|\nu^{(r)}(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})-1|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2})
which implies

\|(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1).

Moreover,
A.2=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}))
,
because of (
D.56
) and Lemmas
C.2
,
C.3
(iii), and Assumption
2
(a) which implies
\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\leq C_{\xi}
. This, jointly with (
D.57
) and (
D.58
) implies that

\displaystyle A=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})),

(D.59)

since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
because
\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=1
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, by Assumption
6
(b).

Second, by Lemmas
D.1
(ii) and
D.5
(v),

\displaystyle B=

\displaystyle\,\|n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}n^{-1/2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-2},T^{-1})),

(D.60)

and since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
because
\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=1
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, by Assumption
6
(b).
Third,

\displaystyle C\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

(D.61)

by Lemmas
C.3
(iii) and
C.7
(i).
Fourth, and last,

\displaystyle D\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(0)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,D.1+D.2+D.3,\;\text{say.}

Then,
D.1=O_{p}(\max(n^{-3/2},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}))
, by Lemmas
C.7
(i) and
D.5
(iv),
while we have
D.2=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}))
, because of Lemmas
C.3
(iii) and
D.5
(ii) and since
\|\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(\sqrt{n})
because
\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{2}]=\sigma_{i}^{2}\leq C_{\xi}
by Assumption
2
(a). Last
D.3=O_{p}(n^{-2},T^{-1})
by Lemmas
D.5
(ii) and
D.5
(iv) and since
\|\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(\sqrt{n})
.
Therefore,

D=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(D.62)

By substituting (
D.59
), (
D.60
), (
D.61
), and (
D.62
), into (
D.55
) and then into (
D.54
), we complete the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.18
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
, for
s=t
and
s=T
:

(i)

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|s}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(i)

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|s}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
;

(ii)

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{(0)}_{t|s}\xi_{it}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
.

Proof.

First notice that, for all
k=t-T,\ldots,t-1
,

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{n,t-k}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(1),

(D.63)

by Lemma
C.12
. The proof of part (i) follows by iterating either forward or backwards since both

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(0)}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|
and
\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(0)}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|

are functions of (
D.63
), because of Lemmas
D.15
and
D.16
.

Part (ii) follows from part (i) and Assumption
1
(a). Part (iii) follows by substituting
\mathbf{F}_{t}
with
\xi_{it}
in (
E.95
) and then by applying Lemma
C.12
(ii). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma D.19
.

Let
{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}=(\text{vec}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})^{\prime}\;{\sigma}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}_{1}\cdots\widehat{\sigma}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}_{n})^{\prime}
be the vector of OLS estimators of the entries of
{\bm{\phi}}_{n}
obtained when
\mathbf{F}_{t}
is known, that is whose entries maximize
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})
. Let also
{\bm{\theta}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}=(\text{vec}({\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})^{\prime}\;\text{vech}(\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}))^{\prime}
be the vector of OLS estimators of the entries of
{\bm{\theta}}
obtained when
\mathbf{F}_{t}
is known, that is whose entries maximize
\ell(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\theta}})
. Then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

\sqrt{T}\,\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
;

(ii)

\sqrt{T}\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\sqrt{T}|\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\sqrt{T}\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

\sqrt{T}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
For part (i),

\displaystyle\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\leq\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

(D.64)

where for the numerator we used Lemma

C.12
(ii) while for the denominator we used Lemma
C.13
.

Part (ii) is proved in the same way but using Lemma
C.12
(iii) instead of Lemma
C.12
(ii).

For part (iii), first notice that

\displaystyle{\sigma}_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\xi}_{it}^{\text{\tiny OLS}2}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{x_{it}^{2}+\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}-2x_{it}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\}

\displaystyle+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{2\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})+(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\ \mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})-2x_{it}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{2\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})+(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\ \mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})

\displaystyle-2(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-2(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\xi}_{it}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}^{2}+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\ \mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}),

(D.65)

since by construction
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\xi}_{it}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
. Then,

\displaystyle|\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}\right|+\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{2}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|^{2}\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{2}]\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

because of part (i) and Lemma
C.12
(iv), and also by
Lemma
C.12
(i) combined with Assumption
6
(b).

For part (iv) consistency follows from the fact that
\{\mathbf{v}_{t}\}
is an independent process by Assumption
1
(f) and thus it is a martingale difference process
(
Hamilton 1994
, Proposition 11, pp. 298-299)
. Part (v) follows from part (iv) and
(
Hamilton 1994
, Proposition 11.2, pp. 301)
, since by Assumption
1
(g), the fourth order cumulants of
\{\mathbf{v}_{t}\}
are all finite. This completes the proof.
\Box

Appendix E
Lemmas necessary for proving Proposition
2

Lemma E.1
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
5

(i)

For all
t\in\mathbb{Z}
, all
j=1,\ldots,r
, and all
s>0
,
\mathrm{P}(|F_{jt}|\geq s)\leq\exp\{-K_{F}s^{\delta_{v}}\}
, for some finite positive real
K_{F}
independent of
t
and
j
;

(ii)

for all
i\in\mathbb{N}
and all
T\in\mathbb{N}
,

\mathrm{P}\left(\left\|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|\geq s\right)\leq r\exp\{-\kappa_{3}s^{2}\}+rT\exp\{-\kappa_{4}(s\sqrt{T})^{\beta}\},

for some finite positive reals
\kappa_{3}
,
\kappa_{4}
, and
\beta
independent of
i
and
T
and such that
\frac{1}{\beta}=\frac{1}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{\delta}>1
and
\gamma=\min(\gamma_{F},\gamma_{\xi})
and
\delta\in(0,\frac{\delta_{v}\delta_{\xi}}{\delta_{v}+\delta_{\xi}})
.

Proof.

For all
j=1,\ldots,r
,

\left|F_{jt}\right|\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{r}\left|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}[\mathbf{A}^{k}]_{j\ell}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|\leq r\max_{\ell=1,\ldots,r}\left|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}[\mathbf{A}^{k}]_{j\ell}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|.

Now, since because of Assumption
1
(d) the coefficients
[\mathbf{A}^{k}]_{j\ell}
are summable over
k
, for any
\epsilon>0
and
\eta>0
there exists a positive integer
\bar{K}=\bar{K}(\epsilon,\eta)
independent of
j
,
\ell
, and
t
such that

\mathrm{P}\left(\left|\sum_{k=\bar{K}+1}^{\infty}[\mathbf{A}^{k}]_{j\ell}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|\geq\eta\right)\leq\epsilon,

thus we can always find
\bar{K}
such that we can write

\displaystyle\left|F_{jt}\right|

\displaystyle\leq r\max_{\ell=1,\ldots,r}\left|\sum_{k=0}^{\bar{K}}[\mathbf{A}^{k}]_{j\ell}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|+\left|\sum_{k=\bar{K}+1}^{\infty}[\mathbf{A}^{k}]_{j\ell}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|

\displaystyle\leq r\bar{K}\max_{\ell=1,\ldots,r}\max_{k=0,\ldots,\bar{K}}|[\mathbf{A}^{k}]_{j\ell}|\left|\sum_{k=0}^{\bar{K}}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\leq r\bar{K}C_{A}\left|\sum_{k=0}^{\bar{K}}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|+o_{p}(1),

(E.1)

for some positive real
C_{A}
independent of
j
, where we used again Assumption
1
(d) to bound the coefficients.

Then, from Assumption
5
(a), (
E
), and
Bakhshizadeh et al. 2023
, since
\{\mathbf{v}_{t}\}
is an independent process, and by Assumption
1
and the union bound, for all
j=1,\ldots r
and all
s>0
it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(|F_{jt}|\geq s\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{P}\left(r\bar{K}C_{A}\left|\sum_{k=0}^{\bar{K}}v_{\ell,t-k}\right|\geq s\right)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left\{-C^{\prime}\left(\frac{s}{r\bar{K}C_{A}}\right)^{2}\right\}+\bar{K}\exp\left\{-C^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s}{r\bar{K}C_{A}}\right)^{\delta_{v}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{K}\exp\left\{-C^{\prime\prime\prime}s^{\delta_{v}}\right\}\leq\exp\left\{-K_{F}s^{\delta_{v}}\right\},

(E.2)

for some finite positive reals
C^{\prime}
,
C^{\prime\prime}
,
C^{\prime\prime\prime}
, and
K_{F}
independent of
t
and
j
and where from the second line we omitted the second term which is negligible. This proves part (i).

For all
i=1,\ldots,n
and all
n\in\mathbb{N}
, consider Assumption
5
(b) when
n=1
,
\bm{\lambda}_{i}=1
, and
\sigma_{i}^{2}=1
, then, because of Assumption
3
and (
E.2
), by
Fan
et al. 2011
, for all
j=1,\ldots r
and all
s>0
it holds that:

\mathrm{P}\left(|F_{jt}\xi_{it}|\geq s\right)\leq\exp\left\{-\kappa_{F\xi}s^{\delta}\right\},

(E.3)

for some finite positive reals
\kappa_{F\xi}
and
\delta\in\left(0,\frac{\delta_{v}\delta_{\xi}}{\delta_{v}+\delta_{\xi}}\right)
independent of
i
,
j
, and
t
. Moreover, because of Assumptions
1
(d),
1
(e),
1
(f), and
1
(h), by
Pham and Tran 1985
,
\{\mathbf{F}_{t}\}
is a strong mixing process with mixing coefficients

\alpha_{F}(T)\leq\exp\left\{-c_{F}T^{\gamma_{F}}\right\},

(E.4)

for all
T\in\mathbb{N}
and some finite positive reals
c_{F}
and
\gamma_{F}
independent of
T
. Then, because of (
E.4
) and Assumption
2
(c), by
Bradley 2005
, we have that, for all
i\in\mathbb{N}
and all
j=1,\ldots,r
,
\{F_{jt}\xi_{it}\}
is strong mixing with mixing coefficients:

\alpha_{F\xi}(T)\leq\alpha_{F}(T)+\alpha_{\xi}(T)\leq\exp\left\{-c_{F}T^{\gamma_{F}}\right\}+\exp\left\{-c_{\xi}T^{\gamma_{\xi}}\right\}\leq 2\exp\left\{-c_{F\xi}T^{\gamma}\right\},

(E.5)

for all
T\in\mathbb{N}
and some finite positive reals
c_{F\xi}
and
\gamma=\min(\gamma_{F},\gamma_{\xi})
independent of
T
.

Now, since for all
i\in\mathbb{N}
and all
j=1,\ldots,r
,
\{F_{jt}\xi_{it}\}
satisfies (
E.3
) and (
E.5
), we can apply the results by
Merlevède et al. 2011
or equivalently by
Bosq 2012
, which imply that, for all
s>0
,

\mathrm{P}\left(\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{jt}\xi_{it}\right|\geq s\right)\leq\exp\bigg\{-c_{1}s^{2}\bigg\}+T\exp\bigg\{-c_{2}\Big(s\sqrt{T}\Big)^{\beta}\bigg\}

(E.6)

for some finite positive reals
c_{1}
,
c_{2}
, and
\beta
independent of
i
,
j
, and
T
, where
\frac{1}{\beta}=\frac{1}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{\delta}>1
. Finally, note that

\left\|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{r}\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{jt}\xi_{it}\right|\leq r\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{jt}\xi_{it}\right|,

(E.7)

and from (
E.6
) and (
E.7
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\left\|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|\geq s\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{P}\left(r\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F_{jt}\xi_{it}\right|\geq s\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\exp\bigg\{-c_{1}\bigg(\frac{s}{r}\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}+rT\exp\bigg\{-c_{2}\bigg(\frac{s\sqrt{T}}{r}\bigg)^{\beta}\bigg\},

by setting
\kappa_{3}=\frac{c_{1}}{r^{2}}
and
\kappa_{4}=\frac{c_{2}}{r^{\beta}}
, we prove part (ii) and complete the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.2
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
5
, as
T\to\infty
,

\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|=O_{p}(1).

Proof.
We have,

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\sum_{j=1}^{r}|F_{jt}|\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}|F_{jt}|=O_{p}(\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T),

by Lemma
E.1
(i) and the union bound. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.3
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
5
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(1).

Proof.

By Assumption
5
(b), for all
s>0
, setting
\bm{\lambda}_{i}=\bm{\iota}_{r}
and
\sigma_{i}^{2}=1
therein,

\mathrm{P}\left(n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\xi}_{nt}}\|\geq s\right)\leq\exp\left\{-\mathcal{K}_{\xi}s^{2}\right\}+n\exp\left\{-K_{\xi}(s\sqrt{n})^{\delta_{\xi}}\right\}.

(E.8)

Then, by (
E.8
) and the union bound, for all
s>0
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\xi}_{nt}}\|\geq s\right)

\displaystyle\leq T\mathrm{P}\left(n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\xi}_{nt}}\|\geq s\right)

\displaystyle\leq T\exp\left\{-\mathcal{K}_{\xi}s^{2}\right\}+nT\exp\left\{-K_{\xi}(s\sqrt{n})^{\delta_{\xi}}\right\}.

(E.9)

Thus, from Lemmas
C.2
and
E.2
, and (
E.9
)

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n^{-1/2}\|{\mathbf{x}_{nt}}\|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}_{n}}\|\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\xi}_{nt}}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T)+O_{p}(\sqrt{\log T})+O_{p}(n^{-\delta_{\xi}/2}\max(\log^{1/\delta_{\xi}}n,\log^{1/\delta_{\xi}}T))

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T).

This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.4
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, for all
T\in\mathbb{N}
, as
n\to\infty
,

(i)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|=O(1)
;

(ii)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}\|=O(1)
;

(iii)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t}\|=O(1)
;

(iv)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|T}\|=O(1)
;

(v)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n^{2}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|=O(1)
;

(vi)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n^{2}\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|T}-\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t}\|=O(1)
.

Proof.

From (
A.4
), using Lemma
D.13
we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{t|t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1},

(E.10)

indeed
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
is positive definite and finite by Lemma
D.7
(i) and
D.7
(ii).
Therefore,
since
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
and
\mathbf{P}_{t|t}
are deterministic by Lemma
D.6
(i), we have

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}-\{(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\mathbf{I}_{r}+\mathbf{I}_{r}\}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\|\,\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\|=O(1),

(E.11)

because of Lemma
C.6
(i), which is independent of
t
, and Lemma
D.7
(i). This proves part (i).

For part (ii), from (
A.7
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{-1}(\mathbf{P}_{t+1|T}-\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t})\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{A}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{-1}\|^{2}\,\left\{\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|T}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}\|\right\}.

(E.12)

Now, given that
\mathbf{P}_{T|T}
is obtained by the last iteration of the Kalman filter, for
t=T-1
(
E.12
) becomes:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{A}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T-1}^{-1}\|^{2}\,\left\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T-1}\|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|^{2}\frac{M_{A}^{2}}{\underline{M}_{P}^{2}}\left\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T}\|+M_{P}\right\}=O(n^{-2}),

(E.13)

by part (i), Assumption
1
(d), and Lemma
D.7
(i) and
D.7
(ii). Therefore, from part (i) and (
E.13
)

\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|=O(n^{-2})+O(n^{-1}).

(E.14)

For
t=T-2
, (
E.12
) becomes:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T}-\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{-1}(\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2})\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{-1}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{A}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{-1}\|^{2}\,\left\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}\|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\|^{2}\frac{M_{A}^{2}}{\underline{M}_{P}^{2}}\left\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}\|+M_{P}\right\}=O(n^{-2}),

(E.15)

by part (i), Assumption
1
(d), Lemma
D.7
, and (
E.14
). Therefore, from part (i) and (
E.15
)

\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T}-\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\|=O(n^{-2})+O(n^{-1}).

(E.16)

By comparing (
E.14
) and (
E.16
) it is clear that, the same asymptotic bound holds for all
t=T,\ldots,1

\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|^{2}\frac{M_{A}^{2}}{\underline{M}_{P}^{2}}\left\{\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|T}\|+M_{P}\right\}=O(n^{-2}),

(E.17)

and since
\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|T}\|=O(n^{-1})
then it is asymptotically negligible, thus (
E.17
) holds uniformly in
t=T,\ldots,1
because of part (i), and since
\mathbf{P}_{t|T}
and
\mathbf{P}_{t|t}
are deterministic because of Lemma
D.6
(i). It follows that, by part (i)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}\|\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|=O(n^{-1})+O(1)=O(1).

This proves part (ii).

Parts (iii) and (iv) are proved exactly as parts (i) and (ii), respectively, but using Lemmas
C.6
(ii),
D.7
(iii), and
D.7
(iv) instead of Lemmas
C.6
(i),
D.7
(i), and
D.7
(ii).

Part (v) is proved by (
E.17
). Part (vi) is proved as part (v) by repeating the same reasoning leading to the proof of part (iv).
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.5
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
5
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(ii)

\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t|t-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(iii)

\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t|t-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
Given that
\mathbf{F}_{0|0}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
, from (
A.1
) it follows that
\mathbf{F}_{1|0}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
, then, from (
A.3
)

\mathbf{F}_{1|1}=\mathbf{P}_{1|0}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{1|0}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{n1}=(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{1|0}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{n1}

by Lemma
D.13
which can be applied since
\mathbf{P}_{1|0}
is positive definite by Lemma
D.7
(ii). Thus,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}_{1|1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{1|0}^{-1})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\|\mathbf{x}_{n1}\|=O_{p}(1),

(E.18)

by Lemmas
C.3
(i),
C.2
, and
C.10
, and Assumption
2
(a). Then, from (
A.1
),

\|\mathbf{F}_{2|1}\|\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{1|1}\|=O_{p}(1),

(E.19)

because of (
E.18
) and Assumption
1
(d). Therefore, from (
A.3
), using (
E.19
) and the same arguments leading to (
E.18
),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}_{2|2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{F}_{2|1}\|+\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{1|0}^{-1})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\{\|\mathbf{x}_{n1}\|+\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{2|1}\|\}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1).

(E.20)

It is then clear that (
E.19
) and (
E.20
) hold for all
t=1,\ldots,T
, and the result follows from Lemma
C.10
. This proves part (i).

For part (ii), the proof is identical to part (i) but using Lemma
C.3
(ii) instead of Lemma
C.3
(i).
For part (iii) repeat the same steps as in part (ii) but using Lemma
E.3
instead of Lemma
C.10
, and noticing that
\|\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|
, for all
t=1,\ldots,T
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.6
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
5
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

\sqrt{n}\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(ii)

\sqrt{n}\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(iii)

\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{n}\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
Since
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
is positive definite because of Lemma
D.7
(ii), we can use the Woodbury formula in Lemma
D.13

so that, for any
t=1,\ldots,T
, the Kalman filter estimator defined in (
A.3
) can be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|t}=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}+(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}+(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}+\bm{\xi}_{nt}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}+(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}

\displaystyle+(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt},

(E.21)

where in the last step we used the definition of
\mathbf{x}_{nt}
in (
3
).
Then,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}

\displaystyle-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}\|\,\|\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.22)

by Lemma
E.5
(i) and Lemma
C.6
(i), which can be applied by Lemma
D.7
(i). Similarly,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.23)

by the same arguments leading to (
E.22
) and since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
uniformly in
t=1,\ldots,T
, because
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|^{2}]=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=\text{tr}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})=r
by Assumption
6
(b). From (
E.22
) and (
E.23
) it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|+O_{p}(n^{-1})

\displaystyle\leq\|n(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\|n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|+O_{p}(n^{-1})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

(E.24)

by Lemma
C.3
(iii) and Lemma
C.7
(i). This proves part (i).

Part (ii) is proved in the same way, but using Lemmas
D.7
(iii),
D.7
(iv),
E.5
(ii),
C.6
(ii),
C.3
(iv), and
C.7
(ii), instead of Lemmas
D.7
(i),
D.7
(ii),
E.5
(i),
C.6
(i),
C.3
(iii), and
C.7
(i).
For part (iii) repeat the same steps as in part (ii)
but using Lemma
E.5
(iii) instead of Lemma
E.5
(ii), and since
\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}({\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T})
by Lemma
E.2
.
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.7
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, as
n\to\infty
,

(i)

n\|\mathbf{F}_{t|T}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(ii)

n\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t|T}-\mathbf{F}_{0,t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
.

Proof.
For any
t=1,\ldots,T
, using (
A.6
)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}_{t|T}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|\,\|\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\|\,\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{-1}\|\,\|(\mathbf{F}_{t+1|T}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{t|t})\|.

(E.25)

Now, given that
\mathbf{F}_{T|T}
is obtained by the last iteration of the Kalman filter, for
t=T-1
(
E.25
) becomes:

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T-1}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|\,\|\mathbf{A}\|\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T-1}^{-1}\|\,\left\{\|\mathbf{F}_{T|T}\|+\|\mathbf{A}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T-1}\|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|\,\frac{M_{A}}{\underline{M}_{P}}\left\{\|\mathbf{F}_{T|T}-\mathbf{F}_{T}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{T}\|+M_{A}\left[\|\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T-1}-\mathbf{F}_{T-1}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{T-1}\|\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.26)

by Assumption
1
(d), and Lemmas
D.7
(ii),
E.4
(i), and
E.6
(i), and since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
uniformly in
t=1,\ldots,T
, because
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|^{2}]=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=\text{tr}(\bm{\Gamma}^{F})=r
by Assumption
6
(b).
For
t=T-2
(
E.25
) becomes:

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{T-2|T}-\mathbf{F}_{T-2|T-2}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}\|\,\|\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\|\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{-1}\|\,\|(\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{F}_{T-2|T-2})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}\|\,\frac{M_{A}}{\underline{M}_{P}}\left\{\|\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T-1}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{T-1|T-1}-\mathbf{F}_{T-1}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{T-1}\|\right.

\displaystyle\left.+M_{A}\left[\|\mathbf{F}_{T-2|T-2}-\mathbf{F}_{T-2}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{T-2}\|\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.27)

by (
E.26
), Assumption
1
(d), and Lemmas
D.7
(ii),
E.4
(i), and
E.6
(i), and since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
uniformly in
t=1,\ldots,T
. By comparing (
E.26
) and (
E.27
) it is clear that, the same asymptotic bound holds uniformly in
t=T,\ldots,1
, i.e., from (
E.25
) we get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}_{t|T}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}\|\,\frac{M_{A}}{\underline{M}_{P}}\left\{\|\mathbf{F}_{t+1|T}-\mathbf{F}_{t+1|t+1}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{t+1|t+1}-\mathbf{F}_{t+1}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{t+1}\|\right.

\displaystyle\left.+M_{A}\left[\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|\right]\right\}=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(E.28)

This proves part (i).

Part (ii) is proved in the same way but using Lemmas
D.7
(iv),
E.4
(iii), and
E.6
(ii), instead of Lemmas
D.7
(ii),
E.4
(i), and
E.6
(i).
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.8
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
5
, as
n\to\infty
,

(i)

n\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(ii)

n\|\mathbf{F}_{0,t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\text{\tiny GLS}}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(iii)

n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|t}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(vi)

n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|T}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

where
\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}=(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{t}
and

\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\text{\tiny GLS}}=(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{t}
.

Proof.
For part (i), from (
A.3
) we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}=

\displaystyle\,(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}.

(E.29)

Then,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}}\|

(E.30)

\displaystyle\leq\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

by Lemmas
C.6
(iii),
C.2
, and
C.10
, and Assumption
2
(a). And,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.31)

by (
E.22
) in the proof of Lemma
E.6
(i). By substituting (
E.30
) and (
E.31
) into (
E.29
), we prove part (i).

Part (ii) is proved as part (i), but using Lemmas
C.6
(iv) and
E.6
(ii) instead of Lemmas
C.6
(iii) and
E.6
(i).

For part (iii), from (
A.4
), using the same steps leading to (
D.23
) in the proof of Lemma
D.11
, but when using the true parameters, it holds that:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}=

\displaystyle\,({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}

\displaystyle-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1})^{-1}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1})^{-1}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}.

(E.32)

Notice that all inverses in (
E.32
) are well defined because of Lemmas
C.3
(iii),
D.7
(i), and
D.7
(ii) and Assumption
2
(a).
Therefore, from (
E.32
)

\displaystyle n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\|\,n^{2}\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot\|(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O(1),

(E.33)

because of Lemmas
C.3
(iii),
D.7
(i), and
D.7
(ii), and since
\|(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\|=O(1)
, because of the same arguments leading to (
D.24
) in the proof of Lemma
D.11
, which this time hold by
Lemmas
C.3
(iii),
D.7
(i), and
D.7
(ii), instead of Lemmas
D.5
(iii),
D.8
(i), and
D.8
(ii).

Part (iv) is proved as part (iii), but using Lemmas
C.3
(iv), and Assumption
2
(f) instead of Lemmas
C.3
(iii) and Assumption
2
(a).

For part (v),

n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\leq n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}-P_{t|t}\|+n^{2}\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(1),

because of part (iii) and Lemma
E.4
(v). Part (vi) is proved as part (v), but using part (iv) and Lemma
