Lemma E.9
.

Let
\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})
be the log-likelihood obtained when
\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}
and
\bm{\Gamma}^{v}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
.
Then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T\,\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}(nT)^{-1}\left|\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\right|=O_{p}(1).

Proof.

Throughout we consider generic values of the parameters such that
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
where
\mathcal{O}_{n}=\{\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{i}}^{n}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\Lambda_{n}}\}\times\{\mathcal{O}_{\sigma_{i}^{2}}^{n}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\Gamma^{\xi}_{n}}\}\times\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{A}}\times\mathcal{O}_{\Gamma^{v}}
as defined in Section
4.3.4
. Thus the elements of
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
satisfy Assumptions
1
(a),
1
(d),
1
(e),
2
(a),
2
(b), and
2
(f).

First, recall that the log-likelihood (
5
) can be written as

\displaystyle\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle\,\ell(\bm{X}_{nT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})+\ell(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})^{-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})

(E.34)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}}))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}}))^{\prime}(\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}}))^{-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})),

where we used the fact that
\mathbf{F}_{0}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
by Assumption
1
(i) and we used the definitions:

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})=\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\bm{X}_{nT}]\equiv\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T},

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})=\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}}))(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{F}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}}))^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]\equiv\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}.

(E.35)

Now, since (
E.34
) holds for any
\mathbf{F}_{t}
we can always choose
\mathbf{F}_{t}=\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T}
for all
t=1,\ldots,T
, so that

\displaystyle\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T})

\displaystyle-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t-1|T})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})^{-1}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t-1|T})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}).

(E.36)

Second, consider the log-likelihood (
5
) when the autocorrelation of the factors is not accounted for, i.e., when
\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}
and
\bm{\Gamma}^{v}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
,

\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})=-\frac{T}{2}\log\det\left(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbf{x}_{nt}^{\prime}\left(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\right],

(E.37)

where we are imposing Assumption
6
(b), so that we can set
\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
in the log-likelihood. Clearly, (
E.37
) can also be written as

\displaystyle\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})=

\displaystyle\,\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})+\ell_{0}(\bm{F}_{T})-\ell_{0}(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\mathbf{F}_{t})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))

(E.38)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))^{\prime}(\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))^{-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})),

where

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})=\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\bm{X}_{nT}]\equiv\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T},

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{V}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})=\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]\equiv\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}.

(E.39)

Now, since (
E.38
) holds for any
\mathbf{F}_{t}
we can always choose
\mathbf{F}_{t}=\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T}
for all
t=1,\ldots,T
, so that

\displaystyle\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T}^{\prime}\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}).

(E.40)

Under Assumption
4
the conditional mean,
\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T}
in (
E.35
) is a linear function of
\bm{X}_{nT}
, so it can be obtained by linear projection, and, therefore it is given by the correctly specified Kalman smoother, i.e., using as parameters
\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}
and
\underline{\bm{\theta}}
and when replacing
\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}
with
\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi}
, thus,

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\mathcal{F}}}_{0,t|T}=\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}.

(E.41)

Likewise, under Assumption
4
,
\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T}

in (
E.39
) is also linear, however, since this is the conditional mean for the case in which no dynamics for the factors is specified, it is given by the simpler linear projection (using Lemma
D.13
)

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\mathcal{G}}}_{0,t|T}

\displaystyle=\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}=(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+\mathbf{I}_{r})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}=\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}.

(E.42)

Now, consider the generalized least squares estimator of the factors computed for generic values of the parameters
\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}
:

\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny GLS}}=(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt},

(E.43)

Then, since we restrict to
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny GLS}}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+\mathbf{I}_{r})^{-1}-(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=O(n^{-1})O_{p}(1),

(E.44)

by Lemmas
C.3
(viii),
C.6
(iv), and
C.10
.

Moreover, by Lemmas
E.7
(ii) and
E.8
(ii)

\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny GLS}}\|\leq\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|t}\|+\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|t}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny GLS}}\|=O(n^{-1})O_{p}(1).

(E.45)

In particular, the bound in (
E.45
) is a product of a stochastic and a non-stochastic term because of (
E.28
) in the proof of Lemma
E.7
, and (
E.30
) and (
E.31
) in the proof of Lemma
E.8
.

Therefore, from (
E.44
) and (
E.45
) (see also
Bai and Li 2016
)

\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\|=O(n^{-1})O_{p}(1).

(E.46)

The results in (
E.44
)-(
E.46
) depend on
t
only through the
O_{p}(1)
term which in turn is just function of
\mathbf{x}_{nt}
and do not depend on the choice of the parameters as long as they belong to
\mathcal{O}_{n}
, as assumed. Since the parameters are deterministic and using Lemma
E.3
, we then have

\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\;\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\|=O(n^{-1})O_{p}(\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.47)

Then, consider the conditional covariance in (
E.39
) and letting
\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}=(\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+\mathbf{I}_{r})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}=

\displaystyle\,(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}](\mathbf{I}_{r}-\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}+\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}^{\prime},

where we used also Lemma
C.11
. Moreover,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}]=(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]](\mathbf{I}_{r}-\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}+\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]]\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}^{\prime},

is finite and positive definite,
since
\|\underline{\mathbf{K}}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O(1)
by Lemmas
C.2
,
C.3
(vi), and
C.3
(viii), and because
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\;\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]]
and
\mathbb{E}[\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\;\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\phi}_{n}}[\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]]
are both finite and positive definite by Assumptions
1
(b) and
2
(f), and Lemma
C.1
(v).
Therefore, by Markov’s inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}\setminus\{\bm{\varphi}_{n}\}}\;\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}\|=O_{p}(1),\hskip 9.24994pt\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}\setminus\{\bm{\varphi}_{n}\}}\;\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1).

(E.48)

This bound is tighter when
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\bm{\varphi}_{n}
. Indeed, in that case from (
A.4
) and (
A.7
), setting
\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{0}_{r\times r}
therein, we have

{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\phi}_{n})=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}=\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{I}_{r})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}.

where we also used Assumption
6
(b) for which
\bm{\Gamma}^{F}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
. Therefore,
{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\phi}_{n})
is deterministic and independent of
t
, and such that

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\phi}_{n})\|=O(n^{-1}),\hskip 18.49988pt\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|({\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\phi}_{n}))^{-1}\|=O(n),

(E.49)

because by Lemma
C.6
(ii) which holds since
\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}
is positive definite by Assumption
2
(f). Furthermore, following the same steps leading to (
E.33
) in the proof of Lemma
E.8
we have

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\phi}_{n})-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Gamma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(n^{-2}).

(E.50)

Turning to the conditional covariance in (
E.35
), because of (
E.41
) we can write

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}=

\displaystyle\,\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}+\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}})(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}+\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}})^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}+\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T})(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T})^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T})(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}})^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]+\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}})(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T})^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}].

(E.51)

From (
E.47
) and (
E.51
) we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}\setminus\{\bm{\varphi}_{n}\}}\;\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.52)

This bound is tighter when
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\bm{\varphi}_{n}
. Indeed, we have
{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\varphi}_{n})=\mathbf{P}_{0,t|T}
, which is deterministic by Lemma
D.6
(iii), and

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\varphi}_{n})-{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\varphi}_{n})\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|T}-{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\varphi}_{n})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{0,t|T}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|+\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}-{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}(\bm{\varphi}_{n})\|

\displaystyle=O(n^{-2}),

(E.53)

by (
E.50
) and Lemma
E.8
(vi).

Now, consider

\displaystyle\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg\{\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)^{\prime}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)^{\prime}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}+2\mathbf{x}_{nt}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)\bigg\}-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t-1|T})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})^{-1}(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t-1|T})+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}\prime}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}).

(E.54)

Consider all terms on the rhs of (
E.54
). First, by (
E.47
), for all
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg\{\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)^{\prime}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)+2\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)^{\prime}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}

\displaystyle+2\mathbf{x}_{nt}^{\prime}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\left(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right)\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,T\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\|\,\|\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}\,\|(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\|

\displaystyle+T\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\|\,\|(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\|\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{x}}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+T\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\|^{2}\,\|\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}\,\|(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{x}}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(T\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T),

(E.55)

where we used Lemmas
C.2
and
E.3
, which implies also that
\|\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\|=O_{p}(\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T)
, and
Assumption
2
(a).

Second, for all
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t-1|T})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})^{-1}(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t-1|T})+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}\prime}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left|\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})\right|+\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t-1|T})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})^{-1}(\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\,\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t-1|T})\right|+\left|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}\prime}\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t}^{\text{\tiny REG}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(T\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T),

(E.56)

because of (
E.46
), Assumptions
1
(d) and
1
(e),
and Lemma
E.3
jointly with the same arguments leading to (
E.55
).

Third, by (
E.48
) and (
E.52
), and
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, which is Weyl’s inequality

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})\bigg|=\bigg|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\left(\det\{\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})^{-1}\}\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\bigg|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\log\left(\left\{\nu^{(j)}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T})\right\}\left\{\nu^{(j)}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})\right\}^{-1}\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\bigg|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\log\left(\left\{\nu^{(1)}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})+\nu^{(j)}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})\right\}\left\{\nu^{(j)}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})\right\}^{-1}\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\bigg|\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{r}\log\left(1+\left\{n\|\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}\|\right\}\left\{n\nu^{(j)}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})\right\}^{-1}\right)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\bigg|\frac{T}{2}\left\{n\|\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T}-\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T}\|\right\}\left\{n\nu^{(j)}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{V}}}_{0,t|T})\right\}^{-1}\bigg|+o(Tn^{-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(Tn^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T),

(E.57)

where in the second last line we took into account also (
E.49
) and (
E.53
), hence, (
E.57
) holds for all
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
.

Summing up, by noticing that (
E.55
), (
E.56
), and (
E.57
) hold for all
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
, from (
E.54
) we have:

\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}(nT)^{-1}\left|\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\right|=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.10
.

Let
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{*}=(\text{vec}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}^{2*}_{1}\cdots\widehat{\sigma}^{2*}_{n})^{\prime}
be the vector of QML estimators of the entries of
{\bm{\phi}}_{n}
maximizing

\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})
defined in (
5
),
and let
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{\dagger}=(\text{vec}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\dagger})^{\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}^{2{\dagger}}_{1}\cdots\widehat{\sigma}^{2{\dagger}}_{n})^{\prime}
be the vector of QML estimators of the entries of
{\bm{\phi}}_{n}
maximizing

\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})
defined in (
E.37
) in the proof of Lemma
E.9
. Then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\dagger}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}Tn^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\dagger}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
From Lemma
E.9
we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}

\displaystyle\left|\sup_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\sup_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\sup_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\left|\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.58)

Therefore, by continuity of the log-likelihoods and (
E.58
),
we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\dagger}\|\\
|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}|\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\bigg\|\arg\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\max_{(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\,\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2})^{\prime}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\arg\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\max_{(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\,\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2})^{\prime}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\bigg\|\arg\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\max_{(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\,\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2})^{\prime}\in\mathcal{O}_{1}}(nT)^{-1}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\arg\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\max_{(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\,\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2})^{\prime}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}(nT)^{-1}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\bigg\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

This proves parts (i) and (iii), while part (ii) is a direct consequence of part (i). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.11
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT},T)\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
;

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT},T)\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT},T)\,|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
.

Proof.
For part (i)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{{\dagger}}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{(0)}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|,

(E.61)

where
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}
is the PC estimator defined in Appendix
A.1
. Then, from
Barigozzi 2023

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\dagger}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.62)

while from
Barigozzi 2023

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})).

(E.63)

Part (i) then follows by substituting (
E.62
), (
E.63
), and Lemma
E.10
(i) into (
E.61
).

For part (ii), from
Barigozzi 2023

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\dagger}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.64)

while from
Barigozzi 2023

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})).

(E.65)

Then, part (ii) is proved analogously to part (i), using (
E.64
), (
E.65
), and Lemma
E.10
(ii).

For part (iii), let
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}
. Then, consider

\displaystyle|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|\leq|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}|+|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}|+|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|.

(E.66)

From
Bai and Li 2016
and noticing that the estimator of the idiosyncratic variances is unaffected by the chosen identifying constraints, we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})).

(E.67)

Moreover,

\displaystyle|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|=

\displaystyle\,\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\right\}\right|+2\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it}\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|^{2}\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+2\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|

\displaystyle+2\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}x_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})),

(E.68)

by (
E.62
), (
E.63
), Lemma
C.12
(i) combined with
Assumption
6
(b), Lemma
C.12
(ii), and since
\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\leq\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
by Assumption
1
(a) and Lemma
D.19
(i). Part (iii) then follows by substituting (
E.67
), (
E.68
), and Lemma
E.10
(iii) into (
E.66
).
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.12
.

Let
\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{*}=(\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*})^{\prime}\;\text{vech}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v*})^{\prime})^{\prime}
be the QML estimator of the entries of
{\bm{\theta}}
maximizing

\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})
defined in (
5
), then,
under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}-\mathbf{A}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v*}-\bm{\Gamma}^{v\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
Throughout we consider generic values of the parameters such that
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
where
\mathcal{O}_{n}=\{\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{i}}^{n}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\Lambda_{n}}\}\times\{\mathcal{O}_{\sigma_{i}^{2}}^{n}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\Gamma^{\xi}_{n}}\}\times\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{A}}\times\mathcal{O}_{\Gamma^{v}}
as defined in Section
4.3.4
. Thus the elements of
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
satisfy Assumptions
1
(a),
1
(d),
1
(e),
2
(a),
2
(b), and
2
(f).

The log-likelihood depends on
\underline{\mathbf{A}}
and
\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v}
only through
\ell(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\theta}})
and
\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})
. Let us consider both log-likelihoods separately. First,
since by Assumption
1
(i)
\mathbf{F}_{0}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
, we have

\displaystyle\ell(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\theta}})

\displaystyle=-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})^{-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}),

which is clearly maximized by
{\bm{\theta}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}
.

Second, (see also (
E.34
), (
E.35
), and (
E.41
) in the proof of Lemma
E.9
)

\displaystyle\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\mathcal{P}}}_{0,t|T})^{-1}(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{F}}_{0,t|T}),

Now, by (
E.47
), (
E.52
), and (
E.53
) in the proof of Lemma
E.9
, we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}\bigg|\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n},\underline{\bm{\theta}})-\ell_{0}(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\bigg|=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.69)

The proof of parts (i) and (ii) follows from (
E.69
) and by continuity of the log-likelihood and since
\ell_{0}(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})
does not depend on
\underline{\bm{\theta}}
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.14
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*}-\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
For part (i) consider

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|.

(E.70)

First, note that (
E.62
) in the proof of Lemma
E.11
holds for all
i
, this is seen from the proof of
Barigozzi 2023
, thus, jointly with Lemma
E.10
(i),

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.71)

Second, from
Barigozzi 2023
, when imposing Assumption
6
(b),

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}=

\displaystyle\,\left\{(nT)^{-1}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{j}^{\prime}n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}+\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{j}^{\prime}n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{(nT)^{-1}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{jt}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{j})^{\prime}n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{j})^{\prime}n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,1.a+1.b+1.c+1.d+1.e+1.f,\;\text{ say,}

where

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|1.a\|=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|1.b\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}),

(E.72)

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|1.c\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

which follows by using

•

for
1.a
Assumption
1
(a), Lemma
D.1
(ii), and
Barigozzi 2023
;

•

for
1.b
Assumption
1
(a), Lemma
D.1
(ii), and Lemma
E.1
(ii) and the union bound;

•

for
1.c
Assumption
1
(a), Lemma
D.1
(ii),
E.1
(ii) and the union bound, and
Barigozzi 2023
, where we also used the fact that
\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]|=O(n^{-1})
by Assumption
2
(b);

while
1.d
,
1.e
, and
1.f
are dominated by and
1.a
,
1.b
, and
1.c
, respectively, because of Lemma
D.1
(i).
It follows that

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

(E.73)

By substituting (
E.71
) and (
E.73
) into (
E.70
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))+O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

This proves part (i).

For part (ii), first, consider

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}|

\displaystyle+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|.

(E.74)

From (
E.67
) in the proof of Lemma
E.11

\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

(E.75)

Indeed from
Bai and Li 2016
we see that (
E.75
) is decomposed into the sum of 13 terms, and all depend on
i
only through
\bm{\lambda}_{i}
which is such that
\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\leq M_{\lambda}
by Assumption
1
(a), with the exceptions of the following terms

\displaystyle A_{1}

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})^{\prime}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})\right|=O_{p}(\max(T^{-1}\log n,n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T)),

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}2\left|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{{\dagger}\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi{\dagger}})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{{\dagger}}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right|=O_{p}((n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}),

\displaystyle A_{3}

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}2\left|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{{\dagger}\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi{\dagger}})^{-1}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right\}\left\{T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\right\}\right|=O_{p}(T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n}),

where we used:

•

for
A_{1}
part (i) and Lemmas
E.10
(i) and
C.12
(i) jointly with Assumption
6
(b);

•

for
A_{2}
Lemma
E.1
(ii) and the union bound,
plus
Bai and Li 2012
;

•

for
A_{3}
the same arguments used for
A_{2}
plus
Bai and Li 2016
.

Second, notice that, by Lemmas
C.12
(i) and
D.1
(ii), term
1.b
in (
E.72
) is such that

\displaystyle 1.b

\displaystyle=({\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})+o_{p}(T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}).

(E.76)

Thus, from the above arguments and (
E.76
),

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

(E.77)

and, therefore, from (
E.71
) and (
E.77
)

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

(E.78)

Then, from (
E.68
) in the proof of Lemma
E.11

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\ddagger}}-\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|^{2}\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{{\dagger}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

(E.79)

by (
E.72
), (
E.78
), Assumption
1
(a), Lemma
C.12
(i) combined with Assumption
6
(b), and since

\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})+O(1)
again by (
E.72
) and Assumption
1
(a).

Finally,

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\sigma_{i}^{2\text{\tiny OLS}}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}\prime}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}).

(E.80)

Indeed, for the first term on the rhs of (
E.80
) we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|^{2}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}^{2}]\right|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}),

(E.81)

by Assumption
1
(a), Lemma
C.12
(i) for the first term,
Barigozzi
et al. 2018
and
Fan
et al. 2011
, which we can apply because of Assumption
5
(b) for the second term, and Lemma
E.1
(ii) and the union bound for the third term. For all other on the rhs of (
E.80
) we just need to use Assumption
1
(a), Lemma
C.12
(i) and the fact that

\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})+O(1)
again by (
E.72
) and Assumption
1
(a).

By using Lemma
E.10
(iii), (
E.75
), (
E.79
), and (
E.80
) into (
E.74
) we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T)+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

which proves part (ii).

Part (iii) immediately follows from part (ii), indeed

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*}-\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}\|\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

For part (iv) we have

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\|=\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}\right\}^{-1}\leq C_{\xi}+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

because of part (ii) and Assumption
2
(a).

To conclude, for part (v) we have

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\leq\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\|\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*}-\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

by parts (iii), (iv), and Assumption
2
(a). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.15
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n)\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n)\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n)\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

\omega_{n,T\delta_{v}}\,\sqrt{n}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
,

with
\omega_{n,T\delta_{v}}=\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n).

Proof.

For part (i) we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,2n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+2n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,2n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+\|\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\|\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\|\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

(E.82)

by Assumptions
1
(a),
2
(a), and Lemmas
E.13
(ii) and
E.14
(v). This proves part (i).

Part (ii) is proved in the same way as part (i).

For part (iii), by part (ii) and
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
which is Weyl’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}|\nu^{(r)}

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})-\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}){\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

(E.83)

Moreover (note that
x-y\geq-|x-y|
for any
x,y\in\mathbb{R}
),

\displaystyle\det(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})=

\displaystyle\,\prod_{j=1}^{r}\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\geq\left\{\nu^{(r)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\right\}^{r}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})-|\nu^{(r)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})-\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}){\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})|\right\}^{r},

thus, by Lemma
C.3
(iv), which implies
\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(r)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})>0
,
and (
E.83
) it follows that, with probability tending to one as
n,T\to\infty
, we have
\det(n^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})>0
, or, equivalently
n^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}
is positive definite,
i.e.
n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
.
This proves part (iii).

For part (iv), we have

\displaystyle n\|

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

because of parts (i) and (iii) and Lemma
C.3
(iii).

Part (v) follows directly from parts (ii) and (iv). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.16
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|t-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|(\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|T}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

For part (i),

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}^{*}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

by Lemma
D.7
(i) and since the second term on the rhs depends only on the estimation error of
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}
,
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v*}
,
n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}
,

n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}

and
n^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}
, which are all bounded by Lemmas
E.13
(ii),
E.13
(iv),
E.13
(v),
E.15
(ii), and
E.15
(iv). This proves part (i).

Part (ii) is proved in the same way as part (i) but using Lemma
D.7
(ii).

For part (iii), from (
A.4
) using the same steps leading to (
D.23
) in the proof of Lemma
D.11
but when using as parameters
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{*}=

\displaystyle\,{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}+\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}

(E.84)

\displaystyle-{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}.

Notice that all inverses in (
E.84
) are well defined because of part (ii) and Lemmas
E.15
(iii) and
E.14
(iv).

Therefore, from (
E.84
)

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{*}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1}\|\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot\|((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n^{-1}),

because of parts (i) and (ii), Lemma
E.15
(iii), and since, by
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
which is Weyl’s inequality,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1}\|=\left\{\nu^{(r)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\nu^{(r)}((\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1})+\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left[\nu^{(1)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\right]^{-1}+\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left[\nu^{(1)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})\right]^{-1}+1\right\}^{-1}\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{1-\left[\nu^{(1)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})\right]^{-1}\right\}\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})\right\}^{-1}+O_{p}(n^{-2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(1),

again by parts (i) and (ii) and Lemma
E.15
(iii). This proves part (iii).

For part (iv), from (
A.7
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{*}-\mathbf{P}_{t|t}^{*}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t}^{*}\|^{2}\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|^{2}\,\|(\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{*})^{-1}\|^{2}\{\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|T}^{*}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{t+1|t}^{*}\|\}.

(E.85)

Start with
t=T-1
, then from (
E.85
),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}^{*}-\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}^{*}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-1}^{*}\|^{2}\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|^{2}\,\|(\mathbf{P}_{T|T-1}^{*})^{-1}\|^{2}\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T}^{*}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T|T-1}^{*}\|\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(E.86)

by parts (i), (ii), and (iii), and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
E.13
(iv). From (
E.86
)
it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-1|T}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-1|T-1}\|+\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-1|T}-\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-1|T-1}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1})+O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(E.87)

Thus, at
t=T-2
, from (
E.85
) and (
E.87
),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T}^{*}-\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}^{*}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}_{T-2|T-2}^{*}\|^{2}\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|^{2}\,\|(\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{*})^{-1}\|^{2}\{\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T}^{*}\|+\|\mathbf{P}_{T-1|T-2}^{*}\|\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(E.88)

From (
E.88
) it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-2|T}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-2|T-2}\|+\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-2|T}-\mathbf{P}^{*}_{T-2|T-2}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1})+O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(E.89)

Since all the bounds in (
E.86
)-(
E.89
) are the same for all
t
, from part (i) and (
E.85
) we have

\displaystyle\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|T}\|\leq\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|t}\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n\|\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|T}-\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)+O_{p}(n^{-1}).

This proves part (iv) and completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.17
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

for all
s=0,\ldots,T
,
\|\mathbf{F}^{*}_{t|s}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t\leq s
;

(ii)

n\|\mathbf{F}^{*}_{t|T}-\mathbf{F}^{*}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

(iii)

n\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{*}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}*}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t
;

where
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}*}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}.

Proof.

The proof of part (i) follows the same steps as the proof of Lemma
D.14
but when using Lemmas
E.13
,
E.14
, and
E.15
, instead of Lemmas
D.1
,
D.3
,
D.4
, and
D.5
.

For part (ii), from (
A.6
) and (
A.1
)

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{*}-{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{*}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t+1|t}^{*})^{-1}\|\,\{\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|T}^{*}\|+\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|t}^{*}\|\}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{*}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t+1|t}^{*})^{-1}\|\,\{\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|T}^{*}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{*}\|\}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

by part (i) (when
s=T
and
s=t
),
Lemmas

E.16
(ii) and

E.16
(iii), and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
E.13
(iv). This proves part (ii).

For part (iii), from (
A.3
) and (
A.1
), by using Lemma
D.13
(see also (
D.49
) in the proof of Lemma
D.16
for more details)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{*}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}^{*}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}+\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}(\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}^{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}

\displaystyle+\left\{(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1})^{-1}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\right\}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}+(\widehat{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}\right\}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|t-1}^{*}.

(E.90)

Notice that the inverses in (
E.90
) are all well defined by Lemmas
E.14
(iv),
E.15
(iii), and
E.16
(ii).

Now, by Lemma
C.5

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(E.91)

Furthermore, by Lemmas
C.6
(iii) and
E.15
(iv)

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1})^{-1}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\|=O(n^{-2}),

(E.92)

and by Lemmas
C.3
(vii) and
E.15
(ii),

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(\sqrt{n}).

(E.93)

Indeed, we can apply Lemmas
C.5
and
C.6
(iii), since
\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
by Lemma
E.16
(ii),

\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)

by Lemma
E.14
(iv), and, by Lemmas
C.2
and
E.13
(ii) we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,2n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\|+n^{-1}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,2n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|+n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,{T}^{-1/2})).

which, by Weyl’s inequality
(
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, Theorem 1)
, implies

\displaystyle n^{-1}|\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})-\nu^{(j)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})|\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,{T}^{-1/2})),

and, therefore, for
j=1,\ldots,r,
,

\underline{C}_{j}\leq\text{p-}\lim\!\!\!\!\!\!\inf_{n,T\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\leq\text{p-}\lim\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{n,T\to\infty}n^{-1}\nu^{(j)}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\leq\overline{C}_{j}.

By using (
E.91
), (
E.92
), (
E.93
) into (
E.90
):

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{*}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}+({\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1})^{-1}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{x}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle+\|\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}+(\widehat{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}^{*})^{-1})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\|{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|t-1}^{*}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.94)

by part (i) (when
s=t-1
), Lemma
C.10
, and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
E.13
(i). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.18
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
, for
s=t
and
s=T
:

(i)

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{*}_{t|s}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(i)

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{*}_{t|s}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
;

(ii)

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}^{*}_{t|s}\xi_{it}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
.

Proof.

First notice that, for all
k=t-T,\ldots,t-1
,

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{n,t-k}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(1),

(E.95)

by Lemma
C.12
. The proof of part (i) follows by iterating either forward or backwards since both

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|
and
\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|

are functions of (
E.95
) because of Lemma
E.17
.

Part (ii) follows from part (i) and Assumption
1
(a). Part (iii) follows by substituting
\mathbf{F}_{t}
with
\xi_{it}
in (
E.95
) and then by applying Lemma
C.12
(ii). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.19
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT},T{\log^{-1/2}n})\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*\prime}+\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{*}\}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(1).

Proof.

Start with

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,2\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}-\mathbf{F}_{t})(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|,

(E.96)

and notice that if the first term on the rhs is
o_{p}(1)
then the second term is dominated by the first one. So let us consider the first term on the rhs of (
E.96
):

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{*})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{*}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}*})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}*}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,I^{*}+II^{*}+III^{*},\;\text{ say.}

(E.97)

Let us consider each term in (
E.97
). First,

\displaystyle I^{*}\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t}^{*}\|\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t+1|t}^{*})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbf{F}}_{t+1|t+1}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.98)

by Lemmas
E.16
(ii),
E.16
(iii), and
E.18
, and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}-\mathbf{A}\|=O_{p}(1)
, by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
E.13
(iv). Second, from (
E.90
) and (
E.94
) in the proof of Lemma
E.17

\displaystyle II^{*}\leq

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1})\left\{\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1|t-1}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1})\left\{n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\left\|n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t-1|t-1}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(E.99)

because of Lemmas
C.2
,
C.12
(i), combined with Assumption
6
(b),
C.12
(iii), and
E.18
and since
\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|=O_{p}(1)
by Assumption
1
(d) and Lemma
E.13
(iv).

Finally, let us consider the last term in (
E.97
). First, notice that we can write

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}*}-\mathbf{F}_{t}=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\mathbf{F}_{t}+(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt},

which implies

\displaystyle III^{*}\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})\|\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\sqrt{n}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\sqrt{n}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot n^{-1/2}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,III_{a}^{*}+III_{b}^{*}+III_{c}^{*}+III_{d}^{*},\;\text{ say.}

(E.100)

Then,

\displaystyle III_{a}^{*}\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\|\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|\,\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*}\|\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})),

(E.101)

where we used: for the first term on the rhs
Lemmas
C.3
(iii),
C.12
(i), combined with Assumption
6
(b), and
C.8
(iv) since

n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\|=n^{-1}T^{-1}\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\|
, while for the second term on the rhs we used
Assumption
2
(a), and
Lemmas

C.2
,

C.3
(iii),

C.12
(i), combined with Assumption
6
(b), and
E.11
(ii).
Moreover,

\displaystyle III_{b}^{*}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(E.102)

by Lemmas
C.12
(i), combined with Assumption
6
(b),
E.13
(ii), and
E.15
(v),

\displaystyle III_{c}^{*}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(E.103)

by Lemmas
C.3
(iii) and
C.8
(iv),
and

\displaystyle III_{d}^{*}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(E.104)

by Lemmas
C.12
(iii) and
E.15
(v). By substituting (
E.101
), (
E.102
), (
E.103
), and (
E.104
) into (
E.100
) we have

\displaystyle III^{*}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})).

(E.105)

Combining (
E.98
), (
E.99
), and (
E.105
) we have

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(E.106)

which once substituted into (
E.96
), jointly with Lemma
E.16
(iv) give

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*\prime}+\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{*}\}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*\prime}-T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|+\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{*}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n}))+O_{p}(n^{-1}),

which completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.20
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
6
, the EM estimators of the parameters
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)}
defined in Section
3.2
, exist and are unique, for any
k\geq 0
.

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{O}_{i}=\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{i}}\times\mathcal{O}_{\sigma_{i}^{2}}\subset\mathbb{R}^{r+1}
, and

\mathcal{O}_{r}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{A}}\times\mathcal{O}_{\Gamma^{v}}\subset\mathbb{R}^{r^{2}+r(r+1)/2}

with
\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{i}}
,
\mathcal{O}_{\sigma_{i}^{2}}
,
\mathcal{O}_{\mathcal{A}}
, and
\mathcal{O}_{\Gamma^{v}}
defined in Section
4.3.4
.

At any iteration
k\geq 0
the M-step requires solving the
n+1
finite dimensional maximizations:

(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)},\widehat{\sigma}^{2(k+1)}_{i})=\arg\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\max_{(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i},\underline{\sigma}^{2}_{i})\in\mathcal{O}_{i}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{(k)}}[\ell_{i}(\mathbf{x}_{i1}\ldots\mathbf{x}_{iT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i},\underline{\sigma}^{2}_{i})],\hskip 18.49988pti=1,\ldots,n,

(E.107)

and

(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)},\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k+1)})=\arg\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\max_{(\underline{\mathbf{A}},\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})\in\mathcal{O}_{r}}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{(k)}}[\ell(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\mathbf{A}},\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})],

(E.108)

where

\ell_{i}(\mathbf{x}_{i1}\ldots\mathbf{x}_{iT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i},\underline{\sigma}^{2}_{i})=-\frac{T}{2}\log(\underline{\sigma}^{2}_{i})-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{(x_{it}-\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}_{i}},

(E.109)

and

\ell(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\mathbf{A}},\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})=-\frac{T}{2}\log\det(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})-\frac{1}{2}\!\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\right)^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\underline{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{F}_{t-1}\right).

(E.110)

Now, the log-likelihoods (
E.109
) and (
E.110
) to be maximized are continuous and differentiable in
\mathcal{O}_{i}
and
\mathcal{O}_{r}
, which are a compact sets by Assumptions
1
(a),
1
(d),
1
(e), and
2
(a). Moreover, the log-likelihoods are concave in their arguments, and (
E.107
) and (
E.108
) have a closed form expressions given in (
13
)-(
14
) and (
15
)-(
16
), respectively. Last, notice that the true values of the parameters are fully identified by Assumption
6
.

Therefore, by, e.g.,
Gourieroux and
Monfort 1995
,

\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
and
\widehat{\sigma}^{2(k+1)}_{i}
,
i=1,\ldots,n
, and
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)}
and
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k+1)}
exist and are unique for any
k\geq 0
. In particular, this result holds for
k=k^{*}
, i.e., for the EM estimators, and for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
since (
E.107
) can be solved separately for any
i
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.21
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
4
, and
6
,
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
has a a local maximum denoted as
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}=(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{1}^{**\prime}\cdots\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{n}^{**\prime}\;{\sigma}^{2**}_{1}\cdots{\sigma}^{2**}_{n}\;\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}})^{**\prime},\text{vech}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}})^{v**\prime})
, such that

(i)

for all
i=1,\ldots,n
,
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|=0
;

(ii)

for all
i=1,\ldots,n
,
\lim_{k\to\infty}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k)}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|=0
;

(iii)

\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\|=0
;

(iv)

\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k)}-\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v**}\|=0
;

where all convergences are monotonic.

Proof.

First notice that any maximum of
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
is also a maximum of
\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})\equiv(nT)^{-1}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
. Now, the starting point of the EM is such that
\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(0)})>-\infty
and since
\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
is continuous and differentiable in the interior of
\mathcal{O}_{n}
, then
\{\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\}_{k\geq 0}
is bounded from above for any
n,T\in\mathbb{N}
.

Consider the definitions:

\displaystyle\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{(k)}}[\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})+\tilde{\ell}(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|\bm{X}_{nT}]-\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{(k)}}[\tilde{\ell}(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n},\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})-\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n},\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}).

(E.111)

Then, for any
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
and any
n
and
T
,
