\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})-\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}[\tilde{\ell}(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})-\tilde{\ell}(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nT}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}\left[\left.\log\frac{f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})}\right|\bm{X}_{nT}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{nT}\log\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}\left[\left.\frac{f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})}\right|\bm{X}_{nT}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nT}\log\int_{\mathbb{R}^{rT}}\frac{f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})}f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\mathrm{d}\bm{F}_{T}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nT}\log\int_{\mathbb{R}^{rT}}f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})\mathrm{d}\bm{F}_{T}=\frac{1}{nT}\log(1)=0,

by Jensen’s inequality. Hence, we have (see also
Dempster
et al. 1977
, Lemma 1
)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{\mathcal{H}}(\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}).

(E.112)

Therefore, from (
E.111
) and (
E.112
), for any
k
,

\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)})-\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\geq\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})-\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\geq 0,

(E.113)

where the last inequality holds by definition of the M-step. This shows that the log-likelihood
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})
increases monotonically as
k
increases.

Given that

\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})
has a unique maximum by Lemma
E.20
,
and since for any
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
and
\overline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
, the function
\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\overline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
is continuous in
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
and
\overline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
and the components of the gradient vector
\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\overline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
are continuous in
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
, from
Wu 1983

\lim_{k\to\infty}\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})=\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}),

(E.114)

where the convergence is monotonic and
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}
is a local maximum of
\tilde{\ell}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
.

Now notice that the solution of the M-step is such that
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)}
do not depend on other parameters at the same iteration, while
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)}
depends only on
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
, and
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k+1)}
depends only on
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)}
.

Since we are considering a Gaussian log-likelihood, from
Wu 1983
holds, we have that, for any
i=1,\ldots,n
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})-\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\geq M_{\mathcal{Q}}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2},

(E.115)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)},\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})-\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)},\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\geq M_{\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\|(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)})-(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k)})\|^{2},

for some finite positive reals
M_{\mathcal{Q}}
and
M_{\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}
, independent of
i
, and where we use the short-hand notations:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})=\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{1},\ldots,\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)},\ldots,\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{n},\underline{\sigma}_{1}^{2},\ldots,\underline{\sigma}_{n}^{2},\underline{\mathbf{A}},\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}),

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)},\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})=\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{1},\ldots,\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)},\ldots,\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{n},\underline{\sigma}_{1}^{2},\ldots,\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k)},\ldots,\underline{\sigma}_{n}^{2},\underline{\mathbf{A}},\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}).

And, similarly,

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})-\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\geq M_{A^{\prime}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)}\|^{2},

(E.116)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)},\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k+1)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})-\tilde{\mathcal{Q}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)},\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k)};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})\geq M_{v^{\prime}}\|\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)}\;\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k+1)})-\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k)})\|^{2},

for some finite positive reals
M_{A^{\prime}}
and
M_{v^{\prime}}
.

Therefore, from (
E.113
), (
E.114
), (
E.115
), and (
E.116
), we have

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\|(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)})-(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k)})\|=0,

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)}\;\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k+1)})-\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)};\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k)})\|=0,

which are sufficient conditions for applying the result in
Wu 1983
, i.e., such that

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\|(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k)})-(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**})\|=0,

\displaystyle\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k)}\;\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k)})-\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\;\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v**})\|=0.

This completes the proof.

\Box

Lemma E.22
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}},\sqrt{nT}\log^{-1/2}n,T)\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}},\sqrt{nT}\log^{-1/2}n,T)\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}^{2**}_{i}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2*}_{i}|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v**}-\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v*}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

First, notice that running the EM algorithm using
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
is equivalent to running the EM using

\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
, and such EM will converge to a local maximum of
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
because Lemma
E.21
would hold also in this case. Moreover, since
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
has clearly a unique maximum, then
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}
is a local maximum of
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
but also the unique global maximum of
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
.

Consider the global QML estimator
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}=(\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{*\prime}\;\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{*\prime})^{\prime}
, where we defined
\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{*}=(\text{vec}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{*})^{\prime}\;\widehat{\sigma}_{1}^{2*}\cdots\widehat{\sigma}_{n}^{2*})^{\prime}
and
\widehat{\bm{\theta}}^{*}=(\text{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*})^{\prime}\;\text{vech}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v*})^{\prime})
maximizing
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
.
The elements of
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}
satisfy Lemma
E.13
.

Now, by definition the components of the gradient of
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
computed in
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}
are such that (notice that
\ell(\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
does not depend on
\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
)

\displaystyle\mathbf{0}_{r}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(x_{it}-\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}+\xi_{it}-\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}},

(E.117)

and (
E.117
) is equivalent to

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i})=

\displaystyle\,\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\bm{\lambda}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})+\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}.

(E.118)

By comparing (
E.118
) with Lemma
E.14
(i) (see in particular (
E.71
) and (
E.72
) in its proof),
and by Lemma
C.13
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),

which, from (
E.117
), implies

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}\right\|

(E.119)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})).

Then, since
\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
is linear in
\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
, there exists a positive real
M_{S}^{\prime}
such that

\displaystyle\|T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}\|\geq{M_{S}^{\prime}}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|,

(E.120)

since, by definition,
T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}}=\mathbf{0}_{r}
. Therefore, from (
E.119
) and (
E.120
)

\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),

which proves part (i).

Similarly, using Lemma (
E.14
)(ii) (see in particular (
E.74
), (
E.75
), and (
E.79
) in its proof), we can show that

\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),

(E.121)

and

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}|\geq M_{G}^{\prime}|\widehat{\sigma}^{2**}_{i}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2*}_{i}|.

(E.122)

By using (
E.121
) into (
E.122
), we prove part (ii).

Parts (iii) and (iv) follow in a similar way from Lemma
E.13
(iv) and
E.13
(v), respectively. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.23
.

Consider the EM estimator
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
, for any
k\geq 0
.
Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

\min(n^{2}\log^{-4/\delta_{v}}T,nT\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T{\log^{-1/2}n},T^{3/2}{\log^{-1/2}n})\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|=O_{p}(1),

uniformly in
i
.

Proof.
First, notice that for any
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
and any
n
and
T

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\frac{\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}f(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}=(nT)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{rT}}\frac{\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}f(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}\mathrm{d}\bm{F}_{T}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{rT}}\frac{\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}f(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}\frac{f(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}\mathrm{d}\bm{F}_{T}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{rT}}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})\frac{f(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}{f(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})}\mathrm{d}\bm{F}_{T}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{rT}}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})f(\bm{F}_{T}|\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})\mathrm{d}\bm{F}_{T}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}[\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{\prime};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{\prime}=\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}.

(E.123)

Second, for any
k\geq 0
, by definition of the M-step, by (
E.123
)
and by a Taylor expansion about
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{0}_{r}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)}}

(E.124)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}+\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})+O(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}+\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})+O(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}+\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}),

since the third derivative of
\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})
with respect to
\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
is zero since the second derivative does not depend on
\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
because
\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})
is quadratic in
\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
.
And from (
E.124
) it follows that

\displaystyle T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}=

\displaystyle\,-T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}).

(E.125)

Third, by a Taylor expansion about
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}
, by definition of
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}
which is a local maximum of
\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{0}_{r}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}+T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})

(E.126)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}((\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r})\left\{T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\text{vec}(\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}))|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\check{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}+T^{-1}\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2}n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right),

where
\check{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}
is such that
n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}-\check{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\|\leq n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)}\|
and
n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)}-\check{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\|\leq n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k+1)}\|
. In particular,
the last term in (
E.126
) follows from Lemma
E.9
and
Barigozzi 2023
, which imply

\displaystyle\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}

\displaystyle|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\text{vec}(\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\text{vec}(\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}))-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\text{vec}(\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))|

\displaystyle+\sup_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}}|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\text{vec}(\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}T\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T)+O(n^{-1}T)=O_{p}(n^{-1}T\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.127)

Define the following
r\times r
matrices:

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})=-\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle\bm{I}(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}).

By substituting (
E.125
) into (
E.126
) and rearranging

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})=

\displaystyle\,-\left\{\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}\right\}^{-1}\left\{\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}\right\}

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2}n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}\right\}^{-1}\left\{\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\mathcal{Q}(\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{(k)}}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2}n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})\right\}^{-1}\left\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}+\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2}n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right).

(E.128)

Moreover, by a Taylor approximation about
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}
,

\displaystyle T^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})=T^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}),

\displaystyle T^{-1}\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})=T^{-1}\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right),

(E.129)

where the first relation follows from the fact that, as noticed above,
\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})
does not depend on
\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
, while the second follows again from (
E.127
).

Therefore, from (
E.128
) and (
E.129
)

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})=

\displaystyle\,\left(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\left\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})\right\}^{-1}\left\{\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)})\right\}\right)(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|^{2}n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\left\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\right\}^{-1}\left\{\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\right\}\right)(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right),

(E.130)

see also
Sundberg 1974
;
Sundberg 1976
,
Dempster
et al. 1977
,
Meng and Rubin 1994
,
McLachlan and
Krishnan 2007
, and
Sundberg 2019
.

Let
\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\}^{-1}\{\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\}
, then, by setting
k=k^{*}
in (
E.130
), since
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*}+1)}
, we have

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})=

\displaystyle\,\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*})}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right).

(E.131)

Hence, from (
E.131
), by iterating backwards we get

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*})}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*})}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*}-1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*})}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)\right\}\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*})}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

(E.132)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}+\left\{\sum_{j=0}^{k^{*}}\|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{j}\right\}O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k^{*})}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}+\left\{\sum_{j=0}^{k^{*}}\|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{j}\right\}O_{p}\left(\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right),

where in the second and third step we used Lemma
E.21
, according to which
\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|
for any
k\geq 0
.

Let us consider separately the terms on the rhs of (
E.132
). Because of Lemma
E.22
(i),

\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})-\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),

(E.133)

since
\bm{R}(\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i})
is continuous and differentiable in
\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}
.
Consider the two matrices in
\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})
. First, from (
9
) we can easily see that

\displaystyle T^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})=

\displaystyle\,-T^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-T^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\bm{X}_{nT}]\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle+T^{-1}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\bm{X}_{nT}])(\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\bm{X}_{nT}])^{\prime}|\bm{X}_{nT}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,T^{-1}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*\prime}+\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{*}\right\},

(E.134)

where in the last step we used Assumption
4
which implies
\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}=\mathbb{E}_{\widehat{\varphi}_{n}^{*}}[\mathbf{F}_{t}|\bm{X}_{nT}]
for all
t=1,\ldots,T
. Also note that for (
E.112
) and (
E.113
) in the proof of Lemma
E.21
to hold expectations have to be taken with respect to the same distribution as the one used to compute the log-likelihood. Hence, given that we maximize a mis-specified log-likelihood with diagonal idiosyncratic covariance, in the last step of (
E.134
) we use
\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}
and
\mathbf{P}_{t|T}^{*}
, i.e., which are the outputs of the Kalman smoother implemented using
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi*}
.

Moreover, by Lemma
E.19

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*}\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{*\prime}+\mathbf{P}^{*}_{t|T}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\}\right\|=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

which, once substituted into (
E.134
), gives

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}

\displaystyle T^{-1}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})).

(E.135)

This also shows that
T^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})
is finite and positive definite, as
n,T\to\infty
, since
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}
is finite and positive definite with probability tending to one as
n,T\to\infty
, because of Lemma
C.12
(i) combined with Assumption
6
(b), and since
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}
is finite a positive for all
i=1,\ldots,n
because of Lemma
E.14
(ii) and Assumption
2
(a).

Second, from Lemma
E.9

it follows that

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T^{-1}\left\|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}_{n}^{*}}\right.

\displaystyle-\left.\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{*}}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T^{-1}\left\|\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{*}}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.136)

Furthermore, by a Taylor expansion about
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T^{-1}\left\|\right.

\displaystyle\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{*}}-\left.\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{\dagger}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T^{-1}\left\|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\text{vec}\left(\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})\right)|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{\dagger}}\right\|\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\dagger}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|+O_{p}(\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\dagger}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T),

(E.137)

by Lemma
E.10
(i) and since
\|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}}\text{vec}(\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}))\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}T\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T)
for all
\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}
, by (
E.127
).

Third, from
Barigozzi 2023
:

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T^{-1}

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{\dagger}}-\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT}|\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{\dagger}}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T^{-1}\left\|\nabla_{\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\underline{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\prime}}\ell_{0}(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\bm{\phi}}_{n}=\widehat{\bm{\phi}}_{n}^{\dagger}}-\left(-(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})).

(E.138)

Last, from Lemma
E.10
(iii):

\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2{\dagger}}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}|=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T).

(E.139)

By combining (
E.136
), (
E.137
), (
E.138
), and (
E.139
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}

\displaystyle T^{-1}\left\|\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}).

(E.140)

This is also shows that
T^{-1}\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})
is finite and positive definite, as
n,T\to\infty
, since
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}
is finite and positive definite with probability tending to one as
n,T\to\infty
, because of Lemma
C.12
(i) combined with Assumption
6
(b), and since
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}
is finite a positive for all
i=1,\ldots,n
because of Lemma
E.14
(ii) and Assumption
2
(a).

Therefore, by using (
E.133
), (
E.135
), and (
E.140
), and since, as remarked above
T^{-1}\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})

is finite and positive definite, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{I}_{r}-\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})\}^{-1}\{\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})\}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\}\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})\}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T\|\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\}\|\,T^{-1}\|\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T\|\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\}\|\,T^{-1}\left\|\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})-(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}T\|\{\bm{\mathcal{I}}_{\text{c}}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})^{-1}\}\|\,\left\|(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{I}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*})\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n}).

(E.141)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|+\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}),

(E.142)

because of Lemmas
D.1
(i),
E.13
(i), and
E.22
(i), respectively.

By substituting (
E.141
) and (
E.142
) into (
E.132
), we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}+o_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})\right\}^{k^{*}+1}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}))

(E.143)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1}T^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n},T^{-3/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

uniformly in
i
since the rhs of (
E.141
) does not depend on
i
.
This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.24
.

Consider the EM estimators
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\equiv\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)}
,

\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\equiv\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(k+1)}
, and
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v(k+1)}
,
for any
k\geq 0
.
Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T})\,|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|=O_{p}(1);

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T})\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\|=O_{p}(1);

(iii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T})\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v}-\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{v**}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

Let,

\displaystyle{R}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**})

\displaystyle=1-\left\{\mathbb{E}_{\underline{\varphi}_{n}}[\nabla_{\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT},\bm{F}_{T};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}=\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}}|\bm{X}_{nT}]\right\}^{-1}\left\{\nabla_{\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}}\ell(\bm{X}_{nT};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}_{n})|_{\underline{\sigma}_{i}^{2}=\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}}\right\}.

Then, following the same steps leading to (
E.132
) in the proof of Lemma
E.23
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|\leq

\displaystyle\,|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|\ |{R}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**})|^{k^{*}+1}+C|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-{\sigma}_{i}^{2}|+|{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}|+|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|\right\}|{R}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**})|^{k^{*}+1}+C|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2})),

(E.144)

for some finite positive real
C
independent of
i
, by Lemmas
D.4
(i),

E.13
(iii), and
E.22
(ii),
and since
|{R}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**})|=O_{p}(1)
. This proves part (i).

For part (ii), following again the same steps leading to (
E.132
) in the proof of Lemma
E.23
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\|\ \|{\bm{R}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}+C\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{A}}\|+\|{\mathbf{A}}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}\|+\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{*}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\|\right\}

\displaystyle\cdot\|{\bm{R}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}+C\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(0)}-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})+O_{p}(\min(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}))+O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2})),

for some finite positive real
C
, by Lemmas
D.3
(i),
E.13
(iv),
E.22
(iii), and since
\|{\bm{R}}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{**})\|=O_{p}(1)
. This proves part (ii).

Part (iii) is proved in the same way as part (ii) but using Lemmas
D.3
(ii),
E.13
(v), and
E.22
(iv). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.25
.

Consider the EM estimators
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
and
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\equiv\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)}
, for any
k\geq 0
.
Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n^{2}\log^{-4/\delta_{v}}T,nT\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T{\log^{-1/2}n},T^{3/2}{\log^{-1}n})\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}{\log^{-1/2}n})\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
First notice that from (
E.73
) in the proof of Lemma
E.14

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

(E.145)

while from (
D.6
) in the proof of Lemma
D.4
it is clear that

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-{\sigma}_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

(E.146)

because of (
E.145
).

Then,

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}),

(E.147)

because of (
E.145
) and Lemmas
E.14
(i),
E.22
(i).

Then, from (
E.143
) in the proof of Lemma
E.23

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})\right\}^{k^{*}+1}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1}T^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n},T^{-3/2}{\log n})),

(E.148)

because of (
E.147
) and (
E.141
) in the proof of Lemma
E.23
. This proves part (i).

For part (ii), from (
E.144
) in the proof of Lemma
E.24

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-{\sigma}_{i}^{2}|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2*}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|\right\}

\displaystyle\cdot\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|{R}(\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**})|^{k^{*}+1}+C\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2**}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

because of (
E.146
), and Lemmas
E.14
(ii),
E.22
(ii). This proves part (ii) and completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma E.26
.

Consider the EM algorithm initialized with any deterministic loadings
\check{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}=(\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{1}^{(0)}\cdots\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{\prime}
such that

\text{vec}(\check{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\in\{\mathcal{O}_{\lambda_{i}}^{n}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\Lambda_{n}}\}
as defined in Section
4.3.4
.
Then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT}{\log^{-1/2}n},T{\log^{-1/2}n})\,\|\widehat{{\bm{\lambda}}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|=O_{p}(1),

uniformly in
i
.

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT}{\log^{-1/2}n},T{\log^{-1/2}n})\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{{\bm{\lambda}}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|=O_{p}(1).

Proof.
From (
E.132
) in the proof of Lemma
E.23
,

\displaystyle\|{\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}+\left\{\sum_{j=0}^{k^{*}}\|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{j}\right\}O_{p}\left(\|\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\|\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\ \|\bm{R}(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**})\|^{k^{*}+1}+O_{p}\left(\|\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})\right\}^{k^{*}+1}+O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n}),

by (
E.141
) in the proof of Lemma
E.23
, and since
\|\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|\leq\|\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|+\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\leq M_{\lambda}O(1)
, by definition of the initial estimator, Assumption
1
(a), and Lemmas
E.13
(i) and
E.22
(i). This proves part (i).

Part (ii) is proved in the same way but starting from (
E.148
) in the proof of Lemma
E.25
and by noting that
\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\check{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}\|\leq M_{\lambda}
by the same arguments as before. This completes the proof.
\Box

Appendix F
Lemmas necessary for proving Proposition
3

Lemma F.1
.

Consider the EM estimators
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
,
\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\equiv\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)}
,
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k+1)}
,
for any
k\geq 0
.
Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

(i)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}-\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

\min(\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n,n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T)\,\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof.
For part (i)

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{**}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{*}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1}T^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n},T^{-3/2}{\log n}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n}))+O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

by Lemmas
E.14
(i),
E.22
(i), and
E.25
(i). This proves part (i).

Part (ii) is proved as part (i) but using Lemmas
E.14
(ii),
E.22
(ii), and
E.25
(ii).

Part (iii) immediately follows from part (ii), indeed

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}-\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}\|\leq\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})).

For part (iv) we have

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=\left\{\min_{i=1,\ldots,n}\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\right\}^{-1}\leq C_{\xi}+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

because of part (ii) and Assumption
2
(a).

To conclude, for part (v) we have

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\leq\|(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\|\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}-\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

by parts (iii), (iv), and Assumption
2
(a). This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma F.2
.

Consider the EM estimators
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
,

\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k+1)}
,

\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\equiv\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)}
,
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k+1)}
,
for any
k\geq 0
.
Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n)\,n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n)\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iv)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n)\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(v)

\omega_{n,T,\delta_{v}}\,\sqrt{n}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1)
,

with
\omega_{n,T,\delta_{v}}=\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n).

Proof.
The proof is the same as the proof of Lemma
E.15
but using Proposition
2
(a) (which does not require this lemma to be proved) and Lemma
F.1
instead of Lemmas
E.13
and
E.14
.
\Box

.

.

\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}\equiv{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}^{(k+1)}
,

, and

obtained using the EM estimators
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
,

\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k+1)}
,

\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\equiv\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)}
,
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k+1)}
,
for any

.
Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

for all

,
\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|s}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
t\leq s
;

(ii)

, uniformly in

;

(iii)

, uniformly in
t
;

where
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}.

,

,

, and

.
