Lemma F.5
.

Consider the Kalman filter and smoother estimators
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}\equiv{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t-1}^{(k+1)}
,
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}\equiv{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}^{(k+1)}
, and
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}\equiv{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}^{(k+1)}
obtained using the EM estimators
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(k+1)}
,

\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k+1)}
,

\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\equiv\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(k+1)}
,
and
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi}\equiv\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k+1)}
,
for any
k\geq 0
.
Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
:

(i)

for all
s=0,\ldots,T
,
\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,s}\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|s}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\,n\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|T}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(iii)

\log^{-1/\delta_{v}}T\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\,n\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t|t}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

where
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}.

Proof.
From (
D.47
) in the proof of Lemma
D.14
, but when computed using the EM estimator of the parameters, we see that the only modification is that we need to use
\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}n^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\|=O_{p}()
from Lemma
E.3
. This proves part (i).

Part (ii) follows from part (i) and (
D.48
) in the proof of Lemma
D.15
, but when computed using the EM estimator of the parameters.

Part (iii) follows from (
D.53
) in the proof of Lemma
D.16
, but when computed using the EM estimator of the parameters, and using Lemma
E.3
again
and the fact that
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t-1|t-1}
is a weighted average of
\mathbf{x}_{n1},\ldots,\mathbf{x}_{n,t-1}
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Appendix G
Lemmas necessary for proving Proposition
5

Lemma G.1
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

\min(n,\sqrt{T}\log^{-1/2}n)\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{(0)2}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|=O_{p}(1).

Proof.
From (
D.6
) in the proof of Lemma
D.4
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{i}^{2(0)}-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}x_{it}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[x_{it}^{2}]\right|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|\,\left\{\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\right\}

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|\,\left\{\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\right\}

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|

\displaystyle+2\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}\right\|\,\left\{\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{(0)}-\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|+\max_{i=1,\ldots,n}\|\bm{\lambda}_{i}\|\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),

by Assumption
1
(a), Lemma
E.1
(ii) and the union bound,
and (
E.73
) and (
E.81
) in the proof of Lemma
E.14
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma G.2
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
5
, and
6
, as
n,T\to\infty
, for any
k\geq 0
,

(i)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT}{\log^{-1/2}n},T{\log^{-1/2}n}),\,\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|=O_{p}(1)
;

(ii)

\min(n\log^{-2/\delta_{v}}T,\sqrt{nT}{\log^{-1/2}n},T{\log^{-1/2}n})\,\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\xi_{it}\|=O_{p}(1)
, uniformly in
i
.

Proof.
Throughout, let
\bm{y}_{t}=\mathbf{F}_{t}
or
\bm{y}_{t}=\xi_{it}
.
Then, for any
k\geq 0
we have to consider

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(k)})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(k)}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(k)})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathcal{I}+\mathcal{II}+\mathcal{III},\;\text{ say.}

(G.1)

where
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny{WLS}}(k)}=(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\mathbf{x}_{nt}.

Let us consider each term in (
G.1
). First, when
k=0
we have
\mathcal{I}=O_{p}(n^{-1})
by (
B.5
) in the proof of Proposition
1
(which does not require this lemma to be proved), while, when
k\geq 1

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(k)}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{y}_{t}\right\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T),

(G.2)

by Lemma
F.5
(ii) and since

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq rT^{-2}\max_{j=1,\ldots,r}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}|\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}F_{js}]|\leq 1,

(G.3)

by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Assumption
6
(b), and also

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\xi_{it}\right|^{2}\right]\leq T^{-2}\sum_{t,s=1}^{T}|\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{is}]|\leq T^{-1}M_{3},

(G.4)

by Lemma
C.1
(iii).
Second, when
k=0
we have
\mathcal{II}=O_{p}(n^{-1})
by (
B.6
) in the proof of Proposition
1
(which does not require this lemma to be proved), while, when
k\geq 1

\displaystyle\mathcal{II}\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{F}_{t|t}^{(k)}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny WLS}(k)}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{y}_{t}\right\|=O_{p}(n^{-1}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T),

(G.5)

by Lemma
F.5
(iii), (
G.3
), and (
G.4
).

Third,

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}\leq

\displaystyle\,\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})\|\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+\|n(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}n^{-1/2}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}-n({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}n^{-1/2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\cdot n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)}\|\,\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle+n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\bm{y}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathcal{III}_{a}+\mathcal{III}_{b}+\mathcal{III}_{c}+\mathcal{III}_{d},\;\text{say.}

(G.6)

Then, when
k=0
,
\mathcal{III}_{a}=III_{a}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})
,
\mathcal{III}_{b}=III_{b}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-2},T^{-1}))
, and
\mathcal{III}_{c}=III_{c}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})
, because of (
B.9
), (
B.10
), (
B.11
), and (
B.12
) in the proof of Proposition
1
(which does not require this lemma to be proved).
While, if
k\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{a}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),

(G.7)

by (
B.40
), (
B.41
), and (
B.42
) in the proof of Proposition
3
(which does not require this lemma to be proved), and since

\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\|=O_{p}(1)
by Lemma
C.12
(i) and Assumption
6
(b), and
\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xi_{it}\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2})
by Lemma
C.12
(ii). These last arguments, and (
B.43
) in the proof of Proposition
3
(which does not require this lemma to be proved)
imply also that, if
k\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{b}=O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n},n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n})).

(G.8)

Moreover, if
\bm{y}_{t}=\mathbf{F}_{t}
and
k\geq 1
, then

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{c}=

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(G.9)

by Lemmas
F.2
(iii) and
C.8
(iv). While if
\bm{y}_{t}=\xi_{it}
and
k\geq 1
, then

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{c}=

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}),

(G.10)

by Lemmas
F.2
(iii) and
C.8
(v).

Similarly, if
\bm{y}_{t}=\mathbf{F}_{t}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d}=

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})),&\text{ if }&k=0,\\
O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),&\text{ if }&k\geq 1,\end{array}\right.

where, if
k=0
, we used Lemmas
D.5
(ii),
D.5
(iii), and
C.12
(iii), while, if
k\geq 1
, we used Lemmas
F.2
(ii),
F.2
(iii), and
C.12
(iii).

Finally, if
\bm{y}_{t}=\xi_{it}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d}=

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\{\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,\bigg\{n^{-1}\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\bigg\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\bigg\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{\prime}\{(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}-({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\bigg\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)\prime}(\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi(k)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)})^{-1}\|\,\{\mathcal{III}_{d.1}+\mathcal{III}_{d.2}+\mathcal{III}_{d.3}\},\;\text{say.}

(G.13)

Now,

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d.1}=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(k)}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\|({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1/2}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathcal{III}_{d.1.1}+\mathcal{III}_{d.1.2},\text{say.}

(G.14)

And,

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d.1.1}=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{s}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{s}\bm{\xi}_{ns}^{\prime}\right)({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{s}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-2}\sum_{s,t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{s}\bm{\xi}_{ns}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{s}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-2}\sum_{s,t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{s}\bm{\xi}_{ns}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right\|\,\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}|\xi_{it}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-3/2}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T),

(G.15)

by
(
E.9
) in the proof of Lemma
E.3
, and Lemmas
C.8
(vi) and
C.13
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d.1.2}=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})),&\text{ if }&k=0,\\
O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),&\text{ if }&k\geq 1,\end{array}\right.

where we used Assumption
2
(a), the last relation in (
B.7
) in the proof of Proposition
1
(which does not require this lemma to be proved), and, if
k=0
, we used also
Barigozzi 2023
, or, if
k\geq 1
, we used (
B.39
) in the proof of Proposition
3
(which does not require this lemma to be proved).

By using (
G.15
) and (
G
) into (
G.14
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d.1}=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-3/2}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T)

\displaystyle+\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})),&\text{ if }&k=0,\\
O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1})),&\text{ if }&k\geq 1,\end{array}\right.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d.2}=

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}\{(\widehat{\sigma}_{j}^{2(k)})^{-1}-({\sigma}_{j}^{2})^{-1}\}\xi_{jt}\xi_{it}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,C_{\xi}^{2}\max_{j=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{j}^{2(k)}-{\sigma}_{j}^{(k)}|n^{-1}\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\bm{\lambda}}_{j}\xi_{jt}\xi_{it}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,C_{\xi}^{2}\max_{j=1,\ldots,n}|\widehat{\sigma}_{j}^{2(k)}-{\sigma}_{j}^{(k)}|n^{-1}\bigg\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\xi_{it}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})\cdot\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),&\text{ if }&k=0,\\
O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n})),&\text{ if }&k\geq 1,\end{array}\right.

where, if
k=0
, we used Lemmas
G.1
and
C.8
(ii), while, if
k\geq 1
, we used Lemmas
F.1
(ii) and
C.8
(ii). Term
\mathcal{III}_{d.3}
is dominated by
\mathcal{III}_{d.1}
.

By using Lemmas
F.2
(iii) or
D.5
(iii), together with (
G
) and (
G
) into (
G.13
), we have that if
\bm{y}_{t}=\xi_{it}
, then

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{d}=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-3/2}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T)+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-3/2}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})).

(G.22)

By substituting (
G.7
), (
G.8
), (
G.9
), (
G.10
), (
G
), and (
G.22
) into (
G.6
), if
\bm{y}_{t}=\mathbf{F}_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n},n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n}))+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

(G.23)

while, if
\bm{y}_{t}=\xi_{it}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-2}\log^{4/\delta_{v}}T,T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n},n^{-1}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T\sqrt{\log n}))+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-3/2}\log^{1/\delta_{v}}T)+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle+O_{p}(\max(n^{-3/2}T^{-1/2}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})).

(G.24)

To conclude by substituting (
G.2
), (
G.5
), and either (
G.23
) or (
G.24
) into (
G.1
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

which proves part (i), and

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{t|T}^{(k)}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\xi_{it}\right\|=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1}\log^{2/\delta_{v}}T,n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\log n},T^{-1}\sqrt{\log n})),

which proves part (ii) and completes the proof.
\Box

Appendix H
Lemmas necessary for proving Proposition
6

Lemma H.1
.

Consider the initial estimator of the factors
\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}
defined in Section
A.1
, then, under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
, and
6
,
as
n,T\to\infty
, if
\sqrt{n}/T\to 0
,

\sqrt{n}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0}_{r},\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny PC}}),

for any given
t=1,\ldots,T
, where

\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}^{\text{\tiny PC}}=(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{-1}\left(\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}[\xi_{it}\xi_{jt}]\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{j}}\right)(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda})^{-1}

with
\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\lambda}_{i}\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}
;

Proof.

By definition of the pre-estimator in Section
A.1
:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}=

\displaystyle\,(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}\mathbf{x}_{nt}-\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,n(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\left\{n^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\mathbf{F}_{t}+n^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}+n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right\}

(H.1)

\displaystyle+n\left\{(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x})^{-1}-(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\right\}\left\{n^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\mathbf{F}_{t}+n^{-1}(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}+n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right\}.

Then,

\displaystyle n\|(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\|=n\{\mu_{nr}^{\chi}\}^{-1}\geq\underline{C}_{r},

(H.2)

by Lemma
C.1
(iv). Moreover, by Lemmas,
C.1
(v),
C.11
and
C.12
(vii), and
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
, which is Weyl’s inequality, for all
j=1,\ldots,r
,

\displaystyle n^{-1}|\widehat{\mu}_{j}^{x}-{\mu}_{j}^{\chi}|\leq n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{\Gamma}^{x}\right\|+n^{-1}\|\bm{\Gamma}^{\xi}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})),

(H.3)

which, jointly with (
H.2
), implies

\displaystyle\det(n^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x})=\prod_{j=1}^{r}n^{-1}\widehat{\mu}_{j}^{x}\geq\{n^{-1}\widehat{\mu}_{r}^{x}\}^{r}\geq\{n^{-1}{\mu}_{r}^{\chi}-n^{-1}|\widehat{\mu}_{r}^{x}-{\mu}_{r}^{\chi}|\}^{r}>0,

thus,

\displaystyle n\|(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(1).

(H.4)

From (
H.2
), (
H.3
), and (
H.4
)

\displaystyle n\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x})^{-1}-(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}\|\,n^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x}-\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}\right\|\,n\|(\widehat{\mathbf{M}}_{n}^{x})^{-1}\|=O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},T^{-1/2})).

(H.5)

Furthermore,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\mathbf{F}_{t}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1/2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|+n^{-1}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}-\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}\|^{2}\,\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}))+O_{p}(\max(n^{-2},T^{-1}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2},T^{-1})),

(H.6)

by
Barigozzi 2023
,
Lemmas
C.2
,
C.8
(i),
C.13
, and
D.1
(b),
and since
\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|=O_{p}(1)
because
\mathbb{E}[F_{jt}^{2}]=1
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, by Assumption
6
(b).

Last,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\|(\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}-{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|+n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,n^{-1}\left\|T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\bm{\xi}_{nt}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt}\right\|\,\left\|\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle+n^{-1/2}\|\widehat{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{(0)}-\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\|\,n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})+O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(\max(n^{-1},n^{-1/2}T^{-1/2})),

(H.7)

by
Barigozzi 2023
, Lemmas
C.8
(vi) (setting
\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi}=\mathbf{I}_{n}
and using
\bm{\xi}_{nt}
in place of
\bm{\mathcal{E}}_{nT}
therein),

C.13
, and
D.1
(b),
and since
n^{-1/2}\|\bm{\xi}_{nt}\|=O_{p}(1)
because
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}^{2}\leq nC_{\xi}
by Assumption
2
(a).

By substituting (
H.5
), (
H.6
), and (
H.7
) into (
H.1
), it follows that if
\sqrt{n}/T\to 0
, as
n,T\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t})=n(\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi})^{-1}(n^{-1/2}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{nt})+o_{p}(1).

(H.8)

and since
\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{n}^{\chi}=\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda}
by Assumption
6
(b) which is positive definite, by Assumption
2
(e) (when setting
\sigma_{i}^{2}=1
therein) and Slutsky’s Theorem we complete the proof.
\Box

Appendix I
Derivation of the Kalman filter MSE

Lemma I.1
.

Under Assumption
1
, the DFM (
3
)-(
4
) is both stabilizable and detectable, for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
.

Proof.
We use the definitions in
Anderson and
Moore 1979
. The DFM (
3
)-(
4
) is a linear systems with
r
states. A linear system is stabilizable if its unstable states are controllable and all uncontrollable states are stable, and it is detectable if its unstable states are observable and all unobservable states are stable.

First, by factorizing
\bm{\Gamma}^{v}=\mathbf{H}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}
for some
\mathbf{H}
having full-column rank, we see that
\text{rk}[\mathbf{H}\;(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{H})\cdots(\mathbf{A}^{(r-1)}\mathbf{H})]=r
, thus the linear system is controllable, by Assumption
1
(e). Moreover, there are no unstable states, since because of Assumption
1
(d) all eigenvalues of
{\mathbf{A}}
are smaller than one in absolute value. This implies that the model is stabilizable.

Second, since by Assumption
1
(a) for any given
n\in\mathbb{N}
there exists at least one
i=1,\ldots,n
such that
\|{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\|\geq m_{\lambda}
, then
\mbox{rk}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\geq 1
, while,
\mbox{rk}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})=r
only for
n>N
, then, for a given
n
, there might be
(r-1)
unobservable states, however, as already noticed, they are all stable by Assumption
1
(d). Thus the model is detectable. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma I.2
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, the matrix
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
has a steady-state denoted as
{\mathbf{P}}=\lim_{t\to\infty}\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
.

Proof.

First, given that with our initialization
\mathbf{P}_{0|0}=\bm{\Gamma}^{F}
, then it is positive definite by Assumption
1
(b), therefore also
\mathbf{P}_{1|0}
is positive definite (see also (
A.2
)). Second, as proved in Lemma
I.1
, the linear system defining the DFM (
3
)-(
4
) is stabilizable and detectable.
Therefore, because of
Chan
et al. 1984
, as
t\to\infty
,
{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}
converges to a steady-state
\mathbf{P}
exponentially fast (see Lemma
I.4
below for the rate of convergence), which is a solution of the algebraic Riccati equation (ARE) derived from (
A.5
)

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}+\bm{\Gamma}^{v}.

Moreover, since Lemmas
D.7
(i) and
D.7
(ii) hold for all
T\in\mathbb{N}
, we have

\|\mathbf{P}\|=O(1),\hskip 18.49988pt\|\mathbf{P}^{-1}\|=O(1).

(I.1)

This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma I.3
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, the matrix
\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1}
has a steady-state denoted as
{\bm{\Pi}}=\lim_{t\to\infty}\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1}
.

Proof.
The existence of
{\bm{\Pi}}
follows from the same arguments used in Lemma
I.2
. Moreover, from
Harvey and
Delle Monache 2009
we have that
{\bm{\Pi}}
must satisfy

\displaystyle\bm{\Pi}=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{A}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}+\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Pi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi})(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}

\displaystyle-\mathbf{A}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}-\mathbf{A}\bm{\Pi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}+\bm{\Gamma}^{v}.

Moreover, by the same arguments in Lemmas
D.7
(i) and
D.7
(ii) it holds that
\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1}\|=O(1)
and also
\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|(\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1})^{-1}\|=O(1)
, for all
T\in\mathbb{N}
, thus

\|\bm{\Pi}\|=O(1),\hskip 18.49988pt\|\bm{\Pi}^{-1}\|=O(1).

(I.2)

This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma I.4
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
6
, if
\|\mathbf{P}_{0|0}\|=O(n^{\gamma})
for some
\gamma>0
, then

n\max_{t=\bar{t},\ldots,T}\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}-{\mathbf{P}}\|=o(1)
and

n\max_{t=\bar{t},\ldots,T}\|\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1}-{\bm{\Pi}}\|=o(1)
, where
\bar{t}=\lceil 2+\gamma/2\rceil
.

Proof.

Let
\bm{\Psi}_{1,1}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
and, for
t=2,\ldots,T
,

\bm{\Psi}_{t,1}=\prod_{s=1}^{t-1}[{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{P}}_{s|s-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}{\mathbf{P}}_{s|s-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}].

(I.3)

Then, from
Anderson and
Moore 1979
, we have, for
t=1,\ldots,T
,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}-{\mathbf{P}}=

\displaystyle\,\{{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{P}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}{\mathbf{P}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\}^{t-1}({\mathbf{P}}_{1|0}-{\mathbf{P}})\bm{\Psi}_{t,1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,{\mathbf{A}}^{t-1}\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-{\mathbf{P}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}{\mathbf{P}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\}^{t-1}({\mathbf{P}}_{1|0}-{\mathbf{P}})\bm{\Psi}_{t,1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,{\mathbf{A}}^{t-1}\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\}^{t-1}({\mathbf{P}}_{1|0}-{\mathbf{P}})\bm{\Psi}_{t,1},

(I.4)

where we used Lemma
D.13
.

Now, for
t=2,\ldots,T
, from (
I.3
) and using again Lemma
D.13
, there exists a
\bar{n}
such that for all
n\geq\bar{n}

\displaystyle\|\bm{\Psi}_{t,1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}\|^{t-1}\prod_{s=1}^{t-1}\|\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+\mathbf{P}_{s|s-1}^{-1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\|\leq M_{0}n^{-(t-1)},

(I.5)

for some finite positive real
M_{0}
independent of
n
and
t
,
because of Lemmas
C.6
(i) and
D.7
(ii), and Assumption
1
(d). Moreover,

\|{\mathbf{P}}_{1|0}-{\mathbf{P}}\|=\|\mathbf{A}{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}-{\mathbf{P}}\|\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|\,\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|+\|\mathbf{A}\|\,\|{\mathbf{P}}\|\leq 2\|\mathbf{A}\|\,\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|,

(I.6)

since because of Lemma
D.6
,
\|\mathbf{P}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}\|\leq\|\mathbf{P}_{1|0}\|\leq\mathbf{P}_{0|0}\|
.

Therefore, for
t=2,\ldots,T
, from (
I.4
), (
I.5
), and (
I.6
), there exists a
\bar{n}
such that for all
n\geq\bar{n}

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}-{\mathbf{P}}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,2\|{\mathbf{A}}\|^{t}\|\mathbf{I}_{r}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+{\mathbf{P}}^{-1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}\}\|^{t-1}\,\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|\,\|\bm{\Psi}_{t,1}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,M_{1}n^{-2(t-1)}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|,

(I.7)

for some finite positive real
M_{1}
independent of
n
and
t
, because of Lemmas
C.6
(i) and
D.7
(ii), and Assumption
1
(d).

Now, define
\bar{t}
the first point in time such that the rhs of (
I.7
) is
o(n^{-1})
, i.e., such that

n^{-2(\bar{t}-1)}n^{1+\epsilon}\leq M_{2}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|^{-1},

for any
\epsilon>0
and some finite positive real
M_{2}
independent of
n
and
t
, or equivalently, by letting
K=\log M_{2}
,
\bar{t}
is such that:

\bar{t}\geq(3+\epsilon)/2+{\log\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|}/(2\log n)-K/(2\log n).

(I.8)

Clearly (
I.8
) is always satisfied if we set
\bar{t}=\lceil 2+{\log\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|}/(2\log n)\rceil
. Letting now
\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|=O(n^{\gamma})
for some
\gamma>0
, then we have
\bar{t}=\lceil 2+\gamma/2\rceil
satisfies (
I.8
) and therefore we have at least
\max_{t=\bar{t},\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}-{\mathbf{P}}\|=O(n^{-1})
.
Notice that if
\gamma=0
, i.e.,
\|{\mathbf{P}}_{0|0}\|
is finite, then
\bar{t}=2
and in this case from (
I.7
) we have an even tighter bound as we get
\max_{t=2,\ldots,T}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t|t-1}-{\mathbf{P}}\|=O(n^{-2})
.

The proof for
\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1}
can be done analogously but using the recursions in
Harvey and
Delle Monache 2009
. This completes the proof.
\Box

Lemma I.5
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
6
,

(i)

n\max_{t=\bar{t},\ldots,T}\|\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}\|=O(1)
;

(ii)

n\max_{t=1,\ldots,T}\|\bm{\Pi}_{t|t}\|=O(1)
;

(iii)

n\max_{\bar{t},\ldots,T}\|\bm{\Pi}_{t|t}-\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}\|=o(1)
,

(iv)

\max_{t=\bar{t},\ldots,T}\|n\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|=o(1)
;

where

\bm{\Pi}_{t|t}
is defined in (
22
),
\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}
is obtained from
\bm{\Pi}_{t|t}
when replacing
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
and
\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1}
with their steady states defined in Lemmas
I.2
and
I.3
, respectively,
and

\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
is defined in Proposition
3
.

Proof.
We have

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}=

\displaystyle\,\bm{\Pi}+\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Pi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}

\displaystyle-\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Pi}-\bm{\Pi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}.

(I.9)

Hereafter, for simplicity of notation let
\bm{H}=({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}
.
Using twice Lemma
C.4
we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{P}\left\{\mathbf{P}^{-1}-(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{P}\left\{\mathbf{P}^{-1}-(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\right\}\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}+\mathbf{C},\;\text{ say.}

(I.10)

Then, by Lemma
D.10
and (
I.10
)

\displaystyle\bm{\Pi}-\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Pi}=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{P}(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}\right\}\bm{\Pi}=\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}.

(I.11)

Similarly, again by Lemma
D.10
and (
I.10
)

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}

\displaystyle(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{\Pi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}-\bm{\Pi}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{P}(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}\bm{\Pi}(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}\mathbf{P}-\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}(\bm{H}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}+\mathbf{C}\right\}\bm{\Pi}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\right\}-\bm{\Pi}\left\{\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\bm{\Pi}-\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}+\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}-\bm{\Pi}+\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}+\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}-\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}+\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}.

(I.12)

By substituting (
I.11
) and (
I.12
) into (
I.9
):

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}

\displaystyle+\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}+\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}

\displaystyle-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}+\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}

\displaystyle+\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}-\bm{H}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}-\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\bm{H}+\mathbf{C}\bm{\Pi}\mathbf{C}.

(I.13)

where in the last step we used Lemma
D.13
.
Moreover,

\displaystyle\|\bm{H}\|=

\displaystyle\,\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|=O(n^{-1}),

(I.14)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{C}\|=

\displaystyle\,\|\mathbf{P}(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\mathbf{P}^{-1}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\mathbf{P}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{P}\|\,\|(\mathbf{P})^{-1}\|^{2}\|(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+\mathbf{P})^{-1}\|\,\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\|^{2}=O(n^{-2}),.

(I.15)

because of Lemma
C.3
(iii), (
I.1
) in the proof of Lemma
I.2
,
and since, by
Merikoski and
Kumar 2004
which is Weyl’s inequality,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}})^{-1}\|=\left\{\nu^{(r)}(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}+{\mathbf{P}})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\nu^{(r)}(({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1})+\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left[\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\right]^{-1}+\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\left[\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}})\right]^{-1}+1\right\}^{-1}\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}})\right\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{1-\left[\nu^{(1)}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}})\right]^{-1}\right\}\left\{\nu^{(r)}({\mathbf{P}})\right\}^{-1}+O_{p}(n^{-2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{p}(1),

again by Lemma
C.3
(iii) and (
I.1
) in the proof of Lemma
I.2
. Therefore, from (
I.13
), (
I.14
), and (
I.15
)

\displaystyle n\|\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\|

\displaystyle+n\|\bm{H}\|^{2}\|(\mathbf{P})^{-1}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Pi}\|+2n\|\bm{H}\|\,\|(\mathbf{P})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Pi}\|\,\|\mathbf{C}\|+n\|\mathbf{C}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Pi}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O(1)+O(n^{-1})+O(n^{-2})+O(n^{-3}),

(I.16)

where we used also (
I.1
) and (
I.2
) in the proofs of Lemmas
I.2
and
I.3
, respectively. Since
\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}
does not depend on
t
we prove part (i). Part (ii) is proved analogously by substituting in part (i)
\mathbf{P}
and
\bm{\Pi}
with
\mathbf{P}_{t|t-1}
and
\bm{\Pi}_{t|t-1}
and using Lemma
D.7
(i) and
D.7
(ii) instead of (
I.1
) and (
I.2
).

Part (iii) follows directly from Lemmas
I.2
,
I.3
, and
I.4
, and parts (i) and (ii).

Turning to part (iv), consider the first term on the rhs of (
I.13
), using Lemma
D.13
we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}

\displaystyle(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}=(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}+\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right\}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}

\displaystyle\cdot\left\{(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}+(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}

\displaystyle+\left\{(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right\}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}

\displaystyle+\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\left\{(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\right\}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\left\{(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}(\bm{H}^{-1}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}-(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}+\bm{\mathcal{A}}+\bm{\mathcal{A}}^{\prime}+\bm{\mathcal{B}},\;\text{say.}

(I.17)

Then,

\displaystyle n\|\bm{\mathcal{A}}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{H}\|\,\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\|

\displaystyle=O(n^{-3/2}),

(I.18)

because of Lemmas
C.1
(v),
C.3
(vii), and
C.6
(iii), and (
I.14
). Similarly,

\displaystyle n\|\bm{\mathcal{B}}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}+\mathbf{P}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}-({\bm{\Lambda}}_{n}^{\prime}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{n})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\|

\displaystyle=O(n^{-3}),

(I.19)

because of Lemmas
C.1
(v) and
C.6
(iii). Furthermore,

\displaystyle n\|\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\|

\displaystyle\leq n\|\bm{H}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}\|=O(1),

(I.20)

because of Lemmas
C.1
(v),
C.3
(vii), and (
I.14
).

Thus, by using (
I.18
), (
I.19
), and (
I.20
), from (
I.17
), we have

\displaystyle n\|

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}-\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2n\|\bm{\mathcal{A}}\|+n\|\bm{\mathcal{B}}\|=O(n^{-3/2}).

(I.21)

And, by using (
I.21
) and (
I.16
) into (
I.13
) we have

\displaystyle n\|\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}-\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,n\|\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}+\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\mathbf{P}-\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\|

\displaystyle+n\|\bm{H}\|^{2}\|(\mathbf{P})^{-1}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Pi}\|+2n\|\bm{H}\|\,\|(\mathbf{P})^{-1}\|\,\|\bm{\Pi}\|\,\|\mathbf{C}\|+n\|\mathbf{C}\|^{2}\|\bm{\Pi}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O(n^{-1}).

(I.22)

Finally, notice that, by definition:

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}n\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}n\bm{H}n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}n\bm{H}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}\left\{\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\right\}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\Lambda\Sigma\Lambda})^{-1}=\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}.

(I.23)

Therefore, from (
I.22
) and (
I.23
)

\displaystyle\|n\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}-\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|n\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}-n\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}\|+\|n\bm{H}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Gamma}_{n}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}_{n}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}_{n}\bm{H}-\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}\|=O(n^{-1})+o(1),

and since
\bm{\mathcal{P}}_{t}
and
\bm{\mathcal{W}}_{t}
do not depend on
t
we complete the proof.
\Box
